953 |     // really want to do this when we're looking at the constructor.
954 |     // We'd find all its properties and make sure they followed inheritance

===== M0005 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.getOrCreateConstructor()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 676-717
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns a constructor function either by returning it from the
   * registry if it exists or creating and registering a new type. If
   * there is already a type, then warn if the existing type is
   * different than the one we are creating, though still return the
   * existing function if possible.  The primary purpose of this is
   * that registering a constructor will fail for all built-in types
   * that are initialized in {@link JSTypeRegistry}.  We a) want to
   * make sure that the type information specified in the externs file
   * matches what is in the registry and b) annotate the externs with
   * the {@link JSType} from the registry so that there are not two
   * separate JSType objects for one type.
   */
Method code:
676 |   private FunctionType getOrCreateConstructor() {
677 |     FunctionType fnType = typeRegistry.createConstructorType(
678 |         fnName, contents.getSourceNode(), parametersNode, returnType);
679 |     JSType existingType = typeRegistry.getType(fnName);
680 | 
681 |     if (makesStructs) {
682 |       fnType.setStruct();
683 |     } else if (makesDicts) {
684 |       fnType.setDict();
685 |     }
686 |     if (existingType != null) {
687 |       boolean isInstanceObject = existingType.isInstanceType();
688 |       if (isInstanceObject || fnName.equals("Function")) {
689 |         FunctionType existingFn =
690 |             isInstanceObject ?
691 |             existingType.toObjectType().getConstructor() :
692 |             typeRegistry.getNativeFunctionType(FUNCTION_FUNCTION_TYPE);
693 | 
694 |         if (existingFn.getSource() == null) {
695 |           existingFn.setSource(contents.getSourceNode());
696 |         }
697 | 
698 |         if (!existingFn.hasEqualCallType(fnType)) {
699 |           reportWarning(TYPE_REDEFINITION, fnName,
700 |               fnType.toString(), existingFn.toString());
701 |         }
702 | 
703 |         return existingFn;
704 |       } else {
705 |         // We fall through and return the created type, even though it will fail
706 |         // to register. We have no choice as we have to return a function. We
707 |         // issue an error elsewhere though, so the user should fix it.
708 |       }
709 |     }
710 | 
711 |     maybeSetBaseType(fnType);
712 | 
713 |     if (getScopeDeclaredIn().isGlobal() && !fnName.isEmpty()) {
714 |       typeRegistry.declareType(fnName, fnType.getInstanceType());
715 |     }
716 |     return fnType;
717 |   }

===== M0026 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InferJSDocInfo.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InferJSDocInfo.java
Original range: 92-189
Documentation:

Method code:
92 |   @Override
93 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
94 |     JSDocInfo docInfo;
95 | 
96 |     switch (n.getType()) {
97 |       // Infer JSDocInfo on types of all type declarations on variables.
98 |       case Token.NAME:
99 |         if (parent == null) {
100 |           return;
101 |         }
102 | 
103 |         // Only allow JSDoc on VARs, function declarations, and assigns.
104 |         if (!parent.isVar() &&
105 |             !NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(parent) &&
106 |             !(parent.isAssign() &&
107 |               n == parent.getFirstChild())) {
108 |           return;
109 |         }
110 | 
111 |         // There are four places the doc info could live.
112 |         // 1) A FUNCTION node.
113 |         // /** ... */ function f() { ... }
114 |         // 2) An ASSIGN parent.
115 |         // /** ... */ x = function () { ... }
116 |         // 3) A NAME parent.
117 |         // var x, /** ... */ y = function() { ... }
118 |         // 4) A VAR gramps.
119 |         // /** ... */ var x = function() { ... }
120 |         docInfo = n.getJSDocInfo();
121 |         if (docInfo == null &&
122 |             !(parent.isVar() &&
123 |                 !parent.hasOneChild())) {
124 |           docInfo = parent.getJSDocInfo();
125 |         }
126 | 
127 |         // Try to find the type of the NAME.
128 |         JSType varType = n.getJSType();
129 |         if (varType == null && parent.isFunction()) {
130 |           varType = parent.getJSType();
131 |         }
132 | 
133 |         // If we have no type to attach JSDocInfo to, then there's nothing
134 |         // we can do.
135 |         if (varType == null || docInfo == null) {
136 |           return;
137 |         }
138 | 
139 |         // Dereference the type. If the result is not an object, or already
140 |         // has docs attached, then do nothing.
141 |         ObjectType objType = dereferenceToObject(varType);
142 |         if (objType == null || objType.getJSDocInfo() != null) {
143 |           return;
144 |         }
145 | 
146 |         attachJSDocInfoToNominalTypeOrShape(objType, docInfo, n.getString());
147 |         break;
148 | 
149 |       case Token.GETPROP:
150 |         // Infer JSDocInfo on properties.
151 |         // There are two ways to write doc comments on a property.
152 |         //
153 |         // 1)
154 |         // /** @deprecated */
155 |         // obj.prop = ...
156 |         //
157 |         // 2)
158 |         // /** @deprecated */
159 |         // obj.prop;
160 |         if (parent.isExprResult() ||
161 |             (parent.isAssign() &&
162 |              parent.getFirstChild() == n)) {
163 |           docInfo = n.getJSDocInfo();
164 |           if (docInfo == null) {
165 |             docInfo = parent.getJSDocInfo();
166 |           }
167 |           if (docInfo != null) {
168 |             ObjectType lhsType =
169 |                 dereferenceToObject(n.getFirstChild().getJSType());
170 |             if (lhsType != null) {
171 |               // Put the JSDoc in the property slot, if there is one.
172 |               String propName = n.getLastChild().getString();
173 |               if (lhsType.hasOwnProperty(propName)) {
174 |                 lhsType.setPropertyJSDocInfo(propName, docInfo);
175 |               }
176 | 
177 |               // Put the JSDoc in any constructors or function shapes as well.
178 |               ObjectType propType =
179 |                   dereferenceToObject(lhsType.getPropertyType(propName));
180 |               if (propType != null) {
181 |                 attachJSDocInfoToNominalTypeOrShape(
182 |                     propType, docInfo, n.getQualifiedName());
183 |               }
184 |             }
185 |           }
186 |         }
187 |         break;
188 |     }
189 |   }

===== M0036 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.createFunctionTypeFromNodes(Node,String,JSDocInfo,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 841-950
Documentation:
/**
     * Creates a new function type, based on the given nodes.
     *
     * This handles two cases that are semantically very different, but
     * are not mutually exclusive:
     * - A function literal that needs a type attached to it.
     * - An assignment expression with function-type info in the JsDoc.
     *
     * All parameters are optional, and we will do the best we can to create
     * a function type.
     *
     * This function will always create a function type, so only call it if
     * you're sure that's what you want.
     *
     * @param rValue The function node.
     * @param name the function's name
     * @param info the {@link JSDocInfo} attached to the function definition
     * @param lvalueNode The node where this function is being
     *     assigned. For example, {@code A.prototype.foo = ...} would be used to
     *     determine that this function is a method of A.prototype. May be
     *     null to indicate that this is not being assigned to a qualified name.
     */
Method code:
841 |     private FunctionType createFunctionTypeFromNodes(
842 |         @Nullable Node rValue,
843 |         @Nullable String name,
844 |         @Nullable JSDocInfo info,
845 |         @Nullable Node lvalueNode) {
846 | 
847 |       FunctionType functionType = null;
848 | 
849 |       // Global ctor aliases should be registered with the type registry.
850 |       if (rValue != null && rValue.isQualifiedName() && scope.isGlobal()) {
851 |         Var var = scope.getVar(rValue.getQualifiedName());
852 |         if (var != null && var.getType() != null &&
853 |             var.getType().isFunctionType()) {
854 |           FunctionType aliasedType  = var.getType().toMaybeFunctionType();
855 |           if ((aliasedType.isConstructor() || aliasedType.isInterface()) &&
856 |               !aliasedType.isNativeObjectType()) {
857 |             functionType = aliasedType;
858 | 
859 |             if (name != null && scope.isGlobal()) {
860 |               typeRegistry.declareType(name, functionType.getInstanceType());
861 |             }
862 |           }
863 |         }
864 |       }
865 | 
866 |       if (functionType == null) {
867 |         Node errorRoot = rValue == null ? lvalueNode : rValue;
868 |         boolean isFnLiteral =
869 |             rValue != null && rValue.isFunction();
870 |         Node fnRoot = isFnLiteral ? rValue : null;
871 |         Node parametersNode = isFnLiteral ?
872 |             rValue.getFirstChild().getNext() : null;
873 |         Node fnBlock = isFnLiteral ? parametersNode.getNext() : null;
874 | 
875 |         if (info != null && info.hasType()) {
876 |           JSType type = info.getType().evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
877 | 
878 |           // Known to be not null since we have the FUNCTION token there.
879 |           type = type.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
880 |           if (type.isFunctionType()) {
881 |             functionType = type.toMaybeFunctionType();
882 |             functionType.setJSDocInfo(info);
883 |           }
884 |         }
885 | 
886 |         if (functionType == null) {
887 |           // Find the type of any overridden function.
888 |           Node ownerNode = NodeUtil.getBestLValueOwner(lvalueNode);
889 |           String ownerName = NodeUtil.getBestLValueName(ownerNode);
890 |           Var ownerVar = null;
891 |           String propName = null;
892 |           ObjectType ownerType = null;
893 |           if (ownerName != null) {
894 |             ownerVar = scope.getVar(ownerName);
895 |             if (ownerVar != null) {
896 |               ownerType = ObjectType.cast(ownerVar.getType());
897 |             }
898 |             if (name != null) {
899 |               propName = name.substring(ownerName.length() + 1);
900 |             }
901 |           }
902 | 
903 |           FunctionType overriddenPropType = null;
904 |           if (ownerType != null && propName != null) {
905 |             overriddenPropType =
906 |                 findOverriddenFunction(ownerType, propName);
907 |           }
908 | 
909 |           FunctionTypeBuilder builder =
910 |               new FunctionTypeBuilder(name, compiler, errorRoot, sourceName,
911 |                   scope)
912 |               .setContents(getFunctionAnalysisResults(fnRoot))
913 |               .inferFromOverriddenFunction(overriddenPropType, parametersNode)
914 |               .inferTemplateTypeName(info)
915 |               .inferReturnType(info)
916 |               .inferInheritance(info);
917 | 
918 |           // Infer the context type.
919 |           boolean searchedForThisType = false;
920 |           if (ownerType != null && ownerType.isFunctionPrototypeType() &&
921 |               ownerType.getOwnerFunction().hasInstanceType()) {
922 |             builder.inferThisType(
923 |                 info, ownerType.getOwnerFunction().getInstanceType());
924 |             searchedForThisType = true;
925 |           } else if (ownerNode != null && ownerNode.isThis()) {
926 |             // If 'this' has a type, use that instead.
927 |             // This is a hack, necessary because CollectProperties (below)
928 |             // doesn't run with the scope that it's building,
929 |             // so scope.getTypeOfThis() will be wrong.
930 |             JSType injectedThisType = ownerNode.getJSType();
931 |             builder.inferThisType(
932 |                 info,
933 |                 injectedThisType == null ?
934 |                 scope.getTypeOfThis() : injectedThisType);
935 |             searchedForThisType = true;
936 |           }
937 | 
938 |           if (!searchedForThisType) {
939 |             builder.inferThisType(info);
940 |           }
941 | 
942 |           functionType = builder
943 |               .inferParameterTypes(parametersNode, info)
944 |               .buildAndRegister();
945 |         }
946 |       }
947 | 
948 |       // all done
949 |       return functionType;
950 |     }

/**
   * Looks for a type expression at the current token and if found,
   * returns it. Note that this method consumes input.
   *
   * Parameter type expressions are special for two reasons:
   * <ol>
   *   <li>They must begin with '{', to distinguish type names from param names.
   *   <li>They may end in '=', to denote optionality.
   * </ol>
   *
   * @param token The current token.
   * @return The type expression found or null if none.
   */
Method code:
1133 |   private Node parseAndRecordParamTypeNode(JsDocToken token) {
1134 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(token == JsDocToken.LC);
1135 |     int lineno = stream.getLineno();
1136 |     int startCharno = stream.getCharno();
1137 | 
1138 |     Node typeNode = parseParamTypeExpressionAnnotation(token);
1139 |     if (typeNode != null) {
1140 |       int endLineno = stream.getLineno();
1141 |       int endCharno = stream.getCharno();
1142 | 
1143 |       jsdocBuilder.markTypeNode(typeNode, lineno, startCharno,
1144 |           endLineno, endCharno, true);
1145 |     }
1146 |     return typeNode;
1147 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.createParameters(boolean,JSType...)

/**
   * Creates a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list.
   *
   * @param lastVarArgs whether the last type should considered as a variable
   *     length argument.
   * @param parameterTypes the parameter types. The last element of this array
   *     is considered a variable length argument is {@code lastVarArgs} is
   *     {@code true}.

   */
Method code:
1246 |   private Node createParameters(boolean lastVarArgs, JSType... parameterTypes) {
1247 |     FunctionParamBuilder builder = new FunctionParamBuilder(this);
1248 |     int max = parameterTypes.length - 1;
1249 |     for (int i = 0; i <= max; i++) {
1250 |       if (lastVarArgs && i == max) {
1251 |         builder.addVarArgs(parameterTypes[i]);

1254 |       }
1255 |     }

===== M0066 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseTypeName(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1784-1819
Documentation:
/**
   * TypeName := NameExpression | NameExpression TypeApplication
   * TypeApplication := '.<' TypeExpressionList '>'
   * TypeExpressionList := TypeExpression // a white lie
   */
Method code:
1784 |   private Node parseTypeName(JsDocToken token) {
1785 |     if (token != JsDocToken.STRING) {
1786 |       return reportGenericTypeSyntaxWarning();
1787 |     }
1788 | 
1789 |     String typeName = stream.getString();
1790 |     int lineno = stream.getLineno();
1791 |     int charno = stream.getCharno();
1792 |     while (match(JsDocToken.EOL) &&
1793 |         typeName.charAt(typeName.length() - 1) == '.') {
1794 |       skipEOLs();
1795 |       if (match(JsDocToken.STRING)) {
1796 |         next();
1797 |         typeName += stream.getString();
1798 |       }
1799 |     }
1800 | 
1801 |     Node typeNameNode = newStringNode(typeName, lineno, charno);
1802 | 
1803 |     if (match(JsDocToken.LT)) {
1804 |       next();
1805 |       skipEOLs();
1806 |       Node memberType = parseTypeExpressionList(next());
1807 |       if (memberType != null) {
1808 |         typeNameNode.addChildToFront(memberType);
1809 | 
1810 |         skipEOLs();
1811 |         if (!match(JsDocToken.GT)) {
1812 |           return reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.gt");
1813 |         }
1814 | 
1815 |         next();
1816 |       }
1817 |     }
1818 |     return typeNameNode;
1819 |   }

===== M0069 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getBestLValue(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 3049-3068
Documentation:
/** Find the l-value that the given r-value is being assigned to. */
Method code:
3049 |   static Node getBestLValue(Node n) {
3050 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
3051 |     boolean isFunctionDeclaration = isFunctionDeclaration(n);
3052 |     if (isFunctionDeclaration) {
3053 |       return n.getFirstChild();
3054 |     } else if (parent.isName()) {
3055 |       return parent;
3056 |     } else if (parent.isAssign()) {
3057 |       return parent.getFirstChild();
3058 |     } else if (isObjectLitKey(parent, parent.getParent())) {
3059 |       return parent;
3060 |     } else if (
3061 |         (parent.isHook() && parent.getFirstChild() != n) ||
3062 |         parent.isOr() ||
3063 |         parent.isAnd() ||
3064 |         (parent.isComma() && parent.getFirstChild() != n)) {
3065 |       return getBestLValue(parent);
3066 |     }
3067 |     return null;
3068 |   }

===== M0072 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.branchedFlowThrough(Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 134-246
Documentation:

Method code:
134 |   @Override
135 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
136 |   List<FlowScope> branchedFlowThrough(Node source, FlowScope input) {
137 |     // NOTE(nicksantos): Right now, we just treat ON_EX edges like UNCOND
138 |     // edges. If we wanted to be perfect, we'd actually JOIN all the out
139 |     // lattices of this flow with the in lattice, and then make that the out
140 |     // lattice for the ON_EX edge. But it's probably to expensive to be
141 |     // worthwhile.
142 |     FlowScope output = flowThrough(source, input);
143 |     Node condition = null;
144 |     FlowScope conditionFlowScope = null;
145 |     BooleanOutcomePair conditionOutcomes = null;
146 | 
147 |     List<DiGraphEdge<Node, Branch>> branchEdges = getCfg().getOutEdges(source);
148 |     List<FlowScope> result = Lists.newArrayListWithCapacity(branchEdges.size());
149 |     for (DiGraphEdge<Node, Branch> branchEdge : branchEdges) {
150 |       Branch branch = branchEdge.getValue();
151 |       FlowScope newScope = output;
152 | 
153 |       switch (branch) {
154 |         case ON_TRUE:
155 |           if (NodeUtil.isForIn(source)) {
156 |             // item is assigned a property name, so its type should be string.
157 |             Node item = source.getFirstChild();
158 |             Node obj = item.getNext();
159 | 
160 |             FlowScope informed = traverse(obj, output.createChildFlowScope());
161 | 
162 |             if (item.isVar()) {
163 |               item = item.getFirstChild();
164 |             }
165 |             if (item.isName()) {
166 |               JSType iterKeyType = getNativeType(STRING_TYPE);
167 |               ObjectType objType = getJSType(obj).dereference();
168 |               JSType objIndexType = objType == null ?
169 |                   null : objType.getIndexType();
170 |               if (objIndexType != null && !objIndexType.isUnknownType()) {
171 |                 JSType narrowedKeyType =
172 |                     iterKeyType.getGreatestSubtype(objIndexType);
173 |                 if (!narrowedKeyType.isEmptyType()) {
174 |                   iterKeyType = narrowedKeyType;
175 |                 }
176 |               }
177 |               redeclareSimpleVar(informed, item, iterKeyType);
178 |             }
179 |             newScope = informed;
180 |             break;
181 |           }
182 | 
183 |           // FALL THROUGH
184 | 
185 |         case ON_FALSE:
186 |           if (condition == null) {
187 |             condition = NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(source);
188 |             if (condition == null && source.isCase()) {
189 |               condition = source;
190 | 
191 |               // conditionFlowScope is cached from previous iterations
192 |               // of the loop.
193 |               if (conditionFlowScope == null) {
194 |                 conditionFlowScope = traverse(
195 |                     condition.getFirstChild(), output.createChildFlowScope());
196 |               }
197 |             }
198 |           }
199 | 
200 |           if (condition != null) {
201 |             if (condition.isAnd() ||
202 |                 condition.isOr()) {
203 |               // When handling the short-circuiting binary operators,
204 |               // the outcome scope on true can be different than the outcome
205 |               // scope on false.
206 |               //
207 |               // TODO(nicksantos): The "right" way to do this is to
208 |               // carry the known outcome all the way through the
209 |               // recursive traversal, so that we can construct a
210 |               // different flow scope based on the outcome. However,
211 |               // this would require a bunch of code and a bunch of
212 |               // extra computation for an edge case. This seems to be
213 |               // a "good enough" approximation.
214 | 
215 |               // conditionOutcomes is cached from previous iterations
216 |               // of the loop.
217 |               if (conditionOutcomes == null) {
218 |                 conditionOutcomes = condition.isAnd() ?
219 |                     traverseAnd(condition, output.createChildFlowScope()) :
220 |                     traverseOr(condition, output.createChildFlowScope());
221 |               }
222 |               newScope =
223 |                   reverseInterpreter.getPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
224 |                       condition,
225 |                       conditionOutcomes.getOutcomeFlowScope(
226 |                           condition.getType(), branch == Branch.ON_TRUE),
227 |                       branch == Branch.ON_TRUE);
228 |             } else {
229 |               // conditionFlowScope is cached from previous iterations
230 |               // of the loop.
231 |               if (conditionFlowScope == null) {
232 |                 conditionFlowScope =
233 |                     traverse(condition, output.createChildFlowScope());
234 |               }
235 |               newScope =
236 |                   reverseInterpreter.getPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
237 |                       condition, conditionFlowScope, branch == Branch.ON_TRUE);
238 |             }
239 |           }
240 |           break;
241 |       }
242 | 
243 |       result.add(newScope.optimize());
244 |     }
245 |     return result;
246 |   }

288 |   public void traverseRoots(List<Node> roots) {
289 |     if (roots.isEmpty()) {
290 |       return;
291 |     }
292 | 
293 |     try {
294 |       Node scopeRoot = roots.get(0).getParent();
295 |       Preconditions.checkState(scopeRoot != null);
296 |

298 |       sourceName = "";

===== M0081 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.computeFallThrough(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 796-810
Documentation:
/**
   * Computes the destination node of n when we want to fallthrough into the
   * subtree of n. We don't always create a CFG edge into n itself because of
   * DOs and FORs.
   */
Method code:
796 |   static Node computeFallThrough(Node n) {
797 |     switch (n.getType()) {
798 |       case Token.DO:
799 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getFirstChild());
800 |       case Token.FOR:
801 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(n)) {
802 |           return n.getFirstChild().getNext();
803 |         }
804 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getFirstChild());
805 |       case Token.LABEL:
806 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getLastChild());
807 |       default:
808 |         return n;
809 |     }
810 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseParamTypeExpressionAnnotation(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1590-1625
Documentation:
/**
   * ParamTypeExpressionAnnotation :=
   *     '{' OptionalParameterType '}' |
   *     '{' TopLevelTypeExpression '}' |
   *     '{' '...' TopLevelTypeExpression '}'
   *
   * OptionalParameterType :=
   *     TopLevelTypeExpression '='
   */
Method code:
1590 |   private Node parseParamTypeExpressionAnnotation(JsDocToken token) {
1591 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(token == JsDocToken.LC);
1592 | 
1593 |     skipEOLs();
1594 | 
1595 |     boolean restArg = false;
1596 |     token = next();
1597 |     if (token == JsDocToken.ELLIPSIS) {
1598 |       token = next();
1599 |       if (token == JsDocToken.RC) {
1600 |         // EMPTY represents the UNKNOWN type in the Type AST.
1601 |         return wrapNode(Token.ELLIPSIS, IR.empty());
1602 |       }
1603 |       restArg = true;
1604 |     }
1605 | 
1606 |     Node typeNode = parseTopLevelTypeExpression(token);
1607 |     if (typeNode != null) {
1608 |       skipEOLs();
1609 |       if (restArg) {
1610 |         typeNode = wrapNode(Token.ELLIPSIS, typeNode);
1611 |       } else if (match(JsDocToken.EQUALS)) {
1612 |         next();
1613 |         skipEOLs();
1614 |         typeNode = wrapNode(Token.EQUALS, typeNode);
1615 |       }
1616 | 
1617 |       if (!match(JsDocToken.RC)) {
1618 |         reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.rc");
1619 |       } else {
1620 |         next();
1621 |       }
1622 |     }
1623 | 
1624 |     return typeNode;
1625 |   }

===== M0095 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.getPropertyType(JSType,String,Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 1209-1260
Documentation:

Method code:
1209 |   private JSType getPropertyType(JSType objType, String propName,
1210 |       Node n, FlowScope scope) {
1211 |     // We often have a couple of different types to choose from for the
1212 |     // property. Ordered by accuracy, we have
1213 |     // 1) A locally inferred qualified name (which is in the FlowScope)
1214 |     // 2) A globally declared qualified name (which is in the FlowScope)
1215 |     // 3) A property on the owner type (which is on objType)
1216 |     // 4) A name in the type registry (as a last resort)
1217 |     JSType unknownType = getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1218 |     JSType propertyType = null;
1219 |     boolean isLocallyInferred = false;
1220 | 
1221 |     // Scopes sometimes contain inferred type info about qualified names.
1222 |     String qualifiedName = n.getQualifiedName();
1223 |     StaticSlot<JSType> var = scope.getSlot(qualifiedName);
1224 |     if (var != null) {
1225 |       JSType varType = var.getType();
1226 |       if (varType != null) {
1227 |         boolean isDeclared = !var.isTypeInferred();
1228 |         isLocallyInferred = (var != syntacticScope.getSlot(qualifiedName));
1229 |         if (isDeclared || isLocallyInferred) {
1230 |           propertyType = varType;
1231 |         }
1232 |       }
1233 |     }
1234 | 
1235 |     if (propertyType == null && objType != null) {
1236 |       JSType foundType = objType.findPropertyType(propName);
1237 |       if (foundType != null) {
1238 |         propertyType = foundType;
1239 |       }
1240 |     }
1241 | 
1242 |     if ((propertyType == null || propertyType.isUnknownType())
1243 |         && qualifiedName != null) {
1244 |       // If we find this node in the registry, then we can infer its type.
1245 |       ObjectType regType = ObjectType.cast(registry.getType(qualifiedName));
1246 |       if (regType != null) {
1247 |         propertyType = regType.getConstructor();
1248 |       }
1249 |     }
1250 | 
1251 |     if (propertyType == null) {
1252 |       return getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1253 |     } else if (propertyType.isEquivalentTo(unknownType) && isLocallyInferred) {
1254 |       // If the type has been checked in this scope,
1255 |       // then use CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE instead to indicate that.
1256 |       return getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1257 |     } else {
1258 |       return propertyType;
1259 |     }
1260 |   }

===== M0117 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitGetProp(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1303-1321
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits a GETPROP node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   * @param parent The parent of <code>n</code>
   */
Method code:
1303 |   private void visitGetProp(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
1304 |     // obj.prop or obj.method()
1305 |     // Lots of types can appear on the left, a call to a void function can
1306 |     // never be on the left. getPropertyType will decide what is acceptable
1307 |     // and what isn't.
1308 |     Node property = n.getLastChild();
1309 |     Node objNode = n.getFirstChild();
1310 |     JSType childType = getJSType(objNode);
1311 | 
1312 |     if (childType.isDict()) {
1313 |       report(t, property, TypeValidator.ILLEGAL_PROPERTY_ACCESS, "'.'", "dict");
1314 |     } else if (n.getJSType() != null && parent.isAssign()) {
1315 |       return;
1316 |     } else if (validator.expectNotNullOrUndefined(t, n, childType,
1317 |         "No properties on this expression", getNativeType(OBJECT_TYPE))) {
1318 |       checkPropertyAccess(childType, property.getString(), t, n);
1319 |     }
1320 |     ensureTyped(t, n);
1321 |   }

/**
   * Expect that the first type can be cast to the second type. The first type
   * should be either a subtype or supertype of the second.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal.
   * @param n The node where warnings should point.

===== M0126 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.prioritizeFromEntryNode(DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 196-215
Documentation:
/**
   * Given an entry node, find all the nodes reachable from that node
   * and prioritize them.
   */
Method code:
196 |   private void prioritizeFromEntryNode(DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> entry) {
197 |     PriorityQueue<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>> worklist =
198 |         new PriorityQueue<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>>(10, priorityComparator);
199 |     worklist.add(entry);
200 | 
201 |     while (!worklist.isEmpty()) {
202 |       DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> current = worklist.remove();
203 |       if (nodePriorities.containsKey(current)) {
204 |         continue;
205 |       }
206 | 
207 |       nodePriorities.put(current, ++priorityCounter);
208 | 
209 |       List<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>> successors =
210 |           cfg.getDirectedSuccNodes(current);
211 |       for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : successors) {
212 |         worklist.add(candidate);
213 |       }
214 |     }
215 |   }

===== M0139 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ArrowType.hasEqualParameters(ArrowType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ArrowType.java
Original range: 177-200
Documentation:
/**
   * @return True if our parameter spec is equal to {@code that}'s parameter
   *     spec.
   */
Method code:
177 |   boolean hasEqualParameters(ArrowType that) {
178 |     Node thisParam = parameters.getFirstChild();
179 |     Node otherParam = that.parameters.getFirstChild();
180 |     while (thisParam != null && otherParam != null) {
181 |       JSType thisParamType = thisParam.getJSType();
182 |       JSType otherParamType = otherParam.getJSType();
183 |       if (thisParamType != null) {
184 |         // Both parameter lists give a type for this param, it should be equal
185 |         if (otherParamType != null &&
186 |             !thisParamType.isEquivalentTo(otherParamType)) {
187 |           return false;
188 |         }
189 |       } else {
190 |         if (otherParamType != null) {
191 |           return false;
192 |         }
193 |       }
194 |       thisParam = thisParam.getNext();
195 |       otherParam = otherParam.getNext();
196 |     }
197 |     // One of the parameters is null, so the types are only equal if both
198 |     // parameter lists are null (they are equal).
199 |     return thisParam == otherParam;
200 |   }

===== M0141 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.ensurePropertyDefined(Node,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 510-555
Documentation:
/**
   * Defines a property if the property has not been defined yet.
   */
Method code:
510 |   private void ensurePropertyDefined(Node getprop, JSType rightType) {
511 |     String propName = getprop.getLastChild().getString();
512 |     JSType nodeType = getJSType(getprop.getFirstChild());
513 |     ObjectType objectType = ObjectType.cast(
514 |         nodeType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined());
515 |     if (objectType == null) {
516 |       registry.registerPropertyOnType(propName, nodeType);
517 |     } else {
518 |       if (ensurePropertyDeclaredHelper(getprop, objectType)) {
519 |         return;
520 |       }
521 | 
522 |       if (!objectType.isPropertyTypeDeclared(propName)) {
523 |         // We do not want a "stray" assign to define an inferred property
524 |         // for every object of this type in the program. So we use a heuristic
525 |         // approach to determine whether to infer the property.
526 |         //
527 |         // 1) If the property is already defined, join it with the previously
528 |         //    inferred type.
529 |         // 2) If this isn't an instance object, define it.
530 |         // 3) If the property of an object is being assigned in the constructor,
531 |         //    define it.
532 |         // 4) If this is a stub, define it.
533 |         // 5) Otherwise, do not define the type, but declare it in the registry
534 |         //    so that we can use it for missing property checks.
535 |         if (objectType.hasProperty(propName) ||
536 |             !objectType.isInstanceType()) {
537 |           if ("prototype".equals(propName)) {
538 |             objectType.defineDeclaredProperty(
539 |                 propName, rightType, getprop);
540 |           } else {
541 |             objectType.defineInferredProperty(
542 |                 propName, rightType, getprop);
543 |           }
544 |         } else {
545 |           if (getprop.getFirstChild().isThis() &&
546 |               getJSType(syntacticScope.getRootNode()).isConstructor()) {
547 |             objectType.defineInferredProperty(
548 |                 propName, rightType, getprop);
549 |           } else {
550 |             registry.registerPropertyOnType(propName, objectType);
551 |           }
552 |         }
553 |       }
554 |     }
555 |   }

===== M0145 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 459-806
Documentation:
/**
   * This is the meat of the type checking.  It is basically one big switch,
   * with each case representing one type of parse tree node.  The individual
   * cases are usually pretty straightforward.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   * @param parent The parent of the node n.
   */
Method code:
459 |   @Override
460 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
461 |     JSType childType;
462 |     JSType leftType, rightType;
463 |     Node left, right;
464 |     // To be explicitly set to false if the node is not typeable.
465 |     boolean typeable = true;
466 | 
467 |     switch (n.getType()) {
468 |       case Token.NAME:
469 |         typeable = visitName(t, n, parent);
470 |         break;
471 | 
472 |       case Token.PARAM_LIST:
473 |         typeable = false;
474 |         break;
475 | 
476 |       case Token.COMMA:
477 |         ensureTyped(t, n, getJSType(n.getLastChild()));
478 |         break;
479 | 
480 |       case Token.TRUE:
481 |       case Token.FALSE:
482 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
483 |         break;
484 | 
485 |       case Token.THIS:
486 |         ensureTyped(t, n, t.getScope().getTypeOfThis());
487 |         break;
488 | 
489 |       case Token.NULL:
490 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NULL_TYPE);
491 |         break;
492 | 
493 |       case Token.NUMBER:
494 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NUMBER_TYPE);
495 |         break;
496 | 
497 |       case Token.STRING:
498 |         ensureTyped(t, n, STRING_TYPE);
499 |         break;
500 | 
501 |       case Token.STRING_KEY:
502 |         typeable = false;
503 |         break;
504 | 
505 |       case Token.GETTER_DEF:
506 |       case Token.SETTER_DEF:
507 |         // Object literal keys are handled with OBJECTLIT
508 |         break;
509 | 
510 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
511 |         ensureTyped(t, n, ARRAY_TYPE);
512 |         break;
513 | 
514 |       case Token.REGEXP:
515 |         ensureTyped(t, n, REGEXP_TYPE);
516 |         break;
517 | 
518 |       case Token.GETPROP:
519 |         visitGetProp(t, n, parent);
520 |         typeable = !(parent.isAssign() &&
521 |                      parent.getFirstChild() == n);
522 |         break;
523 | 
524 |       case Token.GETELEM:
525 |         visitGetElem(t, n);
526 |         // The type of GETELEM is always unknown, so no point counting that.
527 |         // If that unknown leaks elsewhere (say by an assignment to another
528 |         // variable), then it will be counted.
529 |         typeable = false;
530 |         break;
531 | 
532 |       case Token.VAR:
533 |         visitVar(t, n);
534 |         typeable = false;
535 |         break;
536 | 
537 |       case Token.NEW:
538 |         visitNew(t, n);
539 |         break;
540 | 
541 |       case Token.CALL:
542 |         visitCall(t, n);
543 |         typeable = !parent.isExprResult();
544 |         break;
545 | 
546 |       case Token.RETURN:
547 |         visitReturn(t, n);
548 |         typeable = false;
549 |         break;
550 | 
551 |       case Token.DEC:
552 |       case Token.INC:
553 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
554 |         validator.expectNumber(
555 |             t, left, getJSType(left), "increment/decrement");
556 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NUMBER_TYPE);
557 |         break;
558 | 
559 |       case Token.NOT:
560 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
561 |         break;
562 | 
563 |       case Token.VOID:
564 |         ensureTyped(t, n, VOID_TYPE);
565 |         break;
566 | 
567 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
568 |         ensureTyped(t, n, STRING_TYPE);
569 |         break;
570 | 
571 |       case Token.BITNOT:
572 |         childType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
573 |         if (!childType.matchesInt32Context()) {
574 |           report(t, n, BIT_OPERATION, NodeUtil.opToStr(n.getType()),
575 |               childType.toString());
576 |         }
577 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NUMBER_TYPE);
578 |         break;
579 | 
580 |       case Token.POS:
581 |       case Token.NEG:
582 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
583 |         validator.expectNumber(t, left, getJSType(left), "sign operator");
584 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NUMBER_TYPE);
585 |         break;
586 | 
587 |       case Token.EQ:
588 |       case Token.NE:
589 |       case Token.SHEQ:
590 |       case Token.SHNE: {
591 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
592 |         right = n.getLastChild();
593 | 
594 |         if (left.isTypeOf()) {
595 |           if (right.isString()) {
596 |             checkTypeofString(t, right, right.getString());
597 |           }
598 |         } else if (right.isTypeOf() && left.isString()) {
599 |           checkTypeofString(t, left, left.getString());
600 |         }
601 | 
602 |         leftType = getJSType(left);
603 |         rightType = getJSType(right);
604 | 
605 |         // We do not want to warn about explicit comparisons to VOID. People
606 |         // often do this if they think their type annotations screwed up.
607 |         //
608 |         // We do want to warn about cases where people compare things like
609 |         // (Array|null) == (Function|null)
610 |         // because it probably means they screwed up.
611 |         //
612 |         // This heuristic here is not perfect, but should catch cases we
613 |         // care about without too many false negatives.
614 |         JSType leftTypeRestricted = leftType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
615 |         JSType rightTypeRestricted = rightType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
616 | 
617 |         TernaryValue result = TernaryValue.UNKNOWN;
618 |         if (n.getType() == Token.EQ || n.getType() == Token.NE) {
619 |           result = leftTypeRestricted.testForEquality(rightTypeRestricted);
620 |           if (n.isNE()) {
621 |             result = result.not();
622 |           }
623 |         } else {
624 |           // SHEQ or SHNE
625 |           if (!leftTypeRestricted.canTestForShallowEqualityWith(
626 |                   rightTypeRestricted)) {
627 |             result = n.getType() == Token.SHEQ ?
628 |                 TernaryValue.FALSE : TernaryValue.TRUE;
629 |           }
630 |         }
631 | 
632 |         if (result != TernaryValue.UNKNOWN) {
633 |           report(t, n, DETERMINISTIC_TEST, leftType.toString(),
634 |               rightType.toString(), result.toString());
635 |         }
636 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
637 |         break;
638 |       }
639 | 
640 |       case Token.LT:
641 |       case Token.LE:
642 |       case Token.GT:
643 |       case Token.GE:
644 |         leftType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
645 |         rightType = getJSType(n.getLastChild());
646 |         if (rightType.isNumber()) {
647 |           validator.expectNumber(
648 |               t, n, leftType, "left side of numeric comparison");
649 |         } else if (leftType.isNumber()) {
650 |           validator.expectNumber(
651 |               t, n, rightType, "right side of numeric comparison");
652 |         } else if (leftType.matchesNumberContext() &&
653 |                    rightType.matchesNumberContext()) {
654 |           // OK.
655 |         } else {
656 |           // Whether the comparison is numeric will be determined at runtime
657 |           // each time the expression is evaluated. Regardless, both operands
658 |           // should match a string context.
659 |           String message = "left side of comparison";
660 |           validator.expectString(t, n, leftType, message);
661 |           validator.expectNotNullOrUndefined(
662 |               t, n, leftType, message, getNativeType(STRING_TYPE));
663 |           message = "right side of comparison";
664 |           validator.expectString(t, n, rightType, message);
665 |           validator.expectNotNullOrUndefined(
666 |               t, n, rightType, message, getNativeType(STRING_TYPE));
667 |         }
668 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
669 |         break;
670 | 
671 |       case Token.IN:
672 |         validator.expectObject(t, n, getJSType(n.getLastChild()),
673 |                                "'in' requires an object");
674 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
675 |         validator.expectString(t, left, getJSType(left), "left side of 'in'");
676 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
677 |         break;
678 | 
679 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
680 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
681 |         right = n.getLastChild();
682 |         rightType = getJSType(right).restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
683 |         validator.expectAnyObject(
684 |             t, left, getJSType(left), "deterministic instanceof yields false");
685 |         validator.expectActualObject(
686 |             t, right, rightType, "instanceof requires an object");
687 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
688 |         break;
689 | 
690 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
691 |         visitAssign(t, n);
692 |         typeable = false;
693 |         break;
694 | 
695 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
696 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
697 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
698 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
699 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
700 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
701 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
702 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
703 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
704 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
705 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
706 |       case Token.LSH:
707 |       case Token.RSH:
708 |       case Token.URSH:
709 |       case Token.DIV:
710 |       case Token.MOD:
711 |       case Token.BITOR:
712 |       case Token.BITXOR:
713 |       case Token.BITAND:
714 |       case Token.SUB:
715 |       case Token.ADD:
716 |       case Token.MUL:
717 |         visitBinaryOperator(n.getType(), t, n);
718 |         break;
719 | 
720 |       case Token.DELPROP:
721 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
722 |         break;
723 | 
724 |       case Token.CASE:
725 |         JSType switchType = getJSType(parent.getFirstChild());
726 |         JSType caseType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
727 |         validator.expectSwitchMatchesCase(t, n, switchType, caseType);
728 |         typeable = false;
729 |         break;
730 | 
731 |       case Token.WITH: {
732 |         Node child = n.getFirstChild();
733 |         childType = getJSType(child);
734 |         validator.expectObject(t, child, childType, "with requires an object");
735 |         typeable = false;
736 |         break;
737 |       }
738 | 
739 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
740 |         visitFunction(t, n);
741 |         break;
742 | 
743 |       // These nodes have no interesting type behavior.
744 |       case Token.LABEL:
745 |       case Token.LABEL_NAME:
746 |       case Token.SWITCH:
747 |       case Token.BREAK:
748 |       case Token.CATCH:
749 |       case Token.TRY:
750 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
751 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
752 |       case Token.BLOCK:
753 |       case Token.EMPTY:
754 |       case Token.DEFAULT_CASE:
755 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
756 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
757 |       case Token.THROW:
758 |         typeable = false;
759 |         break;
760 | 
761 |       // These nodes require data flow analysis.
762 |       case Token.DO:
763 |       case Token.FOR:
764 |       case Token.IF:
765 |       case Token.WHILE:
766 |         typeable = false;
767 |         break;
768 | 
769 |       // These nodes are typed during the type inference.
770 |       case Token.AND:
771 |       case Token.HOOK:
772 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
773 |       case Token.OR:
774 |         if (n.getJSType() != null) { // If we didn't run type inference.
775 |           ensureTyped(t, n);
776 |         } else {
777 |           // If this is an enum, then give that type to the objectlit as well.
778 |           if ((n.isObjectLit())
779 |               && (parent.getJSType() instanceof EnumType)) {
780 |             ensureTyped(t, n, parent.getJSType());
781 |           } else {
782 |             ensureTyped(t, n);
783 |           }
784 |         }
785 |         if (n.isObjectLit()) {
786 |           for (Node key : n.children()) {
787 |             visitObjLitKey(t, key, n);
788 |           }
789 |         }
790 |         break;
791 | 
792 |       default:
793 |         report(t, n, UNEXPECTED_TOKEN, Token.name(n.getType()));
794 |         ensureTyped(t, n);
795 |         break;
796 |     }
797 | 
798 |     // Don't count externs since the user's code may not even use that part.
799 |     typeable = typeable && !inExterns;
800 | 
801 |     if (typeable) {
802 |       doPercentTypedAccounting(t, n);
803 |     }
804 | 
805 |     checkNoTypeCheckSection(n, false);
806 |   }

===== M0159 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.parseDirectives(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 470-485
Documentation:
/**
     * Parse the directives, encode them in the AST, and remove their nodes.
     *
     * For information on ES5 directives, see section 14.1 of
     * ECMA-262, Edition 5.
     *
     * It would be nice if Rhino would eventually take care of this for
     * us, but right now their directive-processing is a one-off.
     */
Method code:
470 |     private void parseDirectives(Node node) {
471 |       // Remove all the directives, and encode them in the AST.
472 |       Set<String> directives = null;
473 |       while (isDirective(node.getFirstChild())) {
474 |         String directive = node.removeFirstChild().getFirstChild().getString();
475 |         if (directives == null) {
476 |           directives = Sets.newHashSet(directive);
477 |         } else {
478 |           directives.add(directive);
479 |         }
480 |       }
481 | 
482 |       if (directives != null) {
483 |         node.setDirectives(directives);
484 |       }
485 |     }

===== M0163 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitAssign(NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 843-945
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits an assignment <code>lvalue = rvalue</code>. If the
   * <code>lvalue</code> is a prototype modification, we change the schema
   * of the object type it is referring to.
   * @param t the traversal
   * @param assign the assign node
   * (<code>assign.isAssign()</code> is an implicit invariant)
   */
Method code:
843 |   private void visitAssign(NodeTraversal t, Node assign) {
844 |     JSDocInfo info = assign.getJSDocInfo();
845 |     Node lvalue = assign.getFirstChild();
846 |     Node rvalue = assign.getLastChild();
847 | 
848 |     // Check property sets to 'object.property' when 'object' is known.
849 |     if (lvalue.isGetProp()) {
850 |       Node object = lvalue.getFirstChild();
851 |       JSType objectJsType = getJSType(object);
852 |       String property = lvalue.getLastChild().getString();
853 | 
854 |       // the first name in this getprop refers to an interface
855 |       // we perform checks in addition to the ones below
856 |       if (object.isGetProp()) {
857 |         JSType jsType = getJSType(object.getFirstChild());
858 |         if (jsType.isInterface() &&
859 |             object.getLastChild().getString().equals("prototype")) {
860 |           visitInterfaceGetprop(t, assign, object, property, lvalue, rvalue);
861 |         }
862 |       }
863 | 
864 |       checkEnumAlias(t, info, rvalue);
865 | 
866 |       // Prototype assignments are special, because they actually affect
867 |       // the definition of a class. These are mostly validated
868 |       // during TypedScopeCreator, and we only look for the "dumb" cases here.
869 |       // object.prototype = ...;
870 |       if (property.equals("prototype")) {
871 |         if (objectJsType != null && objectJsType.isFunctionType()) {
872 |           FunctionType functionType = objectJsType.toMaybeFunctionType();
873 |           if (functionType.isConstructor()) {
874 |             JSType rvalueType = rvalue.getJSType();
875 |             validator.expectObject(t, rvalue, rvalueType,
876 |                 OVERRIDING_PROTOTYPE_WITH_NON_OBJECT);
877 |             return;
878 |           }
879 |         }
880 |       }
881 | 
882 |       // The generic checks for 'object.property' when 'object' is known,
883 |       // and 'property' is declared on it.
884 |       // object.property = ...;
885 |       ObjectType type = ObjectType.cast(
886 |           objectJsType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined());
887 |       if (type != null) {
888 |         if (type.hasProperty(property) &&
889 |             !type.isPropertyTypeInferred(property) &&
890 |             !propertyIsImplicitCast(type, property)) {
891 |           JSType expectedType = type.getPropertyType(property);
892 |           if (!expectedType.isUnknownType()) {
893 |             validator.expectCanAssignToPropertyOf(
894 |                 t, assign, getJSType(rvalue),
895 |                 expectedType, object, property);
896 |             checkPropertyInheritanceOnGetpropAssign(
897 |                 t, assign, object, property, info, expectedType);
898 |             return;
899 |           }
900 |         }
901 |       }
902 | 
903 |       // If we couldn't get the property type with normal object property
904 |       // lookups, then check inheritance anyway with the unknown type.
905 |       checkPropertyInheritanceOnGetpropAssign(
906 |           t, assign, object, property, info, getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE));
907 |     }
908 | 
909 |     // Check qualified name sets to 'object' and 'object.property'.
910 |     // This can sometimes handle cases when the type of 'object' is not known.
911 |     // e.g.,
912 |     // var obj = createUnknownType();
913 |     // /** @type {number} */ obj.foo = true;
914 |     JSType leftType = getJSType(lvalue);
915 |     if (lvalue.isQualifiedName()) {
916 |       // variable with inferred type case
917 |       JSType rvalueType = getJSType(assign.getLastChild());
918 |       Var var = t.getScope().getVar(lvalue.getQualifiedName());
919 |       if (var != null) {
920 |         if (var.isTypeInferred()) {
921 |           return;
922 |         }
923 | 
924 |         if (NodeUtil.getRootOfQualifiedName(lvalue).isThis() &&
925 |             t.getScope() != var.getScope()) {
926 |           // Don't look at "this.foo" variables from other scopes.
927 |           return;
928 |         }
929 | 
930 |         if (var.getType() != null) {
931 |           leftType = var.getType();
932 |         }
933 |       }
934 |     }
935 | 
936 |     // Fall through case for arbitrary LHS and arbitrary RHS.
937 |     Node rightChild = assign.getLastChild();
938 |     JSType rightType = getJSType(rightChild);
939 |     if (validator.expectCanAssignTo(
940 |             t, assign, rightType, leftType, "assignment")) {
941 |       ensureTyped(t, assign, rightType);
942 |     } else {
943 |       ensureTyped(t, assign);
944 |     }
945 |   }

/**
   * Defines a property whose type is inferred.
   * @param propertyName the property's name
   * @param type the type
   * @param propertyNode the node corresponding to the inferred definition of
   *        property that might later be accessed using {@code getPropertyNode}.
   */
Method code:
302 |   public final boolean defineInferredProperty(String propertyName,
303 |       JSType type, Node propertyNode) {
304 |     StaticSlot<JSType> originalSlot = getSlot(propertyName);
305 |     if (hasProperty(propertyName)) {

307 |         // We never want to hide a declared property with an inferred property.
308 |         return true;
309 |       }

321 |     // to register this after defining the property.
322 |     registry.registerPropertyOnType(propertyName, this);
323 |

325 |   }

===== M0174 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 1007-1049
Documentation:
/**
   * A function is a subtype of another if their call methods are related via
   * subtyping and {@code this} is a subtype of {@code that} with regard to
   * the prototype chain.
   */
Method code:
1007 |   @Override
1008 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
1009 |     if (JSType.isSubtypeHelper(this, that)) {
1010 |       return true;
1011 |     }
1012 | 
1013 |     if (that.isFunctionType()) {
1014 |       FunctionType other = that.toMaybeFunctionType();
1015 |       if (other.isInterface()) {
1016 |         // Any function can be assigned to an interface function.
1017 |         return true;
1018 |       }
1019 |       if (this.isInterface()) {
1020 |         // An interface function cannot be assigned to anything.
1021 |         return false;
1022 |       }
1023 |       // If functionA is a subtype of functionB, then their "this" types
1024 |       // should be contravariant. However, this causes problems because
1025 |       // of the way we enforce overrides. Because function(this:SubFoo)
1026 |       // is not a subtype of function(this:Foo), our override check treats
1027 |       // this as an error. It also screws up out standard method
1028 |       // for aliasing constructors. Let's punt on all this for now.
1029 |       // TODO(nicksantos): fix this.
1030 |       boolean treatThisTypesAsCovariant =
1031 |         // If either one of these is a ctor, skip 'this' checking.
1032 |         this.isConstructor() || other.isConstructor() ||
1033 | 
1034 |         // An interface 'this'-type is non-restrictive.
1035 |         // In practical terms, if C implements I, and I has a method m,
1036 |         // then any m doesn't necessarily have to C#m's 'this'
1037 |         // type doesn't need to match I.
1038 |         (other.typeOfThis.getConstructor() != null &&
1039 |              other.typeOfThis.getConstructor().isInterface()) ||
1040 | 
1041 |         // If one of the 'this' types is covariant of the other,
1042 |         // then we'll treat them as covariant (see comment above).
1043 |         other.typeOfThis.isSubtype(this.typeOfThis) ||
1044 |         this.typeOfThis.isSubtype(other.typeOfThis);
1045 |       return treatThisTypesAsCovariant && this.call.isSubtype(other.call);
1046 |     }
1047 | 
1048 |     return getNativeType(JSTypeNative.FUNCTION_PROTOTYPE).isSubtype(that);
1049 |   }

===== M0195 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 467-496
Documentation:

Method code:
467 |     @Override
468 |     public final boolean shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
469 |       inputId = t.getInputId();
470 |       if (n.isFunction() ||
471 |           n.isScript()) {
472 |         Preconditions.checkNotNull(inputId);
473 |         sourceName = NodeUtil.getSourceName(n);
474 |       }
475 | 
476 |       // We do want to traverse the name of a named function, but we don't
477 |       // want to traverse the arguments or body.
478 |       boolean descend = parent == null || !parent.isFunction() ||
479 |           n == parent.getFirstChild() || parent == scope.getRootNode();
480 | 
481 |       if (descend) {
482 |         // Handle hoisted functions on pre-order traversal, so that they
483 |         // get hit before other things in the scope.
484 |         if (NodeUtil.isStatementParent(n)) {
485 |           for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
486 |                child != null;
487 |                child = child.getNext()) {
488 |             if (NodeUtil.isHoistedFunctionDeclaration(child)) {
489 |               defineFunctionLiteral(child, n);
490 |             }
491 |           }
492 |         }
493 |       }
494 | 
495 |       return descend;
496 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtypeHelper(JSType,JSType)

   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common subtyping
   * cases.
   */
Method code:
1106 |   static boolean isSubtypeHelper(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
1107 |     // unknown
1108 |     if (thatType.isUnknownType()) {
1109 |       return true;
1110 |     }
1111 |     // all type
1112 |     if (thatType.isAllType()) {
1113 |       return true;
1114 |     }
1115 |     // equality
1116 |     if (thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType)) {
1117 |       return true;
1118 |     }
1119 |     // unions
1120 |     if (thatType.isUnionType()) {
1121 |       UnionType union = thatType.toMaybeUnionType();
1122 |       for (JSType element : union.alternates) {
1123 |         if (thisType.isSubtype(element)) {
1124 |           return true;
1125 |         }
1126 |       }
1127 |     }
1128 |     // proxy types
1129 |     if (thatType instanceof ProxyObjectType) {
1130 |       return thisType.isSubtype(
1131 |           ((ProxyObjectType) thatType).getReferencedTypeInternal());
1132 |     }
1133 |     return false;
1134 |   }

===== M0201 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 217-292
Documentation:

Method code:
217 |   @Override
218 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(
219 |       NodeTraversal nodeTraversal, Node n, Node parent) {
220 |     astPosition.put(n, astPositionCounter++);
221 | 
222 |     switch (n.getType()) {
223 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
224 |         if (shouldTraverseFunctions || n == cfg.getEntry().getValue()) {
225 |           exceptionHandler.push(n);
226 |           return true;
227 |         }
228 |         return false;
229 |       case Token.TRY:
230 |         exceptionHandler.push(n);
231 |         return true;
232 |     }
233 | 
234 |     /*
235 |      * We are going to stop the traversal depending on what the node's parent
236 |      * is.
237 |      *
238 |      * We are only interested in adding edges between nodes that change control
239 |      * flow. The most obvious ones are loops and IF-ELSE's. A statement
240 |      * transfers control to its next sibling.
241 |      *
242 |      * In case of an expression tree, there is no control flow within the tree
243 |      * even when there are short circuited operators and conditionals. When we
244 |      * are doing data flow analysis, we will simply synthesize lattices up the
245 |      * expression tree by finding the meet at each expression node.
246 |      *
247 |      * For example: within a Token.SWITCH, the expression in question does not
248 |      * change the control flow and need not to be considered.
249 |      */
250 |     if (parent != null) {
251 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
252 |         case Token.FOR:
253 |           // Only traverse the body of the for loop.
254 |           return n == parent.getLastChild();
255 | 
256 |         // Skip the conditions.
257 |         case Token.IF:
258 |         case Token.WHILE:
259 |         case Token.WITH:
260 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild();
261 |         case Token.DO:
262 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild().getNext();
263 |         // Only traverse the body of the cases
264 |         case Token.SWITCH:
265 |         case Token.CASE:
266 |         case Token.CATCH:
267 |         case Token.LABEL:
268 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild();
269 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
270 |           return n == parent.getFirstChild().getNext().getNext();
271 |         case Token.CONTINUE:
272 |         case Token.BREAK:
273 |         case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
274 |         case Token.VAR:
275 |         case Token.RETURN:
276 |         case Token.THROW:
277 |           return false;
278 |         case Token.TRY:
279 |           /* Just before we are about to visit the second child of the TRY node,
280 |            * we know that we will be visiting either the CATCH or the FINALLY.
281 |            * In other words, we know that the post order traversal of the TRY
282 |            * block has been finished, no more exceptions can be caught by the
283 |            * handler at this TRY block and should be taken out of the stack.
284 |            */
285 |           if (n == parent.getFirstChild().getNext()) {
286 |             Preconditions.checkState(exceptionHandler.peek() == parent);
287 |             exceptionHandler.pop();
288 |           }
289 |       }
290 |     }
291 |     return true;
292 |   }

===== M0202 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.lookAheadForTypeAnnotation()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 2365-2381
Documentation:
/**
   * Look ahead for a type annotation by advancing the character stream.
   * Does not modify the token stream.
   * This is kind of a hack, and is only necessary because we use the token
   * stream to parse types, but need the underlying character stream to get
   * JsDoc descriptions.
   * @return Whether we found a type annotation.
   */
Method code:
2365 |   private boolean lookAheadForTypeAnnotation() {
2366 |     boolean matchedLc = false;
2367 |     int c;
2368 |     while (true) {
2369 |       c = stream.getChar();
2370 |       if (c == ' ') {
2371 |         continue;
2372 |       } else if (c == '{') {
2373 |         matchedLc = true;
2374 |         break;
2375 |       } else {
2376 |         break;
2377 |       }
2378 |     }
2379 |     stream.ungetChar(c);
2380 |     return matchedLc;
2381 |   }

===== M0213 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.inferInheritance(JSDocInfo)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 328-383
Documentation:
/**
   * Infer the role of the function (whether it's a constructor or interface)
   * and what it inherits from in JSDocInfo.
   */
Method code:
328 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferInheritance(@Nullable JSDocInfo info) {
329 |     if (info != null) {
330 |       isConstructor = info.isConstructor();
331 |       makesStructs = info.makesStructs();
332 |       makesDicts = info.makesDicts();
333 |       isInterface = info.isInterface();
334 | 
335 |       if (makesStructs && !isConstructor) {
336 |         reportWarning(CONSTRUCTOR_REQUIRED, "@struct", fnName);
337 |       } else if (makesDicts && !isConstructor) {
338 |         reportWarning(CONSTRUCTOR_REQUIRED, "@dict", fnName);
339 |       }
340 | 
341 |       // base type
342 |       if (info.hasBaseType()) {
343 |         if (isConstructor) {
344 |           JSType maybeBaseType =
345 |               info.getBaseType().evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
346 |           if (maybeBaseType != null &&
347 |               maybeBaseType.setValidator(new ExtendedTypeValidator())) {
348 |             baseType = (ObjectType) maybeBaseType;
349 |           }
350 |         } else {
351 |           reportWarning(EXTENDS_WITHOUT_TYPEDEF, fnName);
352 |         }
353 |       }
354 | 
355 |       // implemented interfaces
356 |       if (isConstructor || isInterface) {
357 |         implementedInterfaces = Lists.newArrayList();
358 |         for (JSTypeExpression t : info.getImplementedInterfaces()) {
359 |           JSType maybeInterType = t.evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
360 |           if (maybeInterType != null &&
361 |               maybeInterType.setValidator(new ImplementedTypeValidator())) {
362 |             implementedInterfaces.add((ObjectType) maybeInterType);
363 |           }
364 |         }
365 |       } else if (info.getImplementedInterfaceCount() > 0) {
366 |         reportWarning(CONSTRUCTOR_REQUIRED, "@implements", fnName);
367 |       }
368 | 
369 |       // extended interfaces (for interface only)
370 |       if (isInterface) {
371 |         extendedInterfaces = Lists.newArrayList();
372 |         for (JSTypeExpression t : info.getExtendedInterfaces()) {
373 |           JSType maybeInterfaceType = t.evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
374 |           if (maybeInterfaceType != null &&
375 |               maybeInterfaceType.setValidator(new ExtendedTypeValidator())) {
376 |             extendedInterfaces.add((ObjectType) maybeInterfaceType);
377 |           }
378 |         }
379 |       }
380 |     }
381 | 
382 |     return this;
383 |   }

===== M0217 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.checkDeclaredPropertyInheritance(NodeTraversal,Node,FunctionType,String,JSDocInfo,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1070-1188
Documentation:
/**
   * Given a constructor type and a property name, check that the property has
   * the JSDoc annotation @override iff the property is declared on a
   * superclass. Several checks regarding inheritance correctness are also
   * performed.
   */
Method code:
1070 |   private void checkDeclaredPropertyInheritance(
1071 |       NodeTraversal t, Node n, FunctionType ctorType, String propertyName,
1072 |       JSDocInfo info, JSType propertyType) {
1073 |     // If the supertype doesn't resolve correctly, we've warned about this
1074 |     // already.
1075 |     if (hasUnknownOrEmptySupertype(ctorType)) {
1076 |       return;
1077 |     }
1078 | 
1079 |     FunctionType superClass = ctorType.getSuperClassConstructor();
1080 |     boolean superClassHasProperty = superClass != null &&
1081 |         superClass.getInstanceType().hasProperty(propertyName);
1082 |     boolean superClassHasDeclaredProperty = superClass != null &&
1083 |         superClass.getInstanceType().isPropertyTypeDeclared(propertyName);
1084 | 
1085 |     // For interface
1086 |     boolean superInterfaceHasProperty = false;
1087 |     boolean superInterfaceHasDeclaredProperty = false;
1088 |     if (ctorType.isInterface()) {
1089 |       for (ObjectType interfaceType : ctorType.getExtendedInterfaces()) {
1090 |         superInterfaceHasProperty =
1091 |             superInterfaceHasProperty ||
1092 |             interfaceType.hasProperty(propertyName);
1093 |         superInterfaceHasDeclaredProperty =
1094 |             superInterfaceHasDeclaredProperty ||
1095 |             interfaceType.isPropertyTypeDeclared(propertyName);
1096 |       }
1097 |     }
1098 |     boolean declaredOverride = info != null && info.isOverride();
1099 | 
1100 |     boolean foundInterfaceProperty = false;
1101 |     if (ctorType.isConstructor()) {
1102 |       for (JSType implementedInterface :
1103 |           ctorType.getAllImplementedInterfaces()) {
1104 |         if (implementedInterface.isUnknownType() ||
1105 |             implementedInterface.isEmptyType()) {
1106 |           continue;
1107 |         }
1108 |         FunctionType interfaceType =
1109 |             implementedInterface.toObjectType().getConstructor();
1110 |         Preconditions.checkNotNull(interfaceType);
1111 | 
1112 |         boolean interfaceHasProperty =
1113 |             interfaceType.getPrototype().hasProperty(propertyName);
1114 |         foundInterfaceProperty = foundInterfaceProperty ||
1115 |             interfaceHasProperty;
1116 |         if (reportMissingOverride.isOn()
1117 |             && !declaredOverride
1118 |             && interfaceHasProperty) {
1119 |           // @override not present, but the property does override an interface
1120 |           // property
1121 |           compiler.report(t.makeError(n, reportMissingOverride,
1122 |               HIDDEN_INTERFACE_PROPERTY, propertyName,
1123 |               interfaceType.getTopMostDefiningType(propertyName).toString()));
1124 |         }
1125 |       }
1126 |     }
1127 | 
1128 |     if (!declaredOverride
1129 |         && !superClassHasProperty
1130 |         && !superInterfaceHasProperty) {
1131 |       // nothing to do here, it's just a plain new property
1132 |       return;
1133 |     }
1134 | 
1135 |     ObjectType topInstanceType = superClassHasDeclaredProperty ?
1136 |         superClass.getTopMostDefiningType(propertyName) : null;
1137 |     boolean declaredLocally =
1138 |         ctorType.isConstructor() &&
1139 |         (ctorType.getPrototype().hasOwnProperty(propertyName) ||
1140 |          ctorType.getInstanceType().hasOwnProperty(propertyName));
1141 |     if (reportMissingOverride.isOn()
1142 |         && !declaredOverride
1143 |         && superClassHasDeclaredProperty
1144 |         && declaredLocally) {
1145 |       // @override not present, but the property does override a superclass
1146 |       // property
1147 |       compiler.report(t.makeError(n, reportMissingOverride,
1148 |           HIDDEN_SUPERCLASS_PROPERTY, propertyName,
1149 |           topInstanceType.toString()));
1150 |     }
1151 | 
1152 |     // @override is present and we have to check that it is ok
1153 |     if (superClassHasDeclaredProperty) {
1154 |       // there is a superclass implementation
1155 |       JSType superClassPropType =
1156 |           superClass.getInstanceType().getPropertyType(propertyName);
1157 |       if (!propertyType.canAssignTo(superClassPropType)) {
1158 |         compiler.report(
1159 |             t.makeError(n, HIDDEN_SUPERCLASS_PROPERTY_MISMATCH,
1160 |                 propertyName, topInstanceType.toString(),
1161 |                 superClassPropType.toString(), propertyType.toString()));
1162 |       }
1163 |     } else if (superInterfaceHasDeclaredProperty) {
1164 |       // there is an super interface property
1165 |       for (ObjectType interfaceType : ctorType.getExtendedInterfaces()) {
1166 |         if (interfaceType.hasProperty(propertyName)) {
1167 |           JSType superPropertyType =
1168 |               interfaceType.getPropertyType(propertyName);
1169 |           if (!propertyType.canAssignTo(superPropertyType)) {
1170 |             topInstanceType = interfaceType.getConstructor().
1171 |                 getTopMostDefiningType(propertyName);
1172 |             compiler.report(
1173 |                 t.makeError(n, HIDDEN_SUPERCLASS_PROPERTY_MISMATCH,
1174 |                     propertyName, topInstanceType.toString(),
1175 |                     superPropertyType.toString(),
1176 |                     propertyType.toString()));
1177 |           }
1178 |         }
1179 |       }
1180 |     } else if (!foundInterfaceProperty
1181 |         && !superClassHasProperty
1182 |         && !superInterfaceHasProperty) {
1183 |       // there is no superclass nor interface implementation
1184 |       compiler.report(
1185 |           t.makeError(n, UNKNOWN_OVERRIDE,
1186 |               propertyName, ctorType.getInstanceType().toString()));
1187 |     }
1188 |   }

/**

===== M0227 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseBasicTypeExpression(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1751-1777
Documentation:
/**
   * BasicTypeExpression := '*' | 'null' | 'undefined' | TypeName
   *     | FunctionType | UnionType | RecordType | ArrayType
   */
Method code:
1751 |   private Node parseBasicTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1752 |     if (token == JsDocToken.STAR) {
1753 |       return newNode(Token.STAR);
1754 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LB) {
1755 |       skipEOLs();
1756 |       return parseArrayType(next());
1757 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
1758 |       skipEOLs();
1759 |       return parseRecordType(next());
1760 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LP) {
1761 |       skipEOLs();
1762 |       return parseUnionType(next());
1763 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.STRING) {
1764 |       String string = stream.getString();
1765 |       if ("function".equals(string)) {
1766 |         skipEOLs();
1767 |         return parseFunctionType(next());
1768 |       } else if ("null".equals(string) || "undefined".equals(string)) {
1769 |         return newStringNode(string);
1770 |       } else {
1771 |         return parseTypeName(token);
1772 |       }
1773 |     }
1774 | 
1775 |     restoreLookAhead(token);
1776 |     return reportGenericTypeSyntaxWarning();
1777 |   }

===== M0236 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getStringValue(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 177-222
Documentation:
/**
   * Gets the value of a node as a String, or null if it cannot be converted.
   * When it returns a non-null String, this method effectively emulates the
   * <code>String()</code> JavaScript cast function.
   */
Method code:
177 |   static String getStringValue(Node n) {
178 |     // TODO(user): regex literals as well.
179 |     switch (n.getType()) {
180 |       case Token.STRING:
181 |       case Token.STRING_KEY:
182 |         return n.getString();
183 | 
184 |       case Token.NAME:
185 |         String name = n.getString();
186 |         if ("undefined".equals(name)
187 |             || "Infinity".equals(name)
188 |             || "NaN".equals(name)) {
189 |           return name;
190 |         }
191 |         break;
192 | 
193 |       case Token.NUMBER:
194 |         return getStringValue(n.getDouble());
195 | 
196 |       case Token.FALSE:
197 |         return "false";
198 | 
199 |       case Token.TRUE:
200 |         return "true";
201 | 
202 |       case Token.NULL:
203 |         return "null";
204 | 
205 |       case Token.VOID:
206 |         return "undefined";
207 | 
208 |       case Token.NOT:
209 |         TernaryValue child = getPureBooleanValue(n.getFirstChild());
210 |         if (child != TernaryValue.UNKNOWN) {
211 |           return child.toBoolean(true) ? "false" : "true"; // reversed.
212 |         }
213 |         break;
214 | 
215 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
216 |         return arrayToString(n);
217 | 
218 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
219 |         return "[object Object]";
220 |     }
221 |     return null;
222 |   }
