===== M0241 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.FirstOrderFunctionAnalyzer.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1985-2036
Documentation:

Method code:
1985 |     @Override public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
1986 |       if (t.inGlobalScope()) {
1987 |         return;
1988 |       }
1989 | 
1990 |       if (n.isReturn() && n.getFirstChild() != null) {
1991 |         data.get(t.getScopeRoot()).recordNonEmptyReturn();
1992 |       }
1993 | 
1994 |       if (t.getScopeDepth() <= 1) {
1995 |         // The first-order function analyzer looks at two types of variables:
1996 |         //
1997 |         // 1) Local variables that are assigned in inner scopes ("escaped vars")
1998 |         //
1999 |         // 2) Local variables that are assigned more than once.
2000 |         //
2001 |         // We treat all global variables as escaped by default, so there's
2002 |         // no reason to do this extra computation for them.
2003 |         return;
2004 |       }
2005 | 
2006 |       if (n.isName() && NodeUtil.isLValue(n) &&
2007 |           // Be careful of bleeding functions, which create variables
2008 |           // in the inner scope, not the scope where the name appears.
2009 |           !NodeUtil.isBleedingFunctionName(n)) {
2010 |         String name = n.getString();
2011 |         Scope scope = t.getScope();
2012 |         Var var = scope.getVar(name);
2013 |         if (var != null) {
2014 |           Scope ownerScope = var.getScope();
2015 |           if (ownerScope.isLocal()) {
2016 |             data.get(ownerScope.getRootNode()).recordAssignedName(name);
2017 |           }
2018 | 
2019 |           if (scope != ownerScope && ownerScope.isLocal()) {
2020 |             data.get(ownerScope.getRootNode()).recordEscapedVarName(name);
2021 |           }
2022 |         }
2023 |       } else if (n.isGetProp() && n.isUnscopedQualifiedName() &&
2024 |           NodeUtil.isLValue(n)) {
2025 |         String name = NodeUtil.getRootOfQualifiedName(n).getString();
2026 |         Scope scope = t.getScope();
2027 |         Var var = scope.getVar(name);
2028 |         if (var != null) {
2029 |           Scope ownerScope = var.getScope();
2030 |           if (scope != ownerScope && ownerScope.isLocal()) {
2031 |             data.get(ownerScope.getRootNode())
2032 |                 .recordEscapedQualifiedName(n.getQualifiedName());
2033 |           }
2034 |         }
2035 |       }
2036 |     }

===== M0242 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseBranch(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 477-506
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a branch.
   */
Method code:
477 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
478 |   private void traverseBranch(Node n, Node parent) {
479 |     int type = n.getType();
480 |     if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
481 |       inputId = n.getInputId();
482 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
483 |     }
484 | 
485 |     curNode = n;
486 |     if (!callback.shouldTraverse(this, n, parent)) return;
487 | 
488 |     switch (type) {
489 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
490 |         traverseFunction(n, parent);
491 |         break;
492 | 
493 |       default:
494 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null; ) {
495 |           // child could be replaced, in which case our child node
496 |           // would no longer point to the true next
497 |           Node next = child.getNext();
498 |           traverseBranch(child, n);
499 |           child = next;
500 |         }
501 |         break;
502 |     }
503 | 
504 |     curNode = n;
505 |     callback.visit(this, n, parent);
506 |   }

===== M0245 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isLValue(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 2039-2051
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines whether this node is used as an L-value. Notice that sometimes
   * names are used as both L-values and R-values.
   *
   * We treat "var x;" as a pseudo-L-value, which kind of makes sense if you
   * treat it as "assignment to 'undefined' at the top of the scope". But if
   * we're honest with ourselves, it doesn't make sense, and we only do this
   * because it makes sense to treat this as syntactically similar to
   * "var x = 0;".
   *
   * @param n The node
   * @return True if n is an L-value.
   */
Method code:
2039 |   static boolean isLValue(Node n) {
2040 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(n.isName() || n.isGetProp() ||
2041 |         n.isGetElem());
2042 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
2043 |     return (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(parent) && parent.getFirstChild() == n)
2044 |         || (NodeUtil.isForIn(parent) && parent.getFirstChild() == n)
2045 |         || parent.isVar()
2046 |         || (parent.isFunction() && parent.getFirstChild() == n)
2047 |         || parent.isDec()
2048 |         || parent.isInc()
2049 |         || parent.isParamList()
2050 |         || parent.isCatch();
2051 |   }

/**

225 |                   stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());

819 | 
820 |                 case VERSION:
821 |                   ExtractionInfo versionInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
822 |                   String version = versionInfo.string;
823 | 
824 |                   if (version.length() == 0) {
825 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.versionmissing",
826 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
827 |                   } else {
828 |                     if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVersion(version)) {
829 |                        parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.extraversion",
830 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
831 |                     }
832 |                   }
833 | 
834 |                   token = versionInfo.token;
835 |                   continue retry;
836 | 
837 |                 case DEFINE:
838 |                 case RETURN:
839 |                 case THIS:
840 |                 case TYPE:
841 |                 case TYPEDEF:
842 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
843 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();
844 | 
845 |                   Node typeNode = null;
846 |                   if (!lookAheadForTypeAnnotation() &&
847 |                       annotation == Annotation.RETURN) {
848 |                     // If RETURN doesn't have a type annotation, record
849 |                     // it as the unknown type.
850 |                     typeNode = newNode(Token.QMARK);
851 |                   } else {
852 |                     skipEOLs();
853 |                     token = next();
854 |                     typeNode = parseAndRecordTypeNode(token);
855 |                   }
856 | 
857 |                   if (annotation == Annotation.THIS) {
858 |                     typeNode = wrapNode(Token.BANG, typeNode);
859 |                     if (typeNode != null && token != JsDocToken.LC) {
860 |                       typeNode.putBooleanProp(Node.BRACELESS_TYPE, true);
861 |                     }
862 |                   }
863 |                   type = createJSTypeExpression(typeNode);
864 | 
865 |                   if (type == null) {
866 |                     // error reported during recursive descent
867 |                     // recovering parsing
868 |                   } else {
869 |                     switch (annotation) {
870 |                       case DEFINE:
871 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordDefineType(type)) {
872 |                           parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.define",
873 |                               lineno, charno);
874 |                         }
875 |                         break;
876 | 
877 |                       case RETURN:
878 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordReturnType(type)) {
879 |                           parser.addTypeWarning(
880 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
881 |                           break;
882 |                         }
883 | 
884 |                         // Find the return's description (if applicable).
885 |                         if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
886 |                           ExtractionInfo returnDescriptionInfo =
887 |                               extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
888 | 
889 |                           String returnDescription =
890 |                               returnDescriptionInfo.string;
891 | 
892 |                           if (returnDescription.length() > 0) {
893 |                             jsdocBuilder.recordReturnDescription(
894 |                                 returnDescription);
895 |                           }
896 | 
897 |                           token = returnDescriptionInfo.token;
898 |                         } else {
899 |                           token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
900 |                         }
901 |                         continue retry;
902 | 
903 |                       case THIS:
904 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordThisType(type)) {
905 |                           parser.addTypeWarning(
906 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
907 |                         }
908 |                         break;
909 | 
910 |                       case TYPE:
911 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordType(type)) {
912 |                           parser.addTypeWarning(
913 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
914 |                         }
915 |                         break;
916 | 
917 |                       case TYPEDEF:
918 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordTypedef(type)) {
919 |                           parser.addTypeWarning(
920 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
921 |                         }
922 |                         break;
923 |                     }
924 |                   }
925 | 
926 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
927 |                   continue retry;
928 |               }
929 |             }
930 |           }
931 |           break;
932 | 
933 |         case EOC:
934 |           if (hasParsedFileOverviewDocInfo()) {
935 |             fileOverviewJSDocInfo = retrieveAndResetParsedJSDocInfo();
936 |           }
937 |           checkExtendedTypes(extendedTypes);
938 |           return true;
939 | 
940 |         case EOF:
941 |           // discard any accumulated information
942 |           jsdocBuilder.build(null);
943 |           parser.addParserWarning("msg.unexpected.eof",
944 |               stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
945 |           checkExtendedTypes(extendedTypes);
946 |           return false;
947 | 
948 |         case EOL:
949 |           if (state == State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE) {
950 |             state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
951 |           }
952 |           token = next();
953 |           continue retry;
954 | 
955 |         default:
956 |           if (token == JsDocToken.STAR && state == State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION) {
957 |             token = next();
958 |             continue retry;
959 |           } else {
960 |             state = State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE;
961 |             token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
962 |             continue retry;
963 |           }
964 |       }
965 | 
966 |       // next token
967 |       token = next();
968 |     }
969 |   }

===== M0270 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DataFlowAnalysis.analyze(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DataFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 201-229
Documentation:
/**
   * Finds a fixed-point solution. The function has the side effect of replacing
   * the existing node annotations with the computed solutions using {@link
   * com.google.javascript.jscomp.graph.GraphNode#setAnnotation(Annotation)}.
   *
   * <p>Initially, each node's input and output flow state contains the value
   * given by {@link #createInitialEstimateLattice()} (with the exception of the
   * entry node of the graph which takes on the {@link #createEntryLattice()}
   * value. Each node will use the output state of its predecessor and compute a
   * output state according to the instruction. At that time, any nodes that
   * depends on the node's newly modified output value will need to recompute
   * their output state again. Each step will perform a computation at one node
   * until no extra computation will modify any existing output state anymore.
   *
   * @param maxSteps Max number of iterations before the method stops and throw
   *        a {@link MaxIterationsExceededException}. This will prevent the
   *        analysis from going into a infinite loop.
   */
Method code:
201 |   final void analyze(int maxSteps) {
202 |     initialize();
203 |     int step = 0;
204 |     while (!orderedWorkSet.isEmpty()) {
205 |       if (step > maxSteps) {
206 |         throw new MaxIterationsExceededException(
207 |           "Analysis did not terminate after " + maxSteps + " iterations");
208 |       }
209 |       DiGraphNode<N, Branch> curNode = orderedWorkSet.iterator().next();
210 |       orderedWorkSet.remove(curNode);
211 |       joinInputs(curNode);
212 |       if (flow(curNode)) {
213 |         // If there is a change in the current node, we want to grab the list
214 |         // of nodes that this node affects.
215 |         List<DiGraphNode<N, Branch>> nextNodes = isForward() ?
216 |             cfg.getDirectedSuccNodes(curNode) :
217 |             cfg.getDirectedPredNodes(curNode);
218 |         for (DiGraphNode<N, Branch> nextNode : nextNodes) {
219 |           if (nextNode != cfg.getImplicitReturn()) {
220 |             orderedWorkSet.add(nextNode);
221 |           }
222 |         }
223 |       }
224 |       step++;
225 |     }
226 |     if (isForward()) {
227 |       joinInputs(getCfg().getImplicitReturn());
228 |     }
229 |   }

===== M0273 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DataFlowAnalysis.BranchedForwardDataFlowAnalysis.flow(DiGraphNode<N, Branch>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DataFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 443-455
Documentation:

Method code:
443 |     @Override
444 |     protected final boolean flow(DiGraphNode<N, Branch> node) {
445 |       BranchedFlowState<L> state = node.getAnnotation();
446 |       List<L> outBefore = state.out;
447 |       state.out = branchedFlowThrough(node.getValue(), state.in);
448 |       Preconditions.checkState(outBefore.size() == state.out.size());
449 |       for (int i = 0; i < outBefore.size(); i++) {
450 |         if (!outBefore.get(i).equals(state.out.get(i))) {
451 |           return true;
452 |         }
453 |       }
454 |       return false;
455 |     }

588 |   @Override
589 |   public JSType getPropertyType(String name) {
590 |     if (!hasOwnProperty(name)) {
591 |       // Define the "call", "apply", and "bind" functions lazily.
592 |       boolean isCall = "call".equals(name);
593 |       boolean isBind = "bind".equals(name);
594 |       if (isCall || isBind) {
595 |         defineDeclaredProperty(name, getCallOrBindSignature(isCall), source);

599 |

601 |         // or an arguments object. We don't model the arguments object,

604 |         builder.addOptionalParams(

607 |                 registry.getNativeType(JSTypeNative.OBJECT_TYPE)));
608 |

610 |             new FunctionBuilder(registry)
611 |             .withParams(builder)
612 |             .withReturnType(getReturnType())
613 |             .build(),
614 |             source);
615 |       }

/**
   * Reduce the alternates into a non-union type.
   * If the alternates can't be accurately represented with a non-union
   * type, return null.
   */
Method code:
221 |   private JSType reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion() {
222 |     if (isAllType) {
223 |       return registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
224 |     } else if (isNativeUnknownType) {
225 |       if (areAllUnknownsChecked) {
226 |         return registry.getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
227 |       } else {
228 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
229 |       }

232 |       if (size > maxUnionSize) {
233 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
234 |       } else if (size > 1) {
235 |         return null;
236 |       } else if (size == 1) {
237 |         return alternates.iterator().next();
238 |       } else {
239 |         return registry.getNativeType(NO_TYPE);
240 |       }
241 |     }

===== M0285 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.inferFromOverriddenFunction(FunctionType,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 252-310
Documentation:
/**
   * Infer the parameter and return types of a function from
   * the parameter and return types of the function it is overriding.
   *
   * @param oldType The function being overridden. Does nothing if this is null.
   * @param paramsParent The LP node of the function that we're assigning to.
   *     If null, that just means we're not initializing this to a function
   *     literal.
   */
Method code:
252 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferFromOverriddenFunction(
253 |       @Nullable FunctionType oldType, @Nullable Node paramsParent) {
254 |     if (oldType == null) {
255 |       return this;
256 |     }
257 | 
258 |     returnType = oldType.getReturnType();
259 |     returnTypeInferred = oldType.isReturnTypeInferred();
260 |     if (paramsParent == null) {
261 |       // Not a function literal.
262 |       parametersNode = oldType.getParametersNode();
263 |       if (parametersNode == null) {
264 |         parametersNode = new FunctionParamBuilder(typeRegistry).build();
265 |       }
266 |     } else {
267 |       // We're overriding with a function literal. Apply type information
268 |       // to each parameter of the literal.
269 |       FunctionParamBuilder paramBuilder =
270 |           new FunctionParamBuilder(typeRegistry);
271 |       Iterator<Node> oldParams = oldType.getParameters().iterator();
272 |       boolean warnedAboutArgList = false;
273 |       boolean oldParamsListHitOptArgs = false;
274 |       for (Node currentParam = paramsParent.getFirstChild();
275 |            currentParam != null; currentParam = currentParam.getNext()) {
276 |         if (oldParams.hasNext()) {
277 |           Node oldParam = oldParams.next();
278 |           Node newParam = paramBuilder.newParameterFromNode(oldParam);
279 | 
280 |           oldParamsListHitOptArgs = oldParamsListHitOptArgs ||
281 |               oldParam.isVarArgs() ||
282 |               oldParam.isOptionalArg();
283 | 
284 |           // The subclass method might write its var_args as individual
285 |           // arguments.
286 |           if (currentParam.getNext() != null && newParam.isVarArgs()) {
287 |             newParam.setVarArgs(false);
288 |             newParam.setOptionalArg(true);
289 |           }
290 |         } else {
291 |           warnedAboutArgList |= addParameter(
292 |               paramBuilder,
293 |               typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE),
294 |               warnedAboutArgList,
295 |               codingConvention.isOptionalParameter(currentParam) ||
296 |                   oldParamsListHitOptArgs,
297 |               codingConvention.isVarArgsParameter(currentParam));
298 |         }
299 |       }
300 | 
301 |       // Clone any remaining params that aren't in the function literal,
302 |       // but make them optional.
303 |       while (oldParams.hasNext()) {
304 |         paramBuilder.newOptionalParameterFromNode(oldParams.next());
305 |       }
306 | 
307 |       parametersNode = paramBuilder.build();
308 |     }
309 |     return this;
310 |   }

Original range: 94-109
Documentation:
/**
   * Add variable arguments to the end of the parameter list.
   * @return False if this is called after var args are added.
   */
Method code:
94 |   public boolean addVarArgs(JSType type) {
95 |     if (hasVarArgs()) {
96 |       return false;
97 |     }
98 | 
99 |     // There are two types of variable argument functions:
100 |     // 1) Programmer-defined var args
101 |     // 2) Native bottom types that can accept any argument.
102 |     // For the first one, "undefined" is a valid value for all arguments.
103 |     // For the second, we do not want to cast it up to undefined.
104 |     if (!type.isEmptyType()) {
105 |       type = registry.createOptionalType(type);
106 |     }
107 |     newParameter(type).setVarArgs(true);
108 |     return true;
109 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getBestJSDocInfo(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 3020-3046
Documentation:
/** Find the best JSDoc for the given node. */
Method code:
3020 |   static JSDocInfo getBestJSDocInfo(Node n) {
3021 |     JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
3022 |     if (info == null) {
3023 |       Node parent = n.getParent();
3024 |       if (parent == null) {
3025 |         return null;
3026 |       }
3027 | 
3028 |       if (parent.isName()) {
3029 |         return getBestJSDocInfo(parent);
3030 |       } else if (parent.isAssign()) {
3031 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3032 |       } else if (isObjectLitKey(parent, parent.getParent())) {
3033 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3034 |       } else if (parent.isFunction()) {
3035 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3036 |       } else if (parent.isVar() && parent.hasOneChild()) {
3037 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3038 |       } else if ((parent.isHook() && parent.getFirstChild() != n) ||
3039 |                  parent.isOr() ||
3040 |                  parent.isAnd() ||
3041 |                  (parent.isComma() && parent.getFirstChild() != n)) {
3042 |         return getBestJSDocInfo(parent);
3043 |       }
3044 |     }
3045 |     return info;
3046 |   }

/**
   * <p>Checks enum aliases.
   *
   * <p>We verify that the enum element type of the enum used
   * for initialization is a subtype of the enum element type of
   * the enum the value is being copied in.</p>
   *
   * <p>Example:</p>
   * <pre>var myEnum = myOtherEnum;</pre>
   *
   * <p>Enum aliases are irregular, so we need special code for this :(</p>
   *
   * @param value the value used for initialization of the enum

1813 |   private void checkEnumAlias(
1814 |       NodeTraversal t, JSDocInfo declInfo, Node value) {

1816 |       return;

1820 |     if (!valueType.isEnumType()) {
1821 |       return;
1822 |     }

1826 |         valueEnumType.getElementsType().getPrimitiveType();
1827 |     validator.expectCanAssignTo(t, value, valueEnumPrimitiveType,

===== M0308 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 830-857
Documentation:
/**
   * Connects cfgNode to the proper CATCH block if target subtree might throw
   * an exception. If there are FINALLY blocks reached before a CATCH, it will
   * make the corresponding entry in finallyMap.
   */
Method code:
830 |   private void connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(Node cfgNode, Node target) {
831 |     if (mayThrowException(target) && !exceptionHandler.isEmpty()) {
832 |       Node lastJump = cfgNode;
833 |       for (Node handler : exceptionHandler) {
834 |         if (handler.isFunction()) {
835 |           return;
836 |         }
837 |         Preconditions.checkState(handler.isTry());
838 |         Node catchBlock = NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(handler);
839 | 
840 |         if (!NodeUtil.hasCatchHandler(catchBlock)) { // No catch but a FINALLY.
841 |           if (lastJump == cfgNode) {
842 |             createEdge(cfgNode, Branch.ON_EX, handler.getLastChild());
843 |           } else {
844 |             finallyMap.put(lastJump, handler.getLastChild());
845 |           }
846 |         } else { // Has a catch.
847 |           if (lastJump == cfgNode) {
848 |             createEdge(cfgNode, Branch.ON_EX, catchBlock);
849 |             return;
850 |           } else {
851 |             finallyMap.put(lastJump, catchBlock);
852 |           }
853 |         }
854 |         lastJump = handler;
855 |       }
856 |     }
857 |   }

===== M0319 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.updateScopeForTypeChange(FlowScope,Node,JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 462-505
Documentation:
/**
   * Updates the scope according to the result of a type change, like
   * an assignment or a type cast.
   */
Method code:
462 |   private void updateScopeForTypeChange(
463 |       FlowScope scope, Node left, JSType leftType, JSType resultType) {
464 |     Preconditions.checkNotNull(resultType);
465 |     switch (left.getType()) {
466 |       case Token.NAME:
467 |         String varName = left.getString();
468 |         Var var = syntacticScope.getVar(varName);
469 | 
470 |         // When looking at VAR initializers for declared VARs, we trust
471 |         // the declared type over the type it's being initialized to.
472 |         // This has two purposes:
473 |         // 1) We avoid re-declaring declared variables so that built-in
474 |         //    types defined in externs are not redeclared.
475 |         // 2) When there's a lexical closure like
476 |         //    /** @type {?string} */ var x = null;
477 |         //    function f() { x = 'xyz'; }
478 |         //    the inference will ignore the lexical closure,
479 |         //    which is just wrong. This bug needs to be fixed eventually.
480 |         boolean isVarDeclaration = left.hasChildren();
481 |         if (!isVarDeclaration || var == null || var.isTypeInferred()) {
482 |           redeclareSimpleVar(scope, left, resultType);
483 |         }
484 |         left.setJSType(isVarDeclaration || leftType == null ?
485 |             resultType : null);
486 | 
487 |         if (var != null && var.isTypeInferred()) {
488 |           JSType oldType = var.getType();
489 |           var.setType(oldType == null ?
490 |               resultType : oldType.getLeastSupertype(resultType));
491 |         }
492 |         break;
493 |       case Token.GETPROP:
494 |         String qualifiedName = left.getQualifiedName();
495 |         if (qualifiedName != null) {
496 |           scope.inferQualifiedSlot(left, qualifiedName,
497 |               leftType == null ? getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE) : leftType,
498 |               resultType);
499 |         }
500 | 
501 |         left.setJSType(resultType);
502 |         ensurePropertyDefined(left, resultType);
503 |         break;
504 |     }
505 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.ParserRunner.parse(StaticSourceFile,String,Config,ErrorReporter,Logger)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/ParserRunner.java
Original range: 104-147
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the JavaScript text given by a reader.
   *
   * @param sourceString Source code from the file.
   * @param errorReporter An error.
   * @param logger A logger.
   * @return The AST of the given text.
   * @throws IOException
   */
Method code:
104 |   public static Node parse(StaticSourceFile sourceFile,
105 |                            String sourceString,
106 |                            Config config,
107 |                            ErrorReporter errorReporter,
108 |                            Logger logger) throws IOException {
109 |     Context cx = Context.enter();
110 |     cx.setErrorReporter(errorReporter);
111 |     cx.setLanguageVersion(Context.VERSION_1_5);
112 |     CompilerEnvirons compilerEnv = new CompilerEnvirons();
113 |     compilerEnv.initFromContext(cx);
114 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingComments(true);
115 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingLocalJsDocComments(true);
116 | 
117 |     // ES5 specifically allows trailing commas
118 |     compilerEnv.setWarnTrailingComma(
119 |         config.languageMode == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3);
120 | 
121 |     // Do our own identifier check for ECMASCRIPT 5
122 |     boolean acceptEs5 =
123 |         config.isIdeMode || config.languageMode != LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3;
124 |     compilerEnv.setReservedKeywordAsIdentifier(acceptEs5);
125 | 
126 |     compilerEnv.setAllowMemberExprAsFunctionName(false);
127 |     compilerEnv.setIdeMode(config.isIdeMode);
128 |     compilerEnv.setRecoverFromErrors(config.isIdeMode);
129 | 
130 |     Parser p = new Parser(compilerEnv, errorReporter);
131 |     AstRoot astRoot = null;
132 |     try {
133 |       astRoot = p.parse(sourceString, sourceFile.getName(), 1);
134 |     } catch (EvaluatorException e) {
135 |       logger.info(
136 |           "Error parsing " + sourceFile.getName() + ": " + e.getMessage());
137 |     } finally {
138 |       Context.exit();
139 |     }
140 |     Node root = null;
141 |     if (astRoot != null) {
142 |       root = IRFactory.transformTree(
143 |           astRoot, sourceFile, sourceString, config, errorReporter);
144 |       root.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
145 |     }
146 |     return root;
147 |   }

/**
   * Creates a JsDocInfoParser and parses the JsDoc string.
   *
   * Used both for handling individual JSDoc comments and for handling
   * file-level JSDoc comments (@fileoverview and @license).
   *
   * @param node The JsDoc Comment node to parse.
   * @param irNode
   * @return A JsDocInfoParser. Will contain either fileoverview JsDoc, or
   *     normal JsDoc, or no JsDoc (if the method parses to the wrong level).
   */
Method code:
347 |   private JsDocInfoParser createJsDocInfoParser(Comment node, Node irNode) {
348 |     String comment = node.getValue();
349 |     int lineno = node.getLineno();
350 |     int position = node.getAbsolutePosition();
351 | 
352 |     // The JsDocInfoParser expects the comment without the initial '/**'.
353 |     int numOpeningChars = 3;
354 |     JsDocInfoParser jsdocParser =
355 |       new JsDocInfoParser(
356 |           new JsDocTokenStream(comment.substring(numOpeningChars),
357 |                                lineno,
358 |                                position2charno(position) + numOpeningChars),
359 |           node,
360 |           irNode,
361 |           config,
362 |           errorReporter);
363 |     jsdocParser.setFileLevelJsDocBuilder(fileLevelJsDocBuilder);
364 |     jsdocParser.setFileOverviewJSDocInfo(fileOverviewInfo);
365 |     jsdocParser.parse();
366 |     return jsdocParser;
367 |   }

===== M0342 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanVars(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 124-178
Documentation:
/**
   * Scans and gather variables declarations under a Node
   */
Method code:
124 |   private void scanVars(Node n, Node parent) {
125 |     switch (n.getType()) {
126 |       case Token.VAR:
127 |         // Declare all variables. e.g. var x = 1, y, z;
128 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
129 |              child != null;) {
130 |           Node next = child.getNext();
131 |           declareVar(child);
132 |           child = next;
133 |         }
134 |         return;
135 | 
136 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
137 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
138 |           return;
139 |         }
140 | 
141 |         String fnName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
142 |         if (fnName.isEmpty()) {
143 |           // This is invalid, but allow it so the checks can catch it.
144 |           return;
145 |         }
146 |         declareVar(n.getFirstChild());
147 |         return;   // should not examine function's children
148 | 
149 |       case Token.CATCH:
150 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 2);
151 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getFirstChild().isName());
152 |         // the first child is the catch var and the third child
153 |         // is the code block
154 | 
155 |         final Node var = n.getFirstChild();
156 |         final Node block = var.getNext();
157 | 
158 |         declareVar(var);
159 |         scanVars(block, n);
160 |         return;  // only one child to scan
161 | 
162 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
163 |         inputId = n.getInputId();
164 |         Preconditions.checkNotNull(inputId);
165 |         break;
166 |     }
167 | 
168 |     // Variables can only occur in statement-level nodes, so
169 |     // we only need to traverse children in a couple special cases.
170 |     if (NodeUtil.isControlStructure(n) || NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(n)) {
171 |       for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
172 |            child != null;) {
173 |         Node next = child.getNext();
174 |         scanVars(child, n);
175 |         child = next;
176 |       }
177 |     }
178 |   }

===== M0350 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitName(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1264-1293
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits a NAME node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   * @param parent The parent of the node n.
   * @return whether the node is typeable or not
   */
Method code:
1264 |   boolean visitName(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
1265 |     // At this stage, we need to determine whether this is a leaf
1266 |     // node in an expression (which therefore needs to have a type
1267 |     // assigned for it) versus some other decorative node that we
1268 |     // can safely ignore.  Function names, arguments (children of LP nodes) and
1269 |     // variable declarations are ignored.
1270 |     // TODO(user): remove this short-circuiting in favor of a
1271 |     // pre order traversal of the FUNCTION, CATCH, LP and VAR nodes.
1272 |     int parentNodeType = parent.getType();
1273 |     if (parentNodeType == Token.FUNCTION ||
1274 |         parentNodeType == Token.CATCH ||
1275 |         parentNodeType == Token.PARAM_LIST ||
1276 |         parentNodeType == Token.VAR) {
1277 |       return false;
1278 |     }
1279 | 
1280 |     JSType type = n.getJSType();
1281 |     if (type == null) {
1282 |       type = getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1283 |       Var var = t.getScope().getVar(n.getString());
1284 |       if (var != null) {
1285 |         JSType varType = var.getType();

1287 |           type = varType;
1288 |         }
1289 |       }
1290 |     }
1291 |     ensureTyped(t, n, type);
1292 |     return true;
1293 |   }

===== M0359 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.transformAsString(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 409-422
Documentation:
/**
     * Transforms the given node and then sets its type to Token.STRING if it
     * was Token.NAME. If its type was already Token.STRING, then quotes it.
     * Used for properties, as the old AST uses String tokens, while the new one
     * uses Name tokens for unquoted strings. For example, in
     * var o = {'a' : 1, b: 2};
     * the string 'a' is quoted, while the name b is turned into a string, but
     * unquoted.
     */
Method code:
409 |     private Node transformAsString(AstNode n) {
410 |       Node ret;
411 |       if (n instanceof Name) {
412 |         ret = transformNameAsString((Name)n);
413 |       } else if (n instanceof NumberLiteral) {
414 |         ret = transformNumberAsString((NumberLiteral)n);
415 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
416 |       } else {
417 |         ret = transform(n);
418 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
419 |       }
420 |       Preconditions.checkState(ret.isString());
421 |       return ret;
422 |     }

===== M0374 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 422-447
Documentation:

Method code:
422 |   @Override
423 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(
424 |       NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
425 |     checkNoTypeCheckSection(n, true);
426 |     switch (n.getType()) {
427 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
428 |         // normal type checking
429 |         final Scope outerScope = t.getScope();
430 |         final String functionPrivateName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
431 |         if (functionPrivateName != null && functionPrivateName.length() > 0 &&
432 |             outerScope.isDeclared(functionPrivateName, false) &&
433 |             // Ideally, we would want to check whether the type in the scope
434 |             // differs from the type being defined, but then the extern
435 |             // redeclarations of built-in types generates spurious warnings.
436 |             !(outerScope.getVar(
437 |                 functionPrivateName).getType() instanceof FunctionType)) {
438 |           report(t, n, FUNCTION_MASKS_VARIABLE, functionPrivateName);
439 |         }
440 | 
441 |         // TODO(user): Only traverse the function's body. The function's
442 |         // name and arguments are traversed by the scope creator, and ideally
443 |         // should not be traversed by the type checker.
444 |         break;
445 |     }
446 |     return true;
447 |   }

/**
     * Declares all of a function's arguments.
     */
Method code:
1929 |     private void declareArguments(Node functionNode) {
1930 |       Node astParameters = functionNode.getFirstChild().getNext();
1931 |       Node body = astParameters.getNext();
1932 |       boolean isFnTypeInferred = functionNode.getBooleanProp(
1933 |           Node.INFERRED_FUNCTION);
1934 |       FunctionType functionType =
1935 |           JSType.toMaybeFunctionType(functionNode.getJSType());
1936 |       if (functionType != null) {
1937 |         Node jsDocParameters = functionType.getParametersNode();
1938 |         if (jsDocParameters != null) {
1939 |           Node jsDocParameter = jsDocParameters.getFirstChild();
1940 |           for (Node astParameter : astParameters.children()) {
1941 |             if (jsDocParameter != null) {
1942 |               defineSlot(astParameter, functionNode,
1943 |                   jsDocParameter.getJSType(), isFnTypeInferred);
1944 |               jsDocParameter = jsDocParameter.getNext();
1945 |             } else {
1946 |               defineSlot(astParameter, functionNode, null, true);
1947 |             }
1948 |           }
1949 |         }
1950 |       }
1951 |     } // end declareArguments

Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 84-119
Documentation:

Method code:
84 |   private void scanRoot(Node n, Scope parent) {
85 |     if (n.isFunction()) {
86 |       if (inputId == null) {
87 |         inputId = NodeUtil.getInputId(n);
88 |         // TODO(johnlenz): inputId maybe null if the FUNCTION node is detached
89 |         // from the AST.
90 |         // Is it meaningful to build a scope for detached FUNCTION node?
91 |       }
92 | 
93 |       final Node fnNameNode = n.getFirstChild();
94 |       final Node args = fnNameNode.getNext();
95 |       final Node body = args.getNext();
96 | 
97 |       // Bleed the function name into the scope, if it hasn't
98 |       // been declared in the outer scope.
99 |       String fnName = fnNameNode.getString();
100 |       if (!fnName.isEmpty() && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
101 |         declareVar(fnNameNode);
102 |       }
103 | 
104 |       // Args: Declare function variables
105 |       Preconditions.checkState(args.isParamList());
106 |       for (Node a = args.getFirstChild(); a != null;
107 |            a = a.getNext()) {
108 |         Preconditions.checkState(a.isName());
109 |         declareVar(a);
110 |       }
111 | 
112 |       // Body
113 |       scanVars(body, n);
114 |     } else {
115 |       // It's the global block
116 |       Preconditions.checkState(scope.getParent() == null);
117 |       scanVars(n, null);
118 |     }
119 |   }

/**
   * Constructs a data flow analysis.
   *
   * <p>Typical usage
   * <pre>
   * DataFlowAnalysis dfa = ...
   * dfa.analyze();
   * </pre>
   *
   * {@link #analyze()} annotates the result to the control flow graph by
   * means of {@link DiGraphNode#setAnnotation} without any
   * modification of the graph itself. Additional calls to {@link #analyze()}
   * recomputes the analysis which can be useful if the control flow graph
   * has been modified.
   *
   * @param targetCfg The control flow graph object that this object performs
   *     on. Modification of the graph requires a separate call to
   *     {@link #analyze()}.
   *
   * @see #analyze()
   */
Method code:
120 |   DataFlowAnalysis(ControlFlowGraph<N> targetCfg, JoinOp<L> joinOp) {
121 |     this.cfg = targetCfg;
122 |     this.joinOp = joinOp;
123 |     Comparator<DiGraphNode<N, Branch>> nodeComparator =
124 |       cfg.getOptionalNodeComparator(isForward());
125 |     if (nodeComparator != null) {
126 |       this.orderedWorkSet = Sets.newTreeSet(nodeComparator);
127 |     } else {
128 |       this.orderedWorkSet = Sets.newLinkedHashSet();
129 |     }
130 |   }

===== M0431 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeValidator.expectUndeclaredVariable(String,CompilerInput,Node,Node,Var,String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeValidator.java
Original range: 531-591
Documentation:
/**
   * Expect that the given variable has not been declared with a type.
   *
   * @param sourceName The name of the source file we're in.
   * @param n The node where warnings should point to.
   * @param parent The parent of {@code n}.
   * @param var The variable that we're checking.
   * @param variableName The name of the variable.
   * @param newType The type being applied to the variable. Mostly just here
   *     for the benefit of the warning.
   * @return The variable we end up with. Most of the time, this will just
   *     be {@code var}, but in some rare cases we will need to declare
   *     a new var with new source info.
   */
Method code:
531 |   Var expectUndeclaredVariable(String sourceName, CompilerInput input,
532 |       Node n, Node parent, Var var, String variableName, JSType newType) {
533 |     Var newVar = var;
534 |     boolean allowDupe = false;
535 |     if (n.isGetProp() ||
536 |         NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(n, parent)) {
537 |       JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
538 |       if (info == null) {
539 |         info = parent.getJSDocInfo();
540 |       }
541 |       allowDupe =
542 |           info != null && info.getSuppressions().contains("duplicate");
543 |     }
544 | 
545 |     JSType varType = var.getType();
546 | 
547 |     // Only report duplicate declarations that have types. Other duplicates
548 |     // will be reported by the syntactic scope creator later in the
549 |     // compilation process.
550 |     if (varType != null &&
551 |         varType != typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE) &&
552 |         newType != null &&
553 |         newType != typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE)) {
554 |       // If there are two typed declarations of the same variable, that
555 |       // is an error and the second declaration is ignored, except in the
556 |       // case of native types. A null input type means that the declaration
557 |       // was made in TypedScopeCreator#createInitialScope and is a
558 |       // native type. We should redeclare it at the new input site.
559 |       if (var.input == null) {
560 |         Scope s = var.getScope();
561 |         s.undeclare(var);
562 |         newVar = s.declare(variableName, n, varType, input, false);
563 | 
564 |         n.setJSType(varType);
565 |         if (parent.isVar()) {
566 |           if (n.getFirstChild() != null) {
567 |             n.getFirstChild().setJSType(varType);
568 |           }
569 |         } else {
570 |           Preconditions.checkState(parent.isFunction());
571 |           parent.setJSType(varType);
572 |         }
573 |       } else {
574 |         // Always warn about duplicates if the overridden type does not
575 |         // match the original type.
576 |         //
577 |         // If the types match, suppress the warning iff there was a @suppress
578 |         // tag, or if the original declaration was a stub.
579 |         if (!(allowDupe ||
580 |               var.getParentNode().isExprResult()) ||
581 |             !newType.isEquivalentTo(varType)) {
582 |           report(JSError.make(sourceName, n, DUP_VAR_DECLARATION,
583 |               variableName, newType.toString(), var.getInputName(),
584 |               String.valueOf(var.nameNode.getLineno()),
585 |               varType.toString()));
586 |         }
587 |       }
588 |     }
589 | 
590 |     return newVar;
591 |   }

===== M0432 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isStatementParent(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1714-1727
Documentation:

Method code:
1714 |   static boolean isStatementParent(Node parent) {
1715 |     // It is not possible to determine definitely if a node is a statement
1716 |     // or not if it is not part of the AST.  A FUNCTION node can be
1717 |     // either part of an expression or a statement.
1718 |     Preconditions.checkState(parent != null);
1719 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
1720 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
1721 |       case Token.BLOCK:
1722 |       case Token.LABEL:
1723 |         return true;
1724 |       default:
1725 |         return false;
1726 |     }
1727 |   }

===== M0440 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.addAlternate(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 121-214
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds an alternate to the union type under construction. Returns this
   * for easy chaining.
   */
Method code:
121 |   UnionTypeBuilder addAlternate(JSType alternate) {
122 |     // build() returns the bottom type by default, so we can
123 |     // just bail out early here.
124 |     if (alternate.isNoType()) {
125 |       return this;
126 |     }
127 | 
128 |     isAllType = isAllType || alternate.isAllType();
129 | 
130 |     boolean isAlternateUnknown = alternate instanceof UnknownType;
131 |     isNativeUnknownType = isNativeUnknownType || isAlternateUnknown;
132 |     if (isAlternateUnknown) {
133 |       areAllUnknownsChecked = areAllUnknownsChecked &&
134 |           alternate.isCheckedUnknownType();
135 |     }
136 |     if (!isAllType && !isNativeUnknownType) {
137 |       if (alternate.isUnionType()) {
138 |         UnionType union = alternate.toMaybeUnionType();
139 |         for (JSType unionAlt : union.getAlternates()) {
140 |           addAlternate(unionAlt);
141 |         }
142 |       } else {
143 |         if (alternates.size() > maxUnionSize) {
144 |           return this;
145 |         }
146 | 
147 |         // Function types are special, because they have their
148 |         // own bizarre sub-lattice. See the comments on
149 |         // FunctionType#supAndInf helper and above at functionTypePosition.
150 |         if (alternate.isFunctionType() && functionTypePosition != -1) {
151 |           // See the comments on functionTypePosition above.
152 |           FunctionType other =
153 |               alternates.get(functionTypePosition).toMaybeFunctionType();
154 |           FunctionType supremum =
155 |               alternate.toMaybeFunctionType().supAndInfHelper(other, true);
156 |           alternates.set(functionTypePosition, supremum);
157 |           result = null;
158 |           return this;
159 |         }
160 | 
161 |         // Look through the alternates we've got so far,
162 |         // and check if any of them are duplicates of
163 |         // one another.
164 |         int currentIndex = 0;
165 |         Iterator<JSType> it = alternates.iterator();
166 |         while (it.hasNext()) {
167 |           JSType current = it.next();
168 | 
169 |           // Unknown and NoResolved types may just be names that haven't
170 |           // been resolved yet. So keep these in the union, and just use
171 |           // equality checking for simple de-duping.
172 |           if (alternate.isUnknownType() ||
173 |               current.isUnknownType() ||
174 |               alternate.isNoResolvedType() ||
175 |               current.isNoResolvedType() ||
176 |               alternate.hasAnyTemplate() ||
177 |               current.hasAnyTemplate()) {
178 |             if (alternate.isEquivalentTo(current)) {
179 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
180 |               return this;
181 |             }
182 |           } else {
183 |             if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
184 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
185 |               return this;
186 |             } else if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
187 |               // Alternate makes current obsolete
188 |               it.remove();
189 | 
190 |               if (currentIndex == functionTypePosition) {
191 |                 functionTypePosition = -1;
192 |               } else if (currentIndex < functionTypePosition) {
193 |                 functionTypePosition--;
194 |                 currentIndex--;
195 |               }
196 |             }
197 |           }
198 |           currentIndex++;
199 |         }
200 | 
201 |         if (alternate.isFunctionType()) {
202 |           // See the comments on functionTypePosition above.
203 |           Preconditions.checkState(functionTypePosition == -1);
204 |           functionTypePosition = alternates.size();
205 |         }
206 | 
207 |         alternates.add(alternate);
208 |         result = null; // invalidate the memoized result
209 |       }
210 |     } else {
211 |       result = null;
212 |     }
213 |     return this;
214 |   }

===== M0445 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.computeFollowNode(Node,Node,ControlFlowAnalysis)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 692-789
Documentation:
/**
   * Computes the follow() node of a given node and its parent. There is a side
   * effect when calling this function. If this function computed an edge that
   * exists a FINALLY, it'll attempt to connect the fromNode to the outer
   * FINALLY according to the finallyMap.
   *
   * @param fromNode The original source node since {@code node} is changed
   *        during recursion.
   * @param node The node that follow() should compute.
   */
Method code:
692 |   private static Node computeFollowNode(
693 |       Node fromNode, Node node, ControlFlowAnalysis cfa) {
694 |     /*
695 |      * This is the case where:
696 |      *
697 |      * 1. Parent is null implies that we are transferring control to the end of
698 |      * the script.
699 |      *
700 |      * 2. Parent is a function implies that we are transferring control back to
701 |      * the caller of the function.
702 |      *
703 |      * 3. If the node is a return statement, we should also transfer control
704 |      * back to the caller of the function.
705 |      *
706 |      * 4. If the node is root then we have reached the end of what we have been
707 |      * asked to traverse.
708 |      *
709 |      * In all cases we should transfer control to a "symbolic return" node.
710 |      * This will make life easier for DFAs.
711 |      */
712 |     Node parent = node.getParent();
713 |     if (parent == null || parent.isFunction() ||
714 |         (cfa != null && node == cfa.root)) {
715 |       return null;
716 |     }
717 | 
718 |     // If we are just before a IF/WHILE/DO/FOR:
719 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
720 |       // The follow() of any of the path from IF would be what follows IF.
721 |       case Token.IF:
722 |         return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
723 |       case Token.CASE:
724 |       case Token.DEFAULT_CASE:
725 |         // After the body of a CASE, the control goes to the body of the next
726 |         // case, without having to go to the case condition.
727 |         if (parent.getNext() != null) {
728 |           if (parent.getNext().isCase()) {
729 |             return parent.getNext().getFirstChild().getNext();
730 |           } else if (parent.getNext().isDefaultCase()) {
731 |             return parent.getNext().getFirstChild();
732 |           } else {
733 |             Preconditions.checkState(false, "Not reachable");
734 |           }
735 |         } else {
736 |           return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
737 |         }
738 |         break;
739 |       case Token.FOR:
740 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(parent)) {
741 |           return parent;
742 |         } else {
743 |           return parent.getFirstChild().getNext().getNext();
744 |         }
745 |       case Token.WHILE:
746 |       case Token.DO:
747 |         return parent;
748 |       case Token.TRY:
749 |         // If we are coming out of the TRY block...
750 |         if (parent.getFirstChild() == node) {
751 |           if (NodeUtil.hasFinally(parent)) { // and have FINALLY block.
752 |             return computeFallThrough(parent.getLastChild());
753 |           } else { // and have no FINALLY.
754 |             return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
755 |           }
756 |         // CATCH block.
757 |         } else if (NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(parent) == node){
758 |           if (NodeUtil.hasFinally(parent)) { // and have FINALLY block.
759 |             return computeFallThrough(node.getNext());
760 |           } else {
761 |             return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
762 |           }
763 |         // If we are coming out of the FINALLY block...
764 |         } else if (parent.getLastChild() == node){
765 |           if (cfa != null) {
766 |             for (Node finallyNode : cfa.finallyMap.get(parent)) {
767 |               cfa.createEdge(fromNode, Branch.ON_EX, finallyNode);
768 |             }
769 |           }
770 |           return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
771 |         }
772 |     }
773 | 
774 |     // Now that we are done with the special cases follow should be its
775 |     // immediate sibling, unless its sibling is a function
776 |     Node nextSibling = node.getNext();
777 | 
778 |     // Skip function declarations because control doesn't get pass into it.
779 |     while (nextSibling != null && nextSibling.isFunction()) {
780 |       nextSibling = nextSibling.getNext();
781 |     }
782 | 
783 |     if (nextSibling != null) {
784 |       return computeFallThrough(nextSibling);
785 |     } else {
786 |       // If there are no more siblings, control is transferred up the AST.
787 |       return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
788 |     }
789 |   }

===== M0449 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.handleStmtList(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 484-523
Documentation:

Method code:
484 |   private void handleStmtList(Node node) {
485 |     Node parent = node.getParent();
486 |     // Special case, don't add a block of empty CATCH block to the graph.
487 |     if (node.isBlock() && parent != null &&
488 |         parent.isTry() &&
489 |         NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(parent) == node &&
490 |         !NodeUtil.hasCatchHandler(node)) {
491 |       return;
492 |     }
493 | 
494 |     // A block transfer control to its first child if it is not empty.
495 |     Node child = node.getFirstChild();
496 | 
497 |     // Function declarations are skipped since control doesn't go into that
498 |     // function (unless it is called)
499 |     while (child != null && child.isFunction()) {
500 |       child = child.getNext();
501 |     }
502 | 
503 |     if (child != null) {
504 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, computeFallThrough(child));
505 |     } else {
506 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, computeFollowNode(node, this));
507 |     }
508 | 
509 |     // Synthetic blocks
510 |     if (parent != null) {
511 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
512 |         case Token.DEFAULT_CASE:
513 |         case Token.CASE:
514 |         case Token.TRY:
515 |           break;
516 |         default:
517 |           if (node.isBlock() && node.isSyntheticBlock()) {
518 |             createEdge(node, Branch.SYN_BLOCK, computeFollowNode(node, this));
519 |           }
520 |           break;
521 |       }
522 |     }
523 |   }

===== M0460 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.addParameter(FunctionParamBuilder,JSType,boolean,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 558-588
Documentation:
/**
   * Add a parameter to the param list.
   * @param builder A builder.
   * @param paramType The parameter type.
   * @param warnedAboutArgList Whether we've already warned about arg ordering
   *     issues (like if optional args appeared before required ones).
   * @param isOptional Is this an optional parameter?
   * @param isVarArgs Is this a var args parameter?
   * @return Whether a warning was emitted.
   */
Method code:
558 |   private boolean addParameter(FunctionParamBuilder builder,
559 |       JSType paramType, boolean warnedAboutArgList,
560 |       boolean isOptional, boolean isVarArgs) {
561 |     boolean emittedWarning = false;
562 |     if (isOptional) {
563 |       // Remembering that an optional parameter has been encountered
564 |       // so that if a non optional param is encountered later, an
565 |       // error can be reported.
566 |       if (!builder.addOptionalParams(paramType) && !warnedAboutArgList) {
567 |         reportWarning(VAR_ARGS_MUST_BE_LAST);
568 |         emittedWarning = true;
569 |       }
570 |     } else if (isVarArgs) {
571 |       if (!builder.addVarArgs(paramType) && !warnedAboutArgList) {
572 |         reportWarning(VAR_ARGS_MUST_BE_LAST);
573 |         emittedWarning = true;
574 |       }
575 |     } else {
576 |       if (!builder.addRequiredParams(paramType) && !warnedAboutArgList) {
577 |         // An optional parameter was seen and this argument is not an optional
578 |         // or var arg so it is an error.
579 |         if (builder.hasVarArgs()) {
580 |           reportWarning(VAR_ARGS_MUST_BE_LAST);
581 |         } else {
582 |           reportWarning(OPTIONAL_ARG_AT_END);
583 |         }
584 |         emittedWarning = true;
585 |       }
586 |     }
587 |     return emittedWarning;
588 |   }

===== M0487 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseTypeExpression(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1704-1745
Documentation:
/**
   * TypeExpression := BasicTypeExpression
   *     | '?' BasicTypeExpression
   *     | '!' BasicTypeExpression
   *     | BasicTypeExpression '?'
   *     | BasicTypeExpression '!'
   *     | '?'
   */
Method code:
1704 |   private Node parseTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1705 |     if (token == JsDocToken.QMARK) {
1706 |       // A QMARK could mean that a type is nullable, or that it's unknown.
1707 |       // We use look-ahead 1 to determine whether it's unknown. Otherwise,
1708 |       // we assume it means nullable. There are 5 cases:
1709 |       // {?} - right curly
1710 |       // {?=} - equals
1711 |       // {function(?, number)} - comma
1712 |       // {function(number, ?)} - right paren
1713 |       // {function(number, ...[?])} - right bracket
1714 |       // {function(): ?|number} - pipe
1715 |       // I'm not a big fan of using look-ahead for this, but it makes
1716 |       // the type language a lot nicer.
1717 |       token = next();
1718 |       if (token == JsDocToken.COMMA ||
1719 |           token == JsDocToken.EQUALS ||
1720 |           token == JsDocToken.RB ||
1721 |           token == JsDocToken.RC ||
1722 |           token == JsDocToken.RP ||
1723 |           token == JsDocToken.PIPE) {
1724 |         restoreLookAhead(token);
1725 |         return newNode(Token.QMARK);
1726 |       }
1727 | 
1728 |       return wrapNode(Token.QMARK, parseBasicTypeExpression(token));
1729 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.BANG) {
1730 |       return wrapNode(Token.BANG, parseBasicTypeExpression(next()));
1731 |     } else {
1732 |       Node basicTypeExpr = parseBasicTypeExpression(token);
1733 |       if (basicTypeExpr != null) {
1734 |         if (match(JsDocToken.QMARK)) {
1735 |           next();
1736 |           return wrapNode(Token.QMARK, basicTypeExpr);
1737 |         } else if (match(JsDocToken.BANG)) {
1738 |           next();
1739 |           return wrapNode(Token.BANG, basicTypeExpr);
1740 |         }
1741 |       }
1742 | 
1743 |       return basicTypeExpr;
1744 |     }
1745 |   }

===== M0491 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.mergeLineCharNo(int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1198-1206
Documentation:
/**
   * Merges the line number and character number in one integer. The Character
   * number takes the first 12 bits and the line number takes the rest. If
   * the character number is greater than <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code> it is
   * adjusted to <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code>.
   */
Method code:
1198 |   protected static int mergeLineCharNo(int lineno, int charno) {
1199 |     if (lineno < 0 || charno < 0) {
1200 |       return -1;
1201 |     } else if ((charno & ~COLUMN_MASK) != 0) {
1202 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | COLUMN_MASK;
1203 |     } else {
1204 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | (charno & COLUMN_MASK);
1205 |     }
1206 |   }

===== M0498 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitVar(NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1425-1454
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits a VAR node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   */
Method code:
1425 |   private void visitVar(NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
1426 |     // TODO(nicksantos): Fix this so that the doc info always shows up
1427 |     // on the NAME node. We probably want to wait for the parser
1428 |     // merge to fix this.
1429 |     JSDocInfo varInfo = n.hasOneChild() ? n.getJSDocInfo() : null;
1430 |     for (Node name : n.children()) {
1431 |       Node value = name.getFirstChild();
1432 |       // A null var would indicate a bug in the scope creation logic.
1433 |       Var var = t.getScope().getVar(name.getString());
1434 | 
1435 |       if (value != null) {
1436 |         JSType valueType = getJSType(value);
1437 |         JSType nameType = var.getType();
1438 |         nameType = (nameType == null) ? getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE) : nameType;
1439 | 
1440 |         JSDocInfo info = name.getJSDocInfo();
1441 |         if (info == null) {
1442 |           info = varInfo;
1443 |         }
1444 | 
1445 |         checkEnumAlias(t, info, value);
1446 |         if (var.isTypeInferred()) {
1447 |           ensureTyped(t, name, valueType);
1448 |         } else {
1449 |           validator.expectCanAssignTo(
1450 |               t, value, valueType, nameType, "initializing variable");
1451 |         }
1452 |       }
1453 |     }
1454 |   }

===== M0509 =====

111 |   @Override

115 |     if (functionScope.isLocal()) {
116 |       if (functionScope.getVar(symbol) == null && !functionScope.isBottom()) {
117 |         functionScope.declare(symbol, node, bottomType, null);
118 |       }
119 | 
120 |       inferSlotType(symbol, inferredType);
121 |     }
122 |   }

===== M0511 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowGraph.isEnteringNewCfgNode(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowGraph.java
Original range: 159-205
Documentation:
/**
   * @return True if n should be represented by a new CFG node in the control
   * flow graph.
   */
Method code:
159 |   public static boolean isEnteringNewCfgNode(Node n) {
160 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
161 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
162 |       case Token.BLOCK:
163 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
164 |       case Token.TRY:
165 |         return true;
166 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
167 |         // A function node represents the start of a function where the name
168 |         // is bleed into the local scope and parameters has been assigned
169 |         // to the formal argument names. The node includes the name of the
170 |         // function and the LP list since we assume the whole set up process
171 |         // is atomic without change in control flow. The next change of
172 |         // control is going into the function's body represent by the second
173 |         // child.
174 |         return n != parent.getFirstChild().getNext();
175 |       case Token.WHILE:
176 |       case Token.DO:
177 |       case Token.IF:
178 |         // Theses control structure is represented by its node that holds the
179 |         // condition. Each of them is a branch node based on its condition.
180 |         return NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(parent) != n;
181 | 
182 |       case Token.FOR:
183 |         // The FOR(;;) node differs from other control structure in that
184 |         // it has a initialization and a increment statement. Those
185 |         // two statements have its corresponding CFG nodes to represent them.
186 |         // The FOR node represents the condition check for each iteration.
187 |         // That way the following:
188 |         // for(var x = 0; x < 10; x++) { } has a graph that is isomorphic to
189 |         // var x = 0; while(x<10) {  x++; }
190 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(parent)) {
191 |           // TODO(user): Investigate how we should handle the case where
192 |           // we have a very complex expression inside the FOR-IN header.
193 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild();
194 |         } else {
195 |           return NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(parent) != n;
196 |         }
197 |       case Token.SWITCH:
198 |       case Token.CASE:
199 |       case Token.CATCH:
200 |       case Token.WITH:
201 |         return n != parent.getFirstChild();
202 |       default:
203 |         return false;
204 |     }
205 |   }

===== M0518 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.traverseName(Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 597-650
Documentation:

Method code:
597 |   private FlowScope traverseName(Node n, FlowScope scope) {
598 |     String varName = n.getString();
599 |     Node value = n.getFirstChild();
600 |     JSType type = n.getJSType();
601 |     if (value != null) {
602 |       scope = traverse(value, scope);
603 |       updateScopeForTypeChange(scope, n, n.getJSType() /* could be null */,
604 |           getJSType(value));
605 |       return scope;
606 |     } else {
607 |       StaticSlot<JSType> var = scope.getSlot(varName);
608 |       if (var != null) {
609 |         // There are two situations where we don't want to use type information
610 |         // from the scope, even if we have it.
611 | 
612 |         // 1) The var is escaped and assigned in an inner scope, e.g.,
613 |         // function f() { var x = 3; function g() { x = null } (x); }
614 |         boolean isInferred = var.isTypeInferred();
615 |         boolean unflowable = isInferred &&
616 |             isUnflowable(syntacticScope.getVar(varName));
617 | 
618 |         // 2) We're reading type information from another scope for an
619 |         // inferred variable. That variable is assigned more than once,
620 |         // and we can't know which type we're getting.
621 |         //
622 |         // var t = null; function f() { (t); } doStuff(); t = {};
623 |         //
624 |         // Notice that this heuristic isn't perfect. For example, you might
625 |         // have:
626 |         //
627 |         // function f() { (t); } f(); var t = 3;
628 |         //
629 |         // In this case, we would infer the first reference to t as
630 |         // type {number}, even though it's undefined.
631 |         boolean nonLocalInferredSlot = false;
632 |         if (isInferred && syntacticScope.isLocal()) {
633 |           Var maybeOuterVar = syntacticScope.getParent().getVar(varName);
634 |           if (var == maybeOuterVar &&
635 |               !maybeOuterVar.isMarkedAssignedExactlyOnce()) {
636 |             nonLocalInferredSlot = true;
637 |           }
638 |         }
639 | 
640 |         if (!unflowable && !nonLocalInferredSlot) {
641 |           type = var.getType();
642 |           if (type == null) {
643 |             type = getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
644 |           }
645 |         }
646 |       }
647 |     }
648 |     n.setJSType(type);
649 |     return scope;
650 |   }

===== M0519 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processFunctionNode(FunctionNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 626-708
Documentation:

Method code:
626 |     @Override
627 |     Node processFunctionNode(FunctionNode functionNode) {
628 |       Name name = functionNode.getFunctionName();
629 |       Boolean isUnnamedFunction = false;
630 |       if (name == null) {
631 |         int functionType = functionNode.getFunctionType();
632 |         if (functionType != FunctionNode.FUNCTION_EXPRESSION) {
633 |           errorReporter.error(
634 |             "unnamed function statement",
635 |             sourceName,
636 |             functionNode.getLineno(), "", 0);
637 | 
638 |           // Return the bare minimum to put the AST in a valid state.
639 |           return newNode(Token.EXPR_RESULT, Node.newNumber(0));
640 |         }
641 |         name = new Name();
642 |         name.setIdentifier("");
643 |         isUnnamedFunction = true;
644 |       }
645 |       Node node = newNode(Token.FUNCTION);
646 |       Node newName = transform(name);
647 |       if (isUnnamedFunction) {
648 |         // Old Rhino tagged the empty name node with the line number of the
649 |         // declaration.
650 |         newName.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
651 |         // TODO(bowdidge) Mark line number of paren correctly.
652 |         // Same problem as below - the left paren might not be on the
653 |         // same line as the function keyword.
654 |         int lpColumn = functionNode.getAbsolutePosition() +
655 |             functionNode.getLp();
656 |         newName.setCharno(position2charno(lpColumn));
657 |         maybeSetLengthFrom(newName, name);
658 |       }
659 | 
660 |       node.addChildToBack(newName);
661 |       Node lp = newNode(Token.PARAM_LIST);
662 |       // The left paren's complicated because it's not represented by an
663 |       // AstNode, so there's nothing that has the actual line number that it
664 |       // appeared on.  We know the paren has to appear on the same line as the
665 |       // function name (or else a semicolon will be inserted.)  If there's no
666 |       // function name, assume the paren was on the same line as the function.
667 |       // TODO(bowdidge): Mark line number of paren correctly.
668 |       Name fnName = functionNode.getFunctionName();
669 |       if (fnName != null) {
670 |         lp.setLineno(fnName.getLineno());
671 |       } else {
672 |         lp.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
673 |       }
674 |       int lparenCharno = functionNode.getLp() +
675 |           functionNode.getAbsolutePosition();
676 | 
677 |       lp.setCharno(position2charno(lparenCharno));
678 |       for (AstNode param : functionNode.getParams()) {
679 |         Node paramNode = transform(param);
680 |         // When in ideMode Rhino can generate a param list with only a single
681 |         // ErrorNode. This is transformed into an EMPTY node. Drop this node in
682 |         // ideMode to keep the AST in a valid state.
683 |         if (paramNode.isName()) {
684 |           lp.addChildToBack(paramNode);
685 |         } else {
686 |           // We expect this in ideMode or when there is an error handling
687 |           // destructuring parameter assignments which aren't supported
688 |           // (an error has already been reported).
689 |           Preconditions.checkState(
690 |               config.isIdeMode
691 |               || paramNode.isObjectLit()
692 |               || paramNode.isArrayLit());
693 |         }
694 |       }
695 |       node.addChildToBack(lp);
696 | 
697 |       Node bodyNode = transform(functionNode.getBody());
698 |       if (!bodyNode.isBlock()) {
699 |         // When in ideMode Rhino tries to parse some constructs the compiler
700 |         // doesn't support, repair it here. see Rhino's
701 |         // Parser#parseFunctionBodyExpr.
702 |         Preconditions.checkState(config.isIdeMode);
703 |         bodyNode = IR.block();
704 |       }
705 |       parseDirectives(bodyNode);
706 |       node.addChildToBack(bodyNode);
707 |      return node;
708 |     }

/**
   * Determines if the subtree might throw an exception.

910 |   public static boolean mayThrowException(Node n) {
911 |     switch (n.getType()) {
912 |       case Token.CALL:
913 |       case Token.GETPROP:
914 |       case Token.GETELEM:
915 |       case Token.THROW:
916 |       case Token.NEW:

927 |         return true;
928 |       }

===== M0543 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.setPrototypeNoCheck(ObjectType,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 448-483
Documentation:
/** Set the prototype without doing any sanity checks. */
Method code:
448 |   private boolean setPrototypeNoCheck(ObjectType prototype, Node propertyNode) {
449 |     ObjectType oldPrototype = prototypeSlot == null
450 |         ? null : (ObjectType) prototypeSlot.getType();
451 |     boolean replacedPrototype = oldPrototype != null;
452 | 
453 |     this.prototypeSlot = new Property("prototype", prototype, true,
454 |         propertyNode == null ? source : propertyNode);
455 |     prototype.setOwnerFunction(this);
456 | 
457 |     if (oldPrototype != null) {
458 |       // Disassociating the old prototype makes this easier to debug--
459 |       // we don't have to worry about two prototypes running around.
460 |       oldPrototype.setOwnerFunction(null);
461 |     }
462 | 
463 |     if (isConstructor() || isInterface()) {
464 |       FunctionType superClass = getSuperClassConstructor();
465 |       if (superClass != null) {
466 |         superClass.addSubType(this);
467 |       }
468 | 
469 |       if (isInterface()) {
470 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : getExtendedInterfaces()) {
471 |           if (interfaceType.getConstructor() != null) {
472 |             interfaceType.getConstructor().addSubType(this);
473 |           }
474 |         }
475 |       }
476 |     }
477 | 
478 |     if (replacedPrototype) {
479 |       clearCachedValues();
480 |     }
481 | 
482 |     return true;
483 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.getLeastSupertype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 771-777
Documentation:
/**
   * Gets the least supertype of {@code this} and {@code that}.
   * The least supertype is the join (&#8744;) or supremum of both types in the
   * type lattice.<p>
   * Examples:
   * <ul>
   * <li>{@code number &#8744; *} = {@code *}</li>
   * <li>{@code number &#8744; Object} = {@code (number, Object)}</li>
   * <li>{@code Number &#8744; Object} = {@code Object}</li>
   * </ul>
   * @return {@code this &#8744; that}
   */
Method code:
771 |   public JSType getLeastSupertype(JSType that) {
772 |     if (that.isUnionType()) {
773 |       // Union types have their own implementation of getLeastSupertype.
774 |       return that.toMaybeUnionType().getLeastSupertype(this);
775 |     }
776 |     return getLeastSupertype(this, that);
777 |   }

===== M0561 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeValidator.expectNotNullOrUndefined(NodeTraversal,Node,JSType,String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeValidator.java
Original range: 254-280
Documentation:
/**
   * Expect the type to be anything but the null or void type. If the
   * expectation is not met, issue a warning at the provided node's
   * source code position. Note that a union type that includes the
   * void type and at least one other type meets the expectation.
   * @return Whether the expectation was met.
   */
Method code:
254 |   boolean expectNotNullOrUndefined(
255 |       NodeTraversal t, Node n, JSType type, String msg, JSType expectedType) {
256 |     if (!type.isNoType() && !type.isUnknownType() &&
257 |         type.isSubtype(nullOrUndefined) &&
258 |         !containsForwardDeclaredUnresolvedName(type)) {
259 | 
260 |       // There's one edge case right now that we don't handle well, and
261 |       // that we don't want to warn about.
262 |       // if (this.x == null) {
263 |       //   this.initializeX();
264 |       //   this.x.foo();
265 |       // }
266 |       // In this case, we incorrectly type x because of how we
267 |       // infer properties locally. See issue 109.
268 |       // http://code.google.com/p/closure-compiler/issues/detail?id=109
269 |       //
270 |       // We do not do this inference globally.
271 |       if (n.isGetProp() &&
272 |           !t.inGlobalScope() && type.isNullType()) {
273 |         return true;
274 |       }
275 | 
276 |       mismatch(t, n, msg, type, expectedType);
277 |       return false;
278 |     }
279 |     return true;
280 |   }

===== M0564 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeValidator.expectCanAssignToPropertyOf(NodeTraversal,Node,JSType,JSType,Node,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeValidator.java
Original range: 361-390
Documentation:
/**
   * Expect that the first type can be assigned to a symbol of the second
   * type.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal.
   * @param n The node to issue warnings on.
   * @param rightType The type on the RHS of the assign.
   * @param leftType The type of the symbol on the LHS of the assign.
   * @param owner The owner of the property being assigned to.
   * @param propName The name of the property being assigned to.
   * @return True if the types matched, false otherwise.
   */
Method code:
361 |   boolean expectCanAssignToPropertyOf(NodeTraversal t, Node n, JSType rightType,
362 |       JSType leftType, Node owner, String propName) {
363 |     // The NoType check is a hack to make typedefs work OK.
364 |     if (!leftType.isNoType() && !rightType.canAssignTo(leftType)) {
365 |       if (bothIntrinsics(rightType, leftType)) {
366 |         // We have a superior warning for this mistake, which gives you
367 |         // the line numbers of both types.
368 |         registerMismatch(rightType, leftType, null);
369 |       } else {
370 |         // Do not type-check interface methods, because we expect that
371 |         // they will have dummy implementations that do not match the type
372 |         // annotations.
373 |         JSType ownerType = getJSType(owner);
374 |         if (ownerType.isFunctionPrototypeType()) {
375 |           FunctionType ownerFn = ownerType.toObjectType().getOwnerFunction();
376 |           if (ownerFn.isInterface() &&
377 |               rightType.isFunctionType() && leftType.isFunctionType()) {
378 |             return true;
379 |           }
380 |         }
381 | 
382 |         mismatch(t, n,
383 |             "assignment to property " + propName + " of " +
384 |             getReadableJSTypeName(owner, true),
385 |             rightType, leftType);
386 |       }
387 |       return false;
388 |     }
389 |     return true;
390 |   }
