===== M0566 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.isEquivalentTo(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 885-909
Documentation:
/**
   * Two function types are equal if their signatures match. Since they don't
   * have signatures, two interfaces are equal if their names match.
   */
Method code:
885 |   @Override
886 |   public boolean isEquivalentTo(JSType otherType) {
887 |     FunctionType that =
888 |         JSType.toMaybeFunctionType(otherType);
889 |     if (that == null) {
890 |       return false;
891 |     }
892 |     if (this.isConstructor()) {
893 |       if (that.isConstructor()) {
894 |         return this == that;
895 |       }
896 |       return false;
897 |     }
898 |     if (this.isInterface()) {
899 |       if (that.isInterface()) {
900 |         return this.getReferenceName().equals(that.getReferenceName());
901 |       }
902 |       return false;
903 |     }
904 |     if (that.isInterface()) {
905 |       return false;
906 |     }
907 |     return this.typeOfThis.isEquivalentTo(that.typeOfThis) &&
908 |         this.call.isEquivalentTo(that.call);
909 |   }

83 |   private void parse(AbstractCompiler compiler) {
84 |     try {
85 |       logger_.fine("Parsing: " + sourceFile.getName());
86 |       root = ParserRunner.parse(sourceFile, sourceFile.getCode(),
87 |           compiler.getParserConfig(),
88 |           compiler.getDefaultErrorReporter(),
89 |           logger_);
90 |     } catch (IOException e) {
91 |       compiler.report(
92 |           JSError.make(AbstractCompiler.READ_ERROR, sourceFile.getName()));
93 |     }
94 | 
95 |     if (root == null || compiler.hasHaltingErrors()) {
96 |       // There was a parse error or IOException, so use a dummy block.
97 |       root = IR.script();
98 |     } else {
99 |       compiler.prepareAst(root);
100 |     }
101 | 
102 |     // Set the source name so that the compiler passes can track
103 |     // the source file and module.
104 |     root.setStaticSourceFile(sourceFile);
105 |   }

===== M0589 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.maybeDeclareQualifiedName(NodeTraversal,JSDocInfo,Node,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1468-1575
Documentation:
/**
     * Declare the symbol for a qualified name in the global scope.
     *
     * @param info The doc info for this property.
     * @param n A top-level GETPROP node (it should not be contained inside
     *     another GETPROP).
     * @param parent The parent of {@code n}.
     * @param rhsValue The node that {@code n} is being initialized to,
     *     or {@code null} if this is a stub declaration.
     */
Method code:
1468 |     void maybeDeclareQualifiedName(NodeTraversal t, JSDocInfo info,
1469 |         Node n, Node parent, Node rhsValue) {
1470 |       Node ownerNode = n.getFirstChild();
1471 |       String ownerName = ownerNode.getQualifiedName();
1472 |       String qName = n.getQualifiedName();
1473 |       String propName = n.getLastChild().getString();
1474 |       Preconditions.checkArgument(qName != null && ownerName != null);
1475 | 
1476 |       // Precedence of type information on GETPROPs:
1477 |       // 1) @type annotation / @enum annotation
1478 |       // 2) ASSIGN to FUNCTION literal
1479 |       // 3) @param/@return annotation (with no function literal)
1480 |       // 4) ASSIGN to something marked @const
1481 |       // 5) ASSIGN to anything else
1482 |       //
1483 |       // 1, 3, and 4 are declarations, 5 is inferred, and 2 is a declaration iff
1484 |       // the function has JsDoc or has not been declared before.
1485 |       //
1486 |       // FUNCTION literals are special because TypedScopeCreator is very smart
1487 |       // about getting as much type information as possible for them.
1488 | 
1489 |       // Determining type for #1 + #2 + #3 + #4
1490 |       JSType valueType = getDeclaredType(t.getSourceName(), info, n, rhsValue);
1491 |       if (valueType == null && rhsValue != null) {
1492 |         // Determining type for #5
1493 |         valueType = rhsValue.getJSType();
1494 |       }
1495 | 
1496 |       // Function prototypes are special.
1497 |       // It's a common JS idiom to do:
1498 |       // F.prototype = { ... };
1499 |       // So if F does not have an explicitly declared super type,
1500 |       // allow F.prototype to be redefined arbitrarily.
1501 |       if ("prototype".equals(propName)) {
1502 |         Var qVar = scope.getVar(qName);
1503 |         if (qVar != null) {
1504 |           // If the programmer has declared that F inherits from Super,
1505 |           // and they assign F.prototype to an object literal,
1506 |           // then they are responsible for making sure that the object literal's
1507 |           // implicit prototype is set up appropriately. We just obey
1508 |           // the @extends tag.
1509 |           ObjectType qVarType = ObjectType.cast(qVar.getType());
1510 |           if (qVarType != null &&
1511 |               rhsValue != null &&
1512 |               rhsValue.isObjectLit()) {
1513 |             typeRegistry.resetImplicitPrototype(
1514 |                 rhsValue.getJSType(), qVarType.getImplicitPrototype());
1515 |           } else if (!qVar.isTypeInferred()) {
1516 |             // If the programmer has declared that F inherits from Super,
1517 |             // and they assign F.prototype to some arbitrary expression,
1518 |             // there's not much we can do. We just ignore the expression,
1519 |             // and hope they've annotated their code in a way to tell us
1520 |             // what props are going to be on that prototype.
1521 |             return;
1522 |           }
1523 |           if (qVar.getScope() == scope) {
1524 |             scope.undeclare(qVar);
1525 |           }
1526 |         }
1527 |       }
1528 | 
1529 |       if (valueType == null) {
1530 |         if (parent.isExprResult()) {
1531 |           stubDeclarations.add(new StubDeclaration(
1532 |               n,
1533 |               t.getInput() != null && t.getInput().isExtern(),
1534 |               ownerName));
1535 |         }
1536 | 
1537 |         return;
1538 |       }
1539 | 
1540 |       boolean inferred = isQualifiedNameInferred(
1541 |           qName, n, info, rhsValue, valueType);
1542 |       if (!inferred) {
1543 |         ObjectType ownerType = getObjectSlot(ownerName);
1544 |         if (ownerType != null) {
1545 |           // Only declare this as an official property if it has not been
1546 |           // declared yet.
1547 |           boolean isExtern = t.getInput() != null && t.getInput().isExtern();
1548 |           if ((!ownerType.hasOwnProperty(propName) ||
1549 |                ownerType.isPropertyTypeInferred(propName)) &&
1550 |               ((isExtern && !ownerType.isNativeObjectType()) ||
1551 |                !ownerType.isInstanceType())) {
1552 |             // If the property is undeclared or inferred, declare it now.
1553 |             ownerType.defineDeclaredProperty(propName, valueType, n);
1554 |           }
1555 |         }
1556 | 
1557 |         // If the property is already declared, the error will be
1558 |         // caught when we try to declare it in the current scope.
1559 |         defineSlot(n, parent, valueType, inferred);
1560 |       } else if (rhsValue != null && rhsValue.isTrue()) {
1561 |         // We declare these for delegate proxy method properties.
1562 |         FunctionType ownerType =
1563 |             JSType.toMaybeFunctionType(getObjectSlot(ownerName));
1564 |         if (ownerType != null) {
1565 |           JSType ownerTypeOfThis = ownerType.getTypeOfThis();
1566 |           String delegateName = codingConvention.getDelegateSuperclassName();
1567 |           JSType delegateType = delegateName == null ?
1568 |               null : typeRegistry.getType(delegateName);
1569 |           if (delegateType != null &&
1570 |               ownerTypeOfThis.isSubtype(delegateType)) {
1571 |             defineSlot(n, parent, getNativeType(BOOLEAN_TYPE), true);
1572 |           }
1573 |         }
1574 |       }
1575 |     }

===== M0603 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.createFromTypeNodesInternal(Node,String,StaticScope<JSType>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 1491-1645
Documentation:
/** @see #createFromTypeNodes(Node, String, StaticScope) */
Method code:
1491 |   private JSType createFromTypeNodesInternal(Node n, String sourceName,
1492 |       StaticScope<JSType> scope) {
1493 |     switch (n.getType()) {
1494 |       case Token.LC: // Record type.
1495 |         return createRecordTypeFromNodes(
1496 |             n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope);
1497 | 
1498 |       case Token.BANG: // Not nullable
1499 |         return createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1500 |             n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope)
1501 |             .restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
1502 | 
1503 |       case Token.QMARK: // Nullable or unknown
1504 |         Node firstChild = n.getFirstChild();
1505 |         if (firstChild == null) {
1506 |           return getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1507 |         }
1508 |         return createDefaultObjectUnion(
1509 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1510 |                 firstChild, sourceName, scope));
1511 | 
1512 |       case Token.EQUALS: // Optional
1513 |         return createOptionalType(
1514 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1515 |                 n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope));
1516 | 
1517 |       case Token.ELLIPSIS: // Var args
1518 |         return createOptionalType(
1519 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1520 |                 n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope));
1521 | 
1522 |       case Token.STAR: // The AllType
1523 |         return getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
1524 | 
1525 |       case Token.LB: // Array type
1526 |         // TODO(nicksantos): Enforce membership restrictions on the Array.
1527 |         return getNativeType(ARRAY_TYPE);
1528 | 
1529 |       case Token.PIPE: // Union type
1530 |         UnionTypeBuilder builder = new UnionTypeBuilder(this);
1531 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null;
1532 |              child = child.getNext()) {
1533 |           builder.addAlternate(
1534 |               createFromTypeNodesInternal(child, sourceName, scope));
1535 |         }
1536 |         return builder.build();
1537 | 
1538 |       case Token.EMPTY: // When the return value of a function is not specified
1539 |         return getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1540 | 
1541 |       case Token.VOID: // Only allowed in the return value of a function.
1542 |         return getNativeType(VOID_TYPE);
1543 | 
1544 |       case Token.STRING:
1545 |         JSType namedType = getType(scope, n.getString(), sourceName,
1546 |             n.getLineno(), n.getCharno());
1547 |         if (resolveMode != ResolveMode.LAZY_NAMES) {
1548 |           namedType = namedType.resolveInternal(reporter, scope);
1549 |         }
1550 |         if ((namedType instanceof ObjectType) &&
1551 |             !(nonNullableTypeNames.contains(n.getString()))) {
1552 |           Node typeList = n.getFirstChild();
1553 |           if (typeList != null &&
1554 |               ("Array".equals(n.getString()) ||
1555 |                "Object".equals(n.getString()))) {
1556 |             JSType parameterType =
1557 |                 createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1558 |                     typeList.getLastChild(), sourceName, scope);
1559 |             namedType = new ParameterizedType(
1560 |                 this, (ObjectType) namedType, parameterType);
1561 |             if (typeList.hasMoreThanOneChild()) {
1562 |               JSType indexType =
1563 |                   createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1564 |                       typeList.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope);
1565 |               namedType = new IndexedType(
1566 |                   this, (ObjectType) namedType, indexType);
1567 |             }
1568 |           }
1569 |           return createDefaultObjectUnion(namedType);
1570 |         } else {
1571 |           return namedType;
1572 |         }
1573 | 
1574 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
1575 |         ObjectType thisType = null;
1576 |         boolean isConstructor = false;
1577 |         Node current = n.getFirstChild();
1578 |         if (current.getType() == Token.THIS ||
1579 |             current.getType() == Token.NEW) {
1580 |           Node contextNode = current.getFirstChild();
1581 |           thisType =
1582 |               ObjectType.cast(
1583 |                   createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1584 |                       contextNode, sourceName, scope)
1585 |                   .restrictByNotNullOrUndefined());
1586 |           if (thisType == null) {
1587 |             reporter.warning(
1588 |                 ScriptRuntime.getMessage0(
1589 |                     current.getType() == Token.THIS ?
1590 |                     "msg.jsdoc.function.thisnotobject" :
1591 |                     "msg.jsdoc.function.newnotobject"),
1592 |                 sourceName,
1593 |                 contextNode.getLineno(), contextNode.getCharno());
1594 |           }
1595 | 
1596 |           isConstructor = current.getType() == Token.NEW;
1597 |           current = current.getNext();
1598 |         }
1599 | 
1600 |         FunctionParamBuilder paramBuilder = new FunctionParamBuilder(this);
1601 | 
1602 |         if (current.getType() == Token.PARAM_LIST) {
1603 |           Node args = current.getFirstChild();
1604 |           for (Node arg = current.getFirstChild(); arg != null;
1605 |                arg = arg.getNext()) {
1606 |             if (arg.getType() == Token.ELLIPSIS) {
1607 |               if (arg.getChildCount() == 0) {
1608 |                 paramBuilder.addVarArgs(getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE));
1609 |               } else {
1610 |                 paramBuilder.addVarArgs(
1611 |                     createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1612 |                         arg.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope));
1613 |               }
1614 |             } else {
1615 |               JSType type = createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1616 |                   arg, sourceName, scope);
1617 |               if (arg.getType() == Token.EQUALS) {
1618 |                 boolean addSuccess = paramBuilder.addOptionalParams(type);
1619 |                 if (!addSuccess) {
1620 |                   reporter.warning(
1621 |                       ScriptRuntime.getMessage0("msg.jsdoc.function.varargs"),
1622 |                       sourceName, arg.getLineno(), arg.getCharno());
1623 |                 }
1624 |               } else {
1625 |                 paramBuilder.addRequiredParams(type);
1626 |               }
1627 |             }
1628 |           }
1629 |           current = current.getNext();
1630 |         }
1631 | 
1632 |         JSType returnType =
1633 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(current, sourceName, scope);
1634 | 
1635 |         return new FunctionBuilder(this)
1636 |             .withParams(paramBuilder)
1637 |             .withReturnType(returnType)
1638 |             .withTypeOfThis(thisType)
1639 |             .setIsConstructor(isConstructor)
1640 |             .build();
1641 |     }
1642 | 
1643 |     throw new IllegalStateException(
1644 |         "Unexpected node in type expression: " + n.toString());
1645 |   }

===== M0638 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getJsDocToken()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 58-194
Documentation:
/**
   * Tokenizes JSDoc comments.
   */
Method code:
58 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
59 |   final JsDocToken getJsDocToken() {
60 |     int c;
61 |     stringBufferTop = 0;
62 |     for (;;) {
63 |       // eat white spaces
64 |       for (;;) {
65 |         charno = -1;
66 |         c = getChar();
67 |         if (c == EOF_CHAR) {
68 |           return JsDocToken.EOF;
69 |         } else if (c == '\n') {
70 |           return JsDocToken.EOL;
71 |         } else if (!isJSSpace(c)) {
72 |           break;
73 |         }
74 |       }
75 | 
76 |       switch (c) {
77 |         // annotation, e.g. @type or @constructor
78 |         case '@':
79 |           do {
80 |             c = getChar();
81 |             if (isAlpha(c)) {
82 |               addToString(c);
83 |             } else {
84 |               ungetChar(c);
85 |               this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
86 |               stringBufferTop = 0;
87 |               return JsDocToken.ANNOTATION;
88 |             }
89 |           } while (true);
90 | 
91 |         case '*':
92 |           if (matchChar('/')) {
93 |             return JsDocToken.EOC;
94 |           } else {
95 |             return JsDocToken.STAR;
96 |           }
97 | 
98 |         case ',':
99 |           return JsDocToken.COMMA;
100 | 
101 |         case '>':
102 |           return JsDocToken.GT;
103 | 
104 |         case '(':
105 |           return JsDocToken.LP;
106 | 
107 |         case ')':
108 |           return JsDocToken.RP;
109 | 
110 |         case '{':
111 |           return JsDocToken.LC;
112 | 
113 |         case '}':
114 |           return JsDocToken.RC;
115 | 
116 |         case '[':
117 |           return JsDocToken.LB;
118 | 
119 |         case ']':
120 |           return JsDocToken.RB;
121 | 
122 |         case '?':
123 |           return JsDocToken.QMARK;
124 | 
125 |         case '!':
126 |           return JsDocToken.BANG;
127 | 
128 |         case ':':
129 |           return JsDocToken.COLON;
130 | 
131 |         case '=':
132 |           return JsDocToken.EQUALS;
133 | 
134 |         case '|':
135 |           matchChar('|');
136 |           return JsDocToken.PIPE;
137 | 
138 |         case '.':
139 |           c = getChar();
140 |           if (c == '<') {
141 |             return JsDocToken.LT;
142 |           } else {
143 |             if (c == '.') {
144 |               c = getChar();
145 |               if (c == '.') {
146 |                 return JsDocToken.ELLIPSIS;
147 |               } else {
148 |                 addToString('.');
149 |               }
150 |             }
151 |             // we may backtrack across line boundary
152 |             ungetBuffer[ungetCursor++] = c;
153 |             c = '.';
154 |           }
155 |           // fall through
156 | 
157 |         default: {
158 |           // recognize a JsDoc string but discard last . if it is followed by
159 |           // a non-JsDoc comment char, e.g. Array.<
160 |           int c1 = c;
161 |           addToString(c);
162 |           int c2 = getChar();
163 |           if (!isJSDocString(c2)) {
164 |             ungetChar(c2);
165 |             this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
166 |             stringBufferTop = 0;
167 |             return JsDocToken.STRING;
168 |           } else {
169 |             do {
170 |               c1 = c2;
171 |               c2 = getChar();
172 |               if (c1 == '.' && c2 == '<') {
173 |                 ungetChar(c2);
174 |                 ungetChar(c1);
175 |                 this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
176 |                 stringBufferTop = 0;
177 |                 return JsDocToken.STRING;
178 |               } else {
179 |                 if (isJSDocString(c2)) {
180 |                   addToString(c1);
181 |                 } else {
182 |                   ungetChar(c2);
183 |                   addToString(c1);
184 |                   this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
185 |                   stringBufferTop = 0;
186 |                   return JsDocToken.STRING;
187 |                 }
188 |               }
189 |             } while (true);
190 |           }
191 |         }
192 |       }
193 |     }
194 |   }

===== M0645 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1910-1912
Documentation:
/**
   * Is a FUNCTION node an function expression? An function expression is one
   * that has either no name or a name that is not added to the current scope.
   *
   * <p>Some examples of function expressions:
   * <pre>
   * (function () {})
   * (function f() {})()
   * [ function f() {} ]
   * var f = function f() {};
   * for (function f() {};;) {}
   * </pre>
   *
   * <p>Some examples of functions that are <em>not</em> expressions:
   * <pre>
   * function f() {}
   * if (x); else function f() {}
   * for (;;) { function f() {} }
   * </pre>
   *
   * @param n A node
   * @return Whether n is an function used within an expression.

===== M0659 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 443-490
Documentation:

Method code:
443 |   @Override
444 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
445 |     if (JSType.isSubtypeHelper(this, that)) {
446 |       return true;
447 |     }
448 | 
449 |     // Union types
450 |     if (that.isUnionType()) {
451 |       // The static {@code JSType.isSubtype} check already decomposed
452 |       // union types, so we don't need to check those again.
453 |       return false;
454 |     }
455 | 
456 |     // record types
457 |     if (that.isRecordType()) {
458 |       return RecordType.isSubtype(this, that.toMaybeRecordType());
459 |     }
460 | 
461 |     // Interfaces
462 |     // Find all the interfaces implemented by this class and compare each one
463 |     // to the interface instance.
464 |     ObjectType thatObj = that.toObjectType();
465 |     FunctionType thatCtor = thatObj == null ? null : thatObj.getConstructor();
466 | 
467 |     if (getConstructor() != null && getConstructor().isInterface()) {
468 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
469 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
470 |           return true;
471 |         }
472 |       }
473 |     } else if (thatCtor != null && thatCtor.isInterface()) {
474 |       Iterable<ObjectType> thisInterfaces = getCtorImplementedInterfaces();
475 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : thisInterfaces) {
476 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
477 |           return true;
478 |         }
479 |       }
480 |     }
481 | 
482 |     // other prototype based objects
483 |     if (isUnknownType() || implicitPrototypeChainIsUnknown()) {
484 |       // If unsure, say 'yes', to avoid spurious warnings.
485 |       // TODO(user): resolve the prototype chain completely in all cases,
486 |       // to avoid guessing.
487 |       return true;
488 |     }
489 |     return this.isImplicitPrototype(thatObj);
490 |   }

===== M0670 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.process(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 148-190
Documentation:

Method code:
148 |   @Override
149 |   public void process(Node externs, Node root) {
150 |     this.root = root;
151 |     astPositionCounter = 0;
152 |     astPosition = Maps.newHashMap();
153 |     nodePriorities = Maps.newHashMap();
154 |     cfg = new AstControlFlowGraph(computeFallThrough(root), nodePriorities,
155 |                                   edgeAnnotations);
156 |     NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, this);
157 |     astPosition.put(null, ++astPositionCounter); // the implicit return is last.
158 | 
159 |     // Now, generate the priority of nodes by doing a depth-first
160 |     // search on the CFG.
161 |     priorityCounter = 0;
162 |     DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> entry = cfg.getEntry();
163 |     prioritizeFromEntryNode(entry);
164 | 
165 |     if (shouldTraverseFunctions) {
166 |       // If we're traversing inner functions, we need to rank the
167 |       // priority of them too.
168 |       for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : cfg.getDirectedGraphNodes()) {
169 |         Node value = candidate.getValue();
170 |         if (value != null && value.isFunction()) {
171 |           Preconditions.checkState(
172 |               !nodePriorities.containsKey(candidate) || candidate == entry);
173 |           prioritizeFromEntryNode(candidate);
174 |         }
175 |       }
176 |     }
177 | 
178 |     // At this point, all reachable nodes have been given a priority, but
179 |     // unreachable nodes have not been given a priority. Put them last.
180 |     // Presumably, it doesn't really matter what priority they get, since
181 |     // this shouldn't happen in real code.
182 |     for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : cfg.getDirectedGraphNodes()) {
183 |       if (!nodePriorities.containsKey(candidate)) {
184 |         nodePriorities.put(candidate, ++priorityCounter);
185 |       }
186 |     }
187 | 
188 |     // Again, the implicit return node is always last.
189 |     nodePriorities.put(cfg.getImplicitReturn(), ++priorityCounter);
190 |   }

===== M0672 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.getQualifiedName()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1570-1585
Documentation:
/**
   * This function takes a set of GETPROP nodes and produces a string that is
   * each property separated by dots. If the node ultimately under the left
   * sub-tree is not a simple name, this is not a valid qualified name.
   *
   * @return a null if this is not a qualified name, or a dot-separated string
   *         of the name and properties.
   */
Method code:
1570 |   public String getQualifiedName() {
1571 |     if (type == Token.NAME) {
1572 |       String name = getString();
1573 |       return name.isEmpty() ? null : name;
1574 |     } else if (type == Token.GETPROP) {
1575 |       String left = getFirstChild().getQualifiedName();
1576 |       if (left == null) {
1577 |         return null;
1578 |       }
1579 |       return left + "." + getLastChild().getString();
1580 |     } else if (type == Token.THIS) {
1581 |       return "this";
1582 |     } else {
1583 |       return null;
1584 |     }
1585 |   }

===== M0674 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getChar()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 336-390
Documentation:

Method code:
336 |   protected int getChar() {
337 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
338 |       cursor++;
339 |       --ungetCursor;
340 |       if (charno == -1) {
341 |         charno = getOffset();
342 |       }
343 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
344 |     }
345 | 
346 |     for(;;) {
347 |       int c;
348 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
349 |         hitEOF = true;
350 |         if (charno == -1) {
351 |           charno = getOffset();
352 |         }
353 |         return EOF_CHAR;
354 |       }
355 |       cursor++;
356 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
357 | 
358 | 
359 |       if (lineEndChar >= 0) {
360 |         if (lineEndChar == '\r' && c == '\n') {
361 |           lineEndChar = '\n';
362 |           continue;
363 |         }
364 |         lineEndChar = -1;
365 |         lineStart = sourceCursor - 1;
366 |         lineno++;
367 |       }
368 | 
369 |       if (c <= 127) {
370 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
371 |           lineEndChar = c;
372 |           c = '\n';
373 |         }
374 |       } else {
375 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
376 |           continue;
377 |         }
378 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
379 |           lineEndChar = c;
380 |           c = '\n';
381 |         }
382 |       }
383 |

===== M0678 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.checkPropertyAccess(JSType,String,NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1330-1358
Documentation:
/**
   * Emit a warning if we can prove that a property cannot possibly be
   * defined on an object. Note the difference between JS and a strictly
   * statically typed language: we're checking if the property
   * *cannot be defined*, whereas a java compiler would check if the
   * property *can be undefined*.
   */
Method code:
1330 |   private void checkPropertyAccess(JSType childType, String propName,
1331 |       NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
1332 |     // If the property type is unknown, check the object type to see if it
1333 |     // can ever be defined. We explicitly exclude CHECKED_UNKNOWN (for
1334 |     // properties where we've checked that it exists, or for properties on
1335 |     // objects that aren't in this binary).
1336 |     JSType propType = getJSType(n);
1337 |     if (propType.isEquivalentTo(typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE))) {
1338 |       childType = childType.autobox();
1339 |       ObjectType objectType = ObjectType.cast(childType);
1340 |       if (objectType != null) {
1341 |         // We special-case object types so that checks on enums can be
1342 |         // much stricter, and so that we can use hasProperty (which is much
1343 |         // faster in most cases).
1344 |         if (!objectType.hasProperty(propName) ||
1345 |             objectType.isEquivalentTo(
1346 |                 typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE))) {
1347 |           if (objectType instanceof EnumType) {
1348 |             report(t, n, INEXISTENT_ENUM_ELEMENT, propName);
1349 |           } else {
1350 |             checkPropertyAccessHelper(objectType, propName, t, n);
1351 |           }
1352 |         }
1353 | 
1354 |       } else {
1355 |         checkPropertyAccessHelper(childType, propName, t, n);
1356 |       }
1357 |     }
1358 |   }

===== M0703 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseFunction(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 511-546
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a function.
   */
Method code:
511 |   private void traverseFunction(Node n, Node parent) {
512 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 3);
513 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.isFunction());
514 | 
515 |     final Node fnName = n.getFirstChild();
516 | 
517 |     boolean isFunctionExpression = (parent != null)
518 |         && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n);
519 | 
520 |     if (!isFunctionExpression) {
521 |       // Functions declarations are in the scope containing the declaration.
522 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
523 |     }
524 | 
525 |     curNode = n;
526 |     pushScope(n);
527 | 
528 |     if (isFunctionExpression) {
529 |       // Function expression names are only accessible within the function
530 |       // scope.
531 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
532 |     }
533 | 
534 |     final Node args = fnName.getNext();
535 |     final Node body = args.getNext();
536 | 
537 |     // Args
538 |     traverseBranch(args, n);
539 | 
540 |     // Body
541 |     Preconditions.checkState(body.getNext() == null &&
542 |             body.isBlock(), body);
543 |     traverseBranch(body, n);
544 | 
545 |     popScope();
546 |   }

Method code:
203 |   private boolean isConnectedInDirection(N n1, Predicate<E> edgeMatcher, N n2) {
204 |     // Verify the nodes.
205 |     DiGraphNode<N, E> dNode1 = getNodeOrFail(n1);
206 |     DiGraphNode<N, E> dNode2 = getNodeOrFail(n2);
207 |     for (DiGraphEdge<N, E> outEdge : dNode1.getOutEdges()) {
208 |       if (outEdge.getDestination() == dNode2 &&
209 |           edgeMatcher.apply(outEdge.getValue())) {
210 |         return true;
211 |       }
212 |     }
213 | 
214 |     return false;
215 |   }

===== M0730 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitFunction(NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1519-1593
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits a {@link Token#FUNCTION} node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   */
Method code:
1519 |   private void visitFunction(NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
1520 |     FunctionType functionType = JSType.toMaybeFunctionType(n.getJSType());
1521 |     String functionPrivateName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
1522 |     if (functionType.isConstructor()) {
1523 |       FunctionType baseConstructor = functionType.getSuperClassConstructor();
1524 |       if (baseConstructor != getNativeType(OBJECT_FUNCTION_TYPE) &&
1525 |           baseConstructor != null &&
1526 |           baseConstructor.isInterface() && functionType.isConstructor()) {
1527 |         compiler.report(
1528 |             t.makeError(n, CONFLICTING_EXTENDED_TYPE,
1529 |                         "constructor", functionPrivateName));
1530 |       } else {
1531 |         if (baseConstructor != getNativeType(OBJECT_FUNCTION_TYPE) &&
1532 |             baseConstructor != null) {
1533 |           if (functionType.makesStructs() && !baseConstructor.makesStructs()) {
1534 |             compiler.report(t.makeError(n, CONFLICTING_EXTENDED_TYPE,
1535 |                                         "struct", functionPrivateName));
1536 |           } else if (functionType.makesDicts() &&
1537 |                      !baseConstructor.makesDicts()) {
1538 |             compiler.report(t.makeError(n, CONFLICTING_EXTENDED_TYPE,
1539 |                                         "dict", functionPrivateName));
1540 |           }
1541 |         }
1542 |         // All interfaces are properly implemented by a class
1543 |         for (JSType baseInterface : functionType.getImplementedInterfaces()) {
1544 |           boolean badImplementedType = false;
1545 |           ObjectType baseInterfaceObj = ObjectType.cast(baseInterface);
1546 |           if (baseInterfaceObj != null) {
1547 |             FunctionType interfaceConstructor =
1548 |               baseInterfaceObj.getConstructor();
1549 |             if (interfaceConstructor != null &&
1550 |                 !interfaceConstructor.isInterface()) {
1551 |               badImplementedType = true;
1552 |             }
1553 |           } else {
1554 |             badImplementedType = true;
1555 |           }
1556 |           if (badImplementedType) {
1557 |             report(t, n, BAD_IMPLEMENTED_TYPE, functionPrivateName);
1558 |           }
1559 |         }
1560 |         // check properties
1561 |         validator.expectAllInterfaceProperties(t, n, functionType);
1562 |       }
1563 |     } else if (functionType.isInterface()) {
1564 |       // Interface must extend only interfaces
1565 |       for (ObjectType extInterface : functionType.getExtendedInterfaces()) {
1566 |         if (extInterface.getConstructor() != null
1567 |             && !extInterface.getConstructor().isInterface()) {
1568 |           compiler.report(
1569 |               t.makeError(n, CONFLICTING_EXTENDED_TYPE,
1570 |                           "interface", functionPrivateName));
1571 |         }
1572 |       }
1573 |       // Interface cannot implement any interfaces
1574 |       if (functionType.hasImplementedInterfaces()) {
1575 |         compiler.report(t.makeError(n,
1576 |             CONFLICTING_IMPLEMENTED_TYPE, functionPrivateName));
1577 |       }
1578 |       // Check whether the extended interfaces have any conflicts
1579 |       if (functionType.getExtendedInterfacesCount() > 1) {
1580 |         // Only check when extending more than one interfaces
1581 |         HashMap<String, ObjectType> properties
1582 |             = new HashMap<String, ObjectType>();
1583 |         HashMap<String, ObjectType> currentProperties
1584 |             = new HashMap<String, ObjectType>();
1585 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : functionType.getExtendedInterfaces()) {
1586 |           currentProperties.clear();
1587 |           checkInterfaceConflictProperties(t, n, functionPrivateName,
1588 |               properties, currentProperties, interfaceType);
1589 |           properties.putAll(currentProperties);
1590 |         }
1591 |       }
1592 |     }
1593 |   }

===== M0739 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 1081-1083
Documentation:
/**
   * Checks whether {@code this} is a subtype of {@code that}.<p>
   *
   * Subtyping rules:
   * <ul>
   * <li>(unknown) &mdash; every type is a subtype of the Unknown type.</li>
   * <li>(no) &mdash; the No type is a subtype of every type.</li>
   * <li>(no-object) &mdash; the NoObject type is a subtype of every object
   * type (i.e. subtypes of the Object type).</li>
   * <li>(ref) &mdash; a type is a subtype of itself.</li>
   * <li>(union-l) &mdash; A union type is a subtype of a type U if all the
   * union type's constituents are a subtype of U. Formally<br>
   * {@code (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>) &lt;: U} if and only
   * {@code T<sub>k</sub> &lt;: U} for all {@code k &isin; 1..n}.</li>
   * <li>(union-r) &mdash; A type U is a subtype of a union type if it is a
   * subtype of one of the union type's constituents. Formally<br>
   * {@code U &lt;: (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>)} if and only
   * if {@code U &lt;: T<sub>k</sub>} for some index {@code k}.</li>
   * <li>(objects) &mdash; an Object {@code O<sub>1</sub>} is a subtype
   * of an object {@code O<sub>2</sub>} if it has more properties
   * than {@code O<sub>2</sub>} and all common properties are
   * pairwise subtypes.</li>
   * </ul>
   *
   * @return {@code this &lt;: that}
   */
Method code:
1081 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
1082 |     return isSubtypeHelper(this, that);
1083 |   }

===== M0747 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.filterNoResolvedType(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 866-892
Documentation:
/**
   * When computing infima, we may get a situation like
   * inf(Type1, Type2)
   * where both types are unresolved, so they're technically
   * subtypes of one another.
   *
   * If this happens, filter them down to NoResolvedType.
   */
Method code:
866 |   static JSType filterNoResolvedType(JSType type) {
867 |     if (type.isNoResolvedType()) {
868 |       // inf(UnresolvedType1, UnresolvedType2) needs to resolve
869 |       // to the base unresolved type, so that the relation is symmetric.
870 |       return type.getNativeType(JSTypeNative.NO_RESOLVED_TYPE);
871 |     } else if (type.isUnionType()) {
872 |       UnionType unionType = type.toMaybeUnionType();
873 |       boolean needsFiltering = false;
874 |       for (JSType alt : unionType.getAlternates()) {
875 |         if (alt.isNoResolvedType()) {
876 |           needsFiltering = true;
877 |           break;
878 |         }
879 |       }
880 | 
881 |       if (needsFiltering) {
882 |         UnionTypeBuilder builder = new UnionTypeBuilder(type.registry);
883 |         for (JSType alt : unionType.getAlternates()) {
884 |           if (!alt.isNoResolvedType()) {
885 |             builder.addAlternate(alt);
886 |           }
887 |         }
888 |         return builder.build();
889 |       }
890 |     }
891 |     return type;
892 |   }

215 |   @Override
216 |   public int hashCode() {
217 |     int hashCode = 0;

/** Handle bleeding functions and function parameters. */
Method code:
1905 |     private void handleFunctionInputs(Node fnNode) {
1906 |       // Handle bleeding functions.
1907 |       Node fnNameNode = fnNode.getFirstChild();
1908 |       String fnName = fnNameNode.getString();
1909 |       if (!fnName.isEmpty()) {
1910 |         Scope.Var fnVar = scope.getVar(fnName);
1911 |         if (fnVar == null ||
1912 |             // Make sure we're not touching a native function. Native
1913 |             // functions aren't bleeding, but may not have a declaration
1914 |             // node.
1915 |             (fnVar.getNameNode() != null &&
1916 |                 // Make sure that the function is actually bleeding by checking
1917 |                 // if has already been declared.
1918 |                 fnVar.getInitialValue() != fnNode)) {
1919 |           defineSlot(fnNameNode, fnNode, fnNode.getJSType(), false);
1920 |         }
1921 |       }
1922 | 
1923 |       declareArguments(fnNode);

/**
   * Builds a single list of all module inputs. Verifies that it contains no
   * duplicates.
   */
Method code:
488 |   private static List<CompilerInput> getAllInputsFromModules(
489 |       List<JSModule> modules) {
490 |     List<CompilerInput> inputs = Lists.newArrayList();
491 |     Map<String, JSModule> inputMap = Maps.newHashMap();
492 |     for (JSModule module : modules) {
493 |       for (CompilerInput input : module.getInputs()) {

498 |         // will get an error down the line.
499 |         inputs.add(input);
500 |         inputMap.put(inputName, module);
501 |       }
502 |     }
503 |     return inputs;
504 |   }

     * Resolve any stub declarations to unknown types if we could not
     * find types for them during traversal.

Method code:
1668 |     void resolveStubDeclarations() {
1669 |       for (StubDeclaration stub : stubDeclarations) {
1670 |         Node n = stub.node;
1671 |         Node parent = n.getParent();
1672 |         String qName = n.getQualifiedName();
1673 |         String propName = n.getLastChild().getString();
1674 |         String ownerName = stub.ownerName;
1675 |         boolean isExtern = stub.isExtern;
1676 |

1678 |           continue;
1679 |         }
1680 |

1682 |         // in the type registry.

1684 |         ObjectType unknownType = typeRegistry.getNativeObjectType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1685 |         defineSlot(n, parent, unknownType, true);

1690 |           // as an unknown type. These are seen often in externs.

1692 |               propName, unknownType, n);
1693 |         } else {

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.getScope()

Documentation:
/** Gets the current scope. */
Method code:
596 |   public Scope getScope() {
597 |     Scope scope = scopes.isEmpty() ? null : scopes.peek();
598 |     if (scopeRoots.isEmpty()) {
599 |       return scope;
600 |     }
601 | 
602 |     Iterator<Node> it = scopeRoots.descendingIterator();
603 |     while (it.hasNext()) {
604 |       scope = scopeCreator.createScope(it.next(), scope);
605 |       scopes.push(scope);
606 |     }
607 |     scopeRoots.clear();
608 | 
609 |     return scope;
610 |   }

     * Find the function that's being overridden on this type, if any.

Method code:
955 |     private FunctionType findOverriddenFunction(
956 |         ObjectType ownerType, String propName) {
957 |       // First, check to see if the property is implemented
958 |       // on a superclass.
959 |       JSType propType = ownerType.getPropertyType(propName);
960 |       if (propType != null && propType.isFunctionType()) {
961 |         return propType.toMaybeFunctionType();
962 |       } else {
963 |         // If it's not, then check to see if it's implemented
964 |         // on an implemented interface.

967 |           propType = iface.getPropertyType(propName);
968 |           if (propType != null && propType.isFunctionType()) {
969 |             return propType.toMaybeFunctionType();
970 |           }
971 |         }
972 |       }
973 |

975 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.defineSlot(Node,Node,String,JSType,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1095-1234

/**
     * Defines a symbol in the current scope.
     *
     * @param n the defining NAME or GETPROP or object literal key node.
     * @param parent the {@code n}'s parent.
     * @param variableName The name that this should be known by.
     * @param type the variable's type. It may be {@code null} if
     *     {@code inferred} is {@code true}.
     * @param inferred Whether the type is inferred or declared.
     */
Method code:
1095 |     void defineSlot(Node n, Node parent, String variableName,
1096 |         JSType type, boolean inferred) {
1097 |       Preconditions.checkArgument(!variableName.isEmpty());
1098 | 
1099 |       boolean isGlobalVar = n.isName() && scope.isGlobal();
1100 |       boolean shouldDeclareOnGlobalThis =
1101 |           isGlobalVar &&
1102 |           (parent.isVar() ||
1103 |            parent.isFunction());
1104 | 
1105 |       // If n is a property, then we should really declare it in the
1106 |       // scope where the root object appears. This helps out people
1107 |       // who declare "global" names in an anonymous namespace.
1108 |       Scope scopeToDeclareIn = scope;
1109 |       if (n.isGetProp() && !scope.isGlobal() &&
1110 |           isQnameRootedInGlobalScope(n)) {
1111 |         Scope globalScope = scope.getGlobalScope();
1112 | 
1113 |         // don't try to declare in the global scope if there's
1114 |         // already a symbol there with this name.
1115 |         if (!globalScope.isDeclared(variableName, false)) {
1116 |           scopeToDeclareIn = scope.getGlobalScope();
1117 |         }
1118 |       }
1119 | 
1120 |       // The input may be null if we are working with a AST snippet. So read
1121 |       // the extern info from the node.
1122 |       boolean isExtern = n.isFromExterns();
1123 |       Var newVar = null;
1124 | 
1125 |       // declared in closest scope?
1126 |       CompilerInput input = compiler.getInput(inputId);
1127 |       if (scopeToDeclareIn.isDeclared(variableName, false)) {
1128 |         Var oldVar = scopeToDeclareIn.getVar(variableName);
1129 |         newVar = validator.expectUndeclaredVariable(
1130 |             sourceName, input, n, parent, oldVar, variableName, type);
1131 |       } else {
1132 |         if (type != null) {
1133 |           setDeferredType(n, type);
1134 |         }
1135 | 
1136 |         newVar =
1137 |           scopeToDeclareIn.declare(variableName, n, type, input, inferred);
1138 | 
1139 |         if (type instanceof EnumType) {
1140 |           Node initialValue = newVar.getInitialValue();
1141 |           boolean isValidValue = initialValue != null &&
1142 |               (initialValue.isObjectLit() ||
1143 |                initialValue.isQualifiedName());
1144 |           if (!isValidValue) {
1145 |             compiler.report(JSError.make(sourceName, n, ENUM_INITIALIZER));
1146 |           }
1147 |         }
1148 |       }
1149 | 
1150 |       // We need to do some additional work for constructors and interfaces.
1151 |       FunctionType fnType = JSType.toMaybeFunctionType(type);
1152 |       if (fnType != null &&
1153 |           // We don't want to look at empty function types.
1154 |           !type.isEmptyType()) {
1155 |         if ((fnType.isConstructor() || fnType.isInterface()) &&
1156 |             !fnType.isEquivalentTo(getNativeType(U2U_CONSTRUCTOR_TYPE))) {
1157 |           // Declare var.prototype in the scope chain.
1158 |           FunctionType superClassCtor = fnType.getSuperClassConstructor();
1159 |           ObjectType.Property prototypeSlot = fnType.getSlot("prototype");
1160 | 
1161 |           // When we declare the function prototype implicitly, we
1162 |           // want to make sure that the function and its prototype
1163 |           // are declared at the same node. We also want to make sure
1164 |           // that the if a symbol has both a Var and a JSType, they have
1165 |           // the same node.
1166 |           //
1167 |           // This consistency is helpful to users of SymbolTable,
1168 |           // because everything gets declared at the same place.
1169 |           prototypeSlot.setNode(n);
1170 | 
1171 |           String prototypeName = variableName + ".prototype";
1172 | 
1173 |           // There are some rare cases where the prototype will already
1174 |           // be declared. See TypedScopeCreatorTest#testBogusPrototypeInit.
1175 |           // Fortunately, other warnings will complain if this happens.
1176 |           Var prototypeVar = scopeToDeclareIn.getVar(prototypeName);
1177 |           if (prototypeVar != null && prototypeVar.scope == scopeToDeclareIn) {
1178 |             scopeToDeclareIn.undeclare(prototypeVar);
1179 |           }
1180 | 
1181 |           scopeToDeclareIn.declare(prototypeName,
1182 |               n, prototypeSlot.getType(), input,
1183 |               /* declared iff there's an explicit supertype */
1184 |               superClassCtor == null ||
1185 |               superClassCtor.getInstanceType().isEquivalentTo(
1186 |                   getNativeType(OBJECT_TYPE)));
1187 | 
1188 |           // Make sure the variable is initialized to something if
1189 |           // it constructs itself.
1190 |           if (newVar.getInitialValue() == null &&
1191 |               !isExtern &&
1192 |               // We want to make sure that when we declare a new instance
1193 |               // type (with @constructor) that there's actually a ctor for it.
1194 |               // This doesn't apply to structural constructors
1195 |               // (like function(new:Array). Checking the constructed
1196 |               // type against the variable name is a sufficient check for
1197 |               // this.
1198 |               variableName.equals(
1199 |                   fnType.getInstanceType().getReferenceName())) {
1200 |             compiler.report(
1201 |                 JSError.make(sourceName, n,
1202 |                     fnType.isConstructor() ?
1203 |                     CTOR_INITIALIZER : IFACE_INITIALIZER,
1204 |                     variableName));
1205 |           }
1206 |         }
1207 |       }
1208 | 
1209 |       if (shouldDeclareOnGlobalThis) {
1210 |         ObjectType globalThis =
1211 |             typeRegistry.getNativeObjectType(GLOBAL_THIS);
1212 |         if (inferred) {
1213 |           globalThis.defineInferredProperty(variableName,
1214 |               type == null ?
1215 |               getNativeType(JSTypeNative.NO_TYPE) :
1216 |               type,
1217 |               n);
1218 |         } else {
1219 |           globalThis.defineDeclaredProperty(variableName, type, n);
1220 |         }
1221 |       }
1222 | 
1223 |       if (isGlobalVar && "Window".equals(variableName)
1224 |           && type != null
1225 |           && type.isFunctionType()
1226 |           && type.isConstructor()) {
1227 |         FunctionType globalThisCtor =
1228 |             typeRegistry.getNativeObjectType(GLOBAL_THIS).getConstructor();
1229 |         globalThisCtor.getInstanceType().clearCachedValues();
1230 |         globalThisCtor.getPrototype().clearCachedValues();
1231 |         globalThisCtor
1232 |             .setPrototypeBasedOn((type.toMaybeFunctionType()).getInstanceType());
1233 |       }
1234 |     }

===== M0792 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.buildAndRegister()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 593-655
Documentation:
/**
   * Builds the function type, and puts it in the registry.
   */
Method code:
593 |   FunctionType buildAndRegister() {
594 |     if (returnType == null) {
595 |       // Infer return types.
596 |       // We need to be extremely conservative about this, because of two
597 |       // competing needs.
598 |       // 1) If we infer the return type of f too widely, then we won't be able
599 |       //    to assign f to other functions.
600 |       // 2) If we infer the return type of f too narrowly, then we won't be
601 |       //    able to override f in subclasses.
602 |       // So we only infer in cases where the user doesn't expect to write
603 |       // @return annotations--when it's very obvious that the function returns
604 |       // nothing.
605 |       if (!contents.mayHaveNonEmptyReturns() &&
606 |           !contents.mayHaveSingleThrow() &&
607 |           !contents.mayBeFromExterns()) {
608 |         returnType = typeRegistry.getNativeType(VOID_TYPE);
609 |         returnTypeInferred = true;
610 |       }
611 |     }
612 | 
613 |     if (returnType == null) {
614 |       returnType = typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
615 |     }
616 | 
617 |     if (parametersNode == null) {
618 |       throw new IllegalStateException(
619 |           "All Function types must have params and a return type");
620 |     }
621 | 
622 |     FunctionType fnType;
623 |     if (isConstructor) {
624 |       fnType = getOrCreateConstructor();
625 |     } else if (isInterface) {
626 |       fnType = typeRegistry.createInterfaceType(
627 |           fnName, contents.getSourceNode());
628 |       if (getScopeDeclaredIn().isGlobal() && !fnName.isEmpty()) {
629 |         typeRegistry.declareType(fnName, fnType.getInstanceType());
630 |       }
631 |       maybeSetBaseType(fnType);
632 |     } else {
633 |       fnType = new FunctionBuilder(typeRegistry)
634 |           .withName(fnName)
635 |           .withSourceNode(contents.getSourceNode())
636 |           .withParamsNode(parametersNode)
637 |           .withReturnType(returnType, returnTypeInferred)
638 |           .withTypeOfThis(thisType)
639 |           .withTemplateNames(templateTypeNames)
640 |           .build();
641 |       maybeSetBaseType(fnType);
642 |     }
643 | 
644 |     if (implementedInterfaces != null) {
645 |       fnType.setImplementedInterfaces(implementedInterfaces);
646 |     }
647 | 
648 |     if (extendedInterfaces != null) {
649 |       fnType.setExtendedInterfaces(extendedInterfaces);
650 |     }
651 | 
652 |     typeRegistry.clearTemplateTypeNames();
653 | 
654 |     return fnType;
655 |   }

499 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {

523 |           if (firstChild.isGetProp() &&
524 |               firstChild.isQualifiedName()) {
525 |             maybeDeclareQualifiedName(t, n.getJSDocInfo(),
526 |                 firstChild, n, firstChild.getNext());
527 |           }
528 |           break;
529 | 
530 |         case Token.CATCH:
531 |           defineCatch(n, parent);
532 |           break;
533 | 
534 |         case Token.VAR:
535 |           defineVar(n, parent);
536 |           break;
537 | 
538 |         case Token.GETPROP:
539 |           // Handle stubbed properties.
540 |           if (parent.isExprResult() &&
541 |               n.isQualifiedName()) {
542 |             maybeDeclareQualifiedName(t, n.getJSDocInfo(), n, parent, null);
543 |           }
544 |           break;
545 |       }
546 | 
547 |       // Analyze any @lends object literals in this statement.
548 |       if (n.getParent() != null && NodeUtil.isStatement(n) &&
549 |           lentObjectLiterals != null) {
550 |         for (Node objLit : lentObjectLiterals) {
551 |           defineObjectLiteral(objLit);
552 |         }
553 |         lentObjectLiterals.clear();
554 |       }
555 |     }

135 |       case Token.LSH:

137 |       case Token.MOD:
138 |       case Token.MUL:
139 |       case Token.NE:
140 |       case Token.OR:
141 |       case Token.RSH:
142 |       case Token.SHEQ:
143 |       case Token.SHNE:
144 |       case Token.SUB:
145 |       case Token.URSH:
146 |         return processInfixExpression((InfixExpression) node);
147 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:

178 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
179 |                                           node.getClass() +

181 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
182 |         }

185 |       case Token.CALL:

192 |       case Token.COLON:

194 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
195 |         return processContinueStatement((ContinueStatement) node);
196 |       case Token.DO:
197 |         return processDoLoop((DoLoop) node);
198 |       case Token.EMPTY:
199 |         return (node instanceof EmptyExpression) ?
200 |             processEmptyExpression((EmptyExpression) node) :
201 |             processEmptyStatement((EmptyStatement) node);
202 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:

257 |       case Token.SCRIPT:

259 |       case Token.STRING:
260 |         return processStringLiteral((StringLiteral) node);
261 |       case Token.SWITCH:
262 |         return processSwitchStatement((SwitchStatement) node);
263 |       case Token.THROW:

265 |       case Token.TRY:

273 |         } else {

275 |                                           node.getClass() +
276 |                                           " type: " +
277 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
278 |         }
279 |       case Token.WHILE:
280 |         return processWhileLoop((WhileLoop) node);
281 |       case Token.WITH:
282 |         return processWithStatement((WithStatement) node);
283 |     }

===== M0820 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.hasUnknownOrEmptySupertype(FunctionType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1194-1216
Documentation:
/**
   * Given a constructor or an interface type, find out whether the unknown
   * type is a supertype of the current type.
   */
Method code:
1194 |   private static boolean hasUnknownOrEmptySupertype(FunctionType ctor) {
1195 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(ctor.isConstructor() || ctor.isInterface());
1196 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(!ctor.isUnknownType());
1197 | 
1198 |     // The type system should notice inheritance cycles on its own
1199 |     // and break the cycle.
1200 |     while (true) {
1201 |       ObjectType maybeSuperInstanceType =
1202 |           ctor.getPrototype().getImplicitPrototype();
1203 |       if (maybeSuperInstanceType == null) {
1204 |         return false;
1205 |       }
1206 |       if (maybeSuperInstanceType.isUnknownType() ||
1207 |           maybeSuperInstanceType.isEmptyType()) {
1208 |         return true;
1209 |       }
1210 |       ctor = maybeSuperInstanceType.getConstructor();
1211 |       if (ctor == null) {
1212 |         return false;
1213 |       }
1214 |       Preconditions.checkState(ctor.isConstructor() || ctor.isInterface());
1215 |     }
1216 |   }

===== M0825 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.LinkedFlowScope.equals(Object)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/LinkedFlowScope.java
Original range: 258-308
Documentation:

Method code:
258 |   @Override
259 |   public boolean equals(Object other) {
260 |     if (other instanceof LinkedFlowScope) {
261 |       LinkedFlowScope that = (LinkedFlowScope) other;
262 |       if (this.optimize() == that.optimize()) {
263 |         return true;
264 |       }
265 | 
266 |       // If two flow scopes are in the same function, then they could have
267 |       // two possible function scopes: the real one and the BOTTOM scope.
268 |       // If they have different function scopes, we *should* iterate through all
269 |       // the variables in each scope and compare. However, 99.9% of the time,
270 |       // they're not equal. And the other .1% of the time, we can pretend
271 |       // they're equal--this just means that data flow analysis will have
272 |       // to propagate the entry lattice a little bit further than it
273 |       // really needs to. Everything will still come out ok.
274 |       if (this.getFunctionScope() != that.getFunctionScope()) {
275 |         return false;
276 |       }
277 | 
278 |       if (cache == that.cache) {
279 |         // If the two flow scopes have the same cache, then we can check
280 |         // equality a lot faster: by just looking at the "dirty" elements
281 |         // in the cache, and comparing them in both scopes.
282 |         for (String name : cache.dirtySymbols) {
283 |           if (diffSlots(getSlot(name), that.getSlot(name))) {
284 |             return false;
285 |           }
286 |         }
287 | 
288 |         return true;
289 |       }
290 | 
291 |       Map<String, StaticSlot<JSType>> myFlowSlots = allFlowSlots();
292 |       Map<String, StaticSlot<JSType>> otherFlowSlots = that.allFlowSlots();
293 | 
294 |       for (StaticSlot<JSType> slot : myFlowSlots.values()) {
295 |         if (diffSlots(slot, otherFlowSlots.get(slot.getName()))) {
296 |           return false;
297 |         }
298 |         otherFlowSlots.remove(slot.getName());
299 |       }
300 |       for (StaticSlot<JSType> slot : otherFlowSlots.values()) {
301 |         if (diffSlots(slot, myFlowSlots.get(slot.getName()))) {
302 |           return false;
303 |         }
304 |       }
305 |       return true;
306 |     }
307 |     return false;
308 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 619-631
Documentation:
/**
   * Tells the type system that {@code owner} may have a property named
   * {@code propertyName}. This allows the registry to keep track of what
   * types a property is defined upon.
   *
   * This is NOT the same as saying that {@code owner} must have a property
   * named type. ObjectType#hasProperty attempts to minimize false positives
   * ("if we're not sure, then don't type check this property"). The type
   * registry, on the other hand, should attempt to minimize false negatives
   * ("if this property is assigned anywhere in the program, it must
   * show up in the type registry").
   */
Method code:
619 |   public void registerPropertyOnType(String propertyName, JSType type) {
620 |     UnionTypeBuilder typeSet = typesIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
621 |     if (typeSet == null) {
622 |       typeSet = new UnionTypeBuilder(this, PROPERTY_CHECKING_UNION_SIZE);
623 |       typesIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet);
624 |     }
625 | 
626 |     typeSet.addAlternate(type);
627 |     addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(propertyName, type);
628 | 
629 |     // Clear cached values that depend on typesIndexedByProperty.
630 |     greatestSubtypeByProperty.remove(propertyName);
631 |   }

   * @param item The item to inspect

Method code:
797 |   private PropListItem removeProp(PropListItem item, int propType) {
798 |     if (item == null) {
799 |       return null;

808 |       }

936 |   public void resolveTypesInScope(StaticScope<JSType> scope) {
937 |     for (NamedType type : unresolvedNamedTypes.get(scope)) {

940 |

944 |       // By default, the global "this" type is just an anonymous object.

===== M0946 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.isQualifiedNameInferred(String,Node,JSDocInfo,Node,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1604-1647
Documentation:
/**
     * Determines whether a qualified name is inferred.
     * NOTE(nicksantos): Determining whether a property is declared or not
     * is really really obnoxious.
     *
     * The problem is that there are two (equally valid) coding styles:
     *
     * (function() {
     *   /* The authoritative definition of goog.bar. /
     *   goog.bar = function() {};
     * })();
     *
     * function f() {
     *   goog.bar();
     *   /* Reset goog.bar to a no-op. /
     *   goog.bar = function() {};
     * }
     *
     * In a dynamic language with first-class functions, it's very difficult
     * to know which one the user intended without looking at lots of
     * contextual information (the second example demonstrates a small case
     * of this, but there are some really pathological cases as well).
     *
     * The current algorithm checks if either the declaration has
     * JsDoc type information, or @const with a known type,
     * or a function literal with a name we haven't seen before.
     */
Method code:
1604 |     private boolean isQualifiedNameInferred(
1605 |         String qName, Node n, JSDocInfo info,
1606 |         Node rhsValue, JSType valueType) {
1607 |       if (valueType == null) {
1608 |         return true;
1609 |       }
1610 | 
1611 |       boolean inferred = true;
1612 |       if (info != null) {
1613 |         inferred = !(info.hasType()
1614 |             || info.hasEnumParameterType()
1615 |             || (info.isConstant() && valueType != null
1616 |                 && !valueType.isUnknownType())
1617 |             || FunctionTypeBuilder.isFunctionTypeDeclaration(info));
1618 |       }
1619 | 
1620 |       if (inferred && rhsValue != null && rhsValue.isFunction()) {
1621 |         if (info != null) {
1622 |           return false;
1623 |         } else if (!scope.isDeclared(qName, false) &&
1624 |             n.isUnscopedQualifiedName()) {
1625 | 
1626 |           // Check if this is in a conditional block.
1627 |           // Functions assigned in conditional blocks are inferred.
1628 |           for (Node current = n.getParent();
1629 |                !(current.isScript() || current.isFunction());
1630 |                current = current.getParent()) {
1631 |             if (NodeUtil.isControlStructure(current)) {
1632 |               return true;
1633 |             }
1634 |           }
1635 | 
1636 |           // Check if this is assigned in an inner scope.
1637 |           // Functions assigned in inner scopes are inferred.
1638 |           AstFunctionContents contents =
1639 |               getFunctionAnalysisResults(scope.getRootNode());
1640 |           if (contents == null ||
1641 |               !contents.getEscapedQualifiedNames().contains(qName)) {
1642 |             return false;
1643 |           }
1644 |         }
1645 |       }
1646 |       return inferred;
1647 |     }

===== M0954 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 403-429
Documentation:

Method code:
403 |   void setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType baseType, Node propertyNode) {
404 |     // This is a bit weird. We need to successfully handle these
405 |     // two cases:
406 |     // Foo.prototype = new Bar();
407 |     // and
408 |     // Foo.prototype = {baz: 3};
409 |     // In the first case, we do not want new properties to get
410 |     // added to Bar. In the second case, we do want new properties
411 |     // to get added to the type of the anonymous object.
412 |     //
413 |     // We handle this by breaking it into two cases:
414 |     //
415 |     // In the first case, we create a new PrototypeObjectType and set
416 |     // its implicit prototype to the type being assigned. This ensures
417 |     // that Bar will not get any properties of Foo.prototype, but properties
418 |     // later assigned to Bar will get inherited properly.
419 |     //
420 |     // In the second case, we just use the anonymous object as the prototype.
421 |     if (baseType.hasReferenceName() ||

/**
     * Look for a type declaration on a property assignment
     * (in an ASSIGN or an object literal key).
     *
     * @param info The doc info for this property.
     * @param lValue The l-value node.
     * @param rValue The node that {@code n} is being initialized to,
     *     or {@code null} if this is a stub declaration.
     */
Method code:
1267 |     private JSType getDeclaredType(String sourceName, JSDocInfo info,
1268 |         Node lValue, @Nullable Node rValue) {
1269 |       if (info != null && info.hasType()) {
1270 |         return getDeclaredTypeInAnnotation(sourceName, lValue, info);
1271 |       } else if (rValue != null && rValue.isFunction() &&
1272 |           shouldUseFunctionLiteralType(
1273 |               JSType.toMaybeFunctionType(rValue.getJSType()), info, lValue)) {
1274 |         return rValue.getJSType();
1275 |       } else if (info != null) {
1276 |         if (info.hasEnumParameterType()) {
1277 |           if (rValue != null && rValue.isObjectLit()) {
1278 |             return rValue.getJSType();

1280 |             return createEnumTypeFromNodes(
1281 |                 rValue, lValue.getQualifiedName(), info, lValue);
1282 |           }
1283 |         } else if (info.isConstructor() || info.isInterface()) {
1284 |           return createFunctionTypeFromNodes(
1285 |               rValue, lValue.getQualifiedName(), info, lValue);
1286 |         } else {
1287 |           // Check if this is constant, and if it has a known type.
1288 |           if (info.isConstant()) {
1289 |             JSType knownType = null;
1290 |             if (rValue != null) {
1291 |               JSDocInfo rValueInfo = rValue.getJSDocInfo();
1292 |               if (rValueInfo != null && rValueInfo.hasType()) {
1293 |                 // If rValue has a type-cast, we use the type in the type-cast.
1294 |                 return rValueInfo.getType().evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
1295 |               } else if (rValue.getJSType() != null
1296 |                   && !rValue.getJSType().isUnknownType()) {
1297 |                 // If rValue's type was already computed during scope creation,
1298 |                 // then we can safely use that.
1299 |                 return rValue.getJSType();
1300 |               } else if (rValue.isOr()) {
1301 |                 // Check for a very specific JS idiom:
1302 |                 // var x = x || TYPE;
1303 |                 // This is used by Closure's base namespace for esoteric
1304 |                 // reasons.
1305 |                 Node firstClause = rValue.getFirstChild();
1306 |                 Node secondClause = firstClause.getNext();
1307 |                 boolean namesMatch = firstClause.isName()
1308 |                     && lValue.isName()
1309 |                     && firstClause.getString().equals(lValue.getString());
1310 |                 if (namesMatch && secondClause.getJSType() != null
1311 |                     && !secondClause.getJSType().isUnknownType()) {
1312 |                   return secondClause.getJSType();
1313 |                 }
1314 |               }
1315 |             }
1316 |           }
1317 |         }
1318 |       }

1321 |     }

392 |   private int getCharIgnoreLineEnd() {
393 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
394 |       cursor++;
395 |       --ungetCursor;
396 |       if (charno == -1) {
397 |         charno = getOffset();
398 |       }

400 |     }
401 | 
402 |     for(;;) {
403 |       int c;
404 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
405 |         hitEOF = true;
406 |         if (charno == -1) {
407 |           charno = getOffset();
408 |         }
409 |         return EOF_CHAR;

412 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);

419 |         }

427 |         }
428 |       }

431 |         charno = getOffset();
432 |       }
433 |

   * Creates a scope with all types declared. Declares newly discovered types
   * and type properties in the type registry.

Method code:
192 |   @Override
193 |   public Scope createScope(Node root, Scope parent) {
194 |     // Constructing the global scope is very different than constructing
195 |     // inner scopes, because only global scopes can contain named classes that
196 |     // show up in the type registry.
197 |     Scope newScope = null;
198 |     AbstractScopeBuilder scopeBuilder = null;
199 |     if (parent == null) {
200 |       // Run a first-order analysis over the syntax tree.
201 |       (new FirstOrderFunctionAnalyzer(compiler, functionAnalysisResults))
202 |           .process(root.getFirstChild(), root.getLastChild());
203 | 
204 |       // Find all the classes in the global scope.
205 |       newScope = createInitialScope(root);
206 | 
207 |       GlobalScopeBuilder globalScopeBuilder = new GlobalScopeBuilder(newScope);
208 |       scopeBuilder = globalScopeBuilder;
209 |       NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, scopeBuilder);
210 |     } else {
211 |       newScope = new Scope(parent, root);
212 |       LocalScopeBuilder localScopeBuilder = new LocalScopeBuilder(newScope);
213 |       scopeBuilder = localScopeBuilder;
214 |       localScopeBuilder.build();
215 |     }
216 | 
217 |     scopeBuilder.resolveStubDeclarations();
218 |     scopeBuilder.resolveTypes();
219 | 
220 |     // Gather the properties in each function that we found in the
221 |     // global scope, if that function has a @this type that we can
222 |     // build properties on.
223 |     for (Node functionNode : scopeBuilder.nonExternFunctions) {
224 |       JSType type = functionNode.getJSType();
225 |       if (type != null && type.isFunctionType()) {
226 |         FunctionType fnType = type.toMaybeFunctionType();
227 |         ObjectType fnThisType = fnType.getTypeOfThis();
228 |         if (!fnThisType.isUnknownType()) {
229 |           NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, functionNode.getLastChild(),
230 |               scopeBuilder.new CollectProperties(fnThisType));
231 |         }
232 |       }
233 |     }
234 | 
235 |     if (parent == null) {
236 |       codingConvention.defineDelegateProxyPrototypeProperties(
237 |           typeRegistry, newScope, delegateProxyPrototypes,
238 |           delegateCallingConventions);
239 |     }
240 |     return newScope;
241 |   }

===== M0974 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ArrowType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ArrowType.java
Original range: 79-171
Documentation:

Method code:
79 |   @Override
80 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType other) {
81 |     if (!(other instanceof ArrowType)) {
82 |       return false;
83 |     }
84 | 
85 |     ArrowType that = (ArrowType) other;
86 | 
87 |     // This is described in Draft 2 of the ES4 spec,
88 |     // Section 3.4.7: Subtyping Function Types.
89 | 
90 |     // this.returnType <: that.returnType (covariant)
91 |     if (!this.returnType.isSubtype(that.returnType)) {
92 |       return false;
93 |     }
94 | 
95 |     // that.paramType[i] <: this.paramType[i] (contravariant)
96 |     //
97 |     // If this.paramType[i] is required,
98 |     // then that.paramType[i] is required.
99 |     //
100 |     // In theory, the "required-ness" should work in the other direction as
101 |     // well. In other words, if we have
102 |     //
103 |     // function f(number, number) {}
104 |     // function g(number) {}
105 |     //
106 |     // Then f *should* not be a subtype of g, and g *should* not be
107 |     // a subtype of f. But in practice, we do not implement it this way.
108 |     // We want to support the use case where you can pass g where f is
109 |     // expected, and pretend that g ignores the second argument.
110 |     // That way, you can have a single "no-op" function, and you don't have
111 |     // to create a new no-op function for every possible type signature.
112 |     //
113 |     // So, in this case, g < f, but f !< g
114 |     Node thisParam = parameters.getFirstChild();
115 |     Node thatParam = that.parameters.getFirstChild();
116 |     while (thisParam != null && thatParam != null) {
117 |       JSType thisParamType = thisParam.getJSType();
118 |       JSType thatParamType = thatParam.getJSType();
119 |       if (thisParamType != null) {
120 |         if (thatParamType == null ||
121 |             !thatParamType.isSubtype(thisParamType)) {
122 |           return false;
123 |         }
124 |       }
125 | 
126 |       boolean thisIsVarArgs = thisParam.isVarArgs();
127 |       boolean thatIsVarArgs = thatParam.isVarArgs();
128 |       boolean thisIsOptional = thisIsVarArgs || thisParam.isOptionalArg();
129 |       boolean thatIsOptional = thatIsVarArgs || thatParam.isOptionalArg();
130 | 
131 |       // "that" can't be a supertype, because it's missing a required argument.
132 |       if (!thisIsOptional && thatIsOptional) {
133 |         // NOTE(nicksantos): In our type system, we use {function(...?)} and
134 |         // {function(...NoType)} to to indicate that arity should not be
135 |         // checked. Strictly speaking, this is not a correct formulation,
136 |         // because now a sub-function can required arguments that are var_args
137 |         // in the super-function. So we special-case this.
138 |         boolean isTopFunction =
139 |             thatIsVarArgs &&
140 |             (thatParamType == null ||
141 |              thatParamType.isUnknownType() ||
142 |              thatParamType.isNoType());
143 |         if (!isTopFunction) {
144 |           return false;
145 |         }
146 |       }
147 | 
148 |       // don't advance if we have variable arguments
149 |       if (!thisIsVarArgs) {
150 |         thisParam = thisParam.getNext();
151 |       }
152 |       if (!thatIsVarArgs) {
153 |         thatParam = thatParam.getNext();
154 |       }
155 | 
156 |       // both var_args indicates the end
157 |       if (thisIsVarArgs && thatIsVarArgs) {
158 |         thisParam = null;
159 |         thatParam = null;
160 |       }
161 |     }
162 | 
163 |     // "that" can't be a supertype, because it's missing a required argument.
164 |     if (thisParam != null
165 |         && !thisParam.isOptionalArg() && !thisParam.isVarArgs()
166 |         && thatParam == null) {
167 |       return false;
168 |     }
169 | 
170 |     return true;
171 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.inferParameterTypes(Node,JSDocInfo)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 438-506
Documentation:
/**
   * Infer the parameter types from the list of argument names and
   * the doc info.
   */
Method code:
438 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferParameterTypes(@Nullable Node argsParent,
439 |       @Nullable JSDocInfo info) {
440 |     if (argsParent == null) {
441 |       if (info == null) {
442 |         return this;
443 |       } else {
444 |         return inferParameterTypes(info);
445 |       }
446 |     }
447 | 
448 |     // arguments
449 |     Node oldParameterType = null;
450 |     if (parametersNode != null) {
451 |       oldParameterType = parametersNode.getFirstChild();
452 |     }
453 | 
454 |     FunctionParamBuilder builder = new FunctionParamBuilder(typeRegistry);
455 |     boolean warnedAboutArgList = false;
456 |     Set<String> allJsDocParams = (info == null) ?
457 |         Sets.<String>newHashSet() :
458 |         Sets.newHashSet(info.getParameterNames());
459 |     boolean foundTemplateType = false;
460 |     boolean isVarArgs = false;
461 |     for (Node arg : argsParent.children()) {
462 |       String argumentName = arg.getString();
463 |       allJsDocParams.remove(argumentName);
464 | 
465 |       // type from JSDocInfo
466 |       JSType parameterType = null;
467 |       boolean isOptionalParam = isOptionalParameter(arg, info);
468 |       isVarArgs = isVarArgsParameter(arg, info);
469 | 
470 |       if (info != null && info.hasParameterType(argumentName)) {
471 |         parameterType =
472 |             info.getParameterType(argumentName).evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
473 |       } else if (oldParameterType != null &&
474 |           oldParameterType.getJSType() != null) {
475 |         parameterType = oldParameterType.getJSType();
476 |         isOptionalParam = oldParameterType.isOptionalArg();
477 |         isVarArgs = oldParameterType.isVarArgs();
478 |       } else {
479 |         parameterType = typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
480 |       }
481 | 
482 |       warnedAboutArgList |= addParameter(
483 |           builder, parameterType, warnedAboutArgList,
484 |           isOptionalParam,
485 |           isVarArgs);
486 | 
487 |       if (oldParameterType != null) {
488 |         oldParameterType = oldParameterType.getNext();
489 |       }
490 |     }
491 | 
492 |     // Copy over any old parameters that aren't in the param list.
493 |     if (!isVarArgs) {
494 |       while (oldParameterType != null && !isVarArgs) {
495 |         builder.newParameterFromNode(oldParameterType);
496 |         oldParameterType = oldParameterType.getNext();
497 |       }
498 |     }
499 | 
500 |     for (String inexistentName : allJsDocParams) {
501 |       reportWarning(INEXISTANT_PARAM, inexistentName, fnName);
502 |     }
503 | 
504 |     parametersNode = builder.build();
505 |     return this;
506 |   }

===== M0998 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.ensureTyped(NodeTraversal,Node,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1885-1909
Documentation:
/**
   * Enforces type casts, and ensures the node is typed.
   *
   * A cast in the way that we use it in JSDoc annotations never
   * alters the generated code and therefore never can induce any runtime
   * operation. What this means is that a 'cast' is really just a compile
   * time constraint on the underlying value. In the future, we may add
   * support for run-time casts for compiled tests.
   *
   * To ensure some shred of sanity, we enforce the notion that the
   * type you are casting to may only meaningfully be a narrower type
   * than the underlying declared type. We also invalidate optimizations
   * on bad type casts.
   *
   * @param t The traversal object needed to report errors.
   * @param n The node getting a type assigned to it.
   * @param type The type to be assigned.
   */
Method code:
1885 |   private void ensureTyped(NodeTraversal t, Node n, JSType type) {
1886 |     // Make sure FUNCTION nodes always get function type.
1887 |     Preconditions.checkState(!n.isFunction() ||
1888 |             type.isFunctionType() ||
1889 |             type.isUnknownType());
1890 |     JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1891 |     if (info != null) {
1892 |       if (info.hasType()) {
1893 |         JSType infoType = info.getType().evaluate(t.getScope(), typeRegistry);
1894 |         validator.expectCanCast(t, n, infoType, type);
1895 |         type = infoType;
1896 |       }
1897 | 
1898 |       if (info.isImplicitCast() && !inExterns) {
1899 |         String propName = n.isGetProp() ?
1900 |             n.getLastChild().getString() : "(missing)";
1901 |         compiler.report(
1902 |             t.makeError(n, ILLEGAL_IMPLICIT_CAST, propName));
1903 |       }
1904 |     }
1905 | 
1906 |     if (n.getJSType() == null) {
1907 |       n.setJSType(type);
1908 |     }
1909 |   }
